Motivation: 现有 Boltzmann 生成器局限于正则系综（固定粒子数），无法直接处理粒子数可变的开放系统；而巨正则系综在吸附、相变、流体等应用中至关重要，因此需要一种能对巨正则分布进行生成建模、并支持无偏修正与密度估计的框架。

Method: 提出巨正则生成器（GCG）的两种设计：1）在可变规模生成模型上以化学势为条件，联合采样粒子数与构型；2）将巨正则分布分解为粒子数分布与相应正则 Boltzmann 密度，正则部分可复用任意现有 Boltzmann 生成器，并将化学势线性依赖解析编码，得到可处理的似然以支持自归一化重要性采样（SNIS）与巨正则蒙特卡洛修正。

Result: 在 Lennard-Jones 流体和沸石中甲烷吸附两个体系上，GCG 能准确重现巨正则可观测量，展示了对不同化学势的泛化能力，并可通过 SNIS 和巨正则蒙特卡洛进行修正。

Conclusion: GCG 成功将生成式建模扩展到巨正则系综，兼具灵活性与可处理似然，为开放体系的高效采样与自由能/巨势相关计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce Grand Canonical Generators (GCG), a generative framework that extends Boltzmann generators to the grand canonical ensemble. We present two designs. The first conditions a variable-size generative model on the chemical potential, sampling particle number and configuration jointly. The second factorizes the grand canonical distribution into a particle-number distribution and the corresponding canonical Boltzmann density. This factorized formulation can use any existing Boltzmann generator for the canonical component, encodes the known linear chemical-potential dependence analytically, and yields a tractable likelihood that supports self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS). Empirically, GCG accurately reproduces grand canonical observables on a Lennard--Jones fluid and methane adsorption in a zeolite, demonstrating generalization across chemical potentials and correction via SNIS and grand canonical Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [167] [MOVE: Multimodal Open-world Verification and Expansion for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00268)
*Zekai Chen,Jiayang Xing,Xun Wu,Miao Zhang,Xunkai Li,Kairui Yang,Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOVE 是一个面向多模态图学习的开放世界类验证与扩展框架，通过联合视觉、文本与图上下文识别未知节点，并用多模态大模型生成候选类，仅在多模态证据一致性支持下才选择性扩展类空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图学习面临部署后新类涌现的挑战：现有方法虽能检测未知节点并借助 LLM 生成候选类描述，但缺乏判断——现有类是否确实不足以覆盖这些节点，以及生成的候选类是否可靠到足以扩展类空间。

Method: 提出 MOVE 框架：1) 联合视觉 token、文本属性与图上下文识别无法归入已有类的节点；2) 利用多模态 LLM 生成候选类；3) 仅在候选类获得多模态证据一致性支持且不引入冗余类别时，才选择性扩展类空间。

Result: 实验表明，MOVE 在未知识别、开放域标注和下游图学习任务上取得了平均 11.87% 的提升。

Conclusion: 实证研究揭示了三个挑战（未知节点识别需要超越单一模态的多模态信息、LLM 生成的类描述未必完整刻画多模态类特征、直接添加候选类会引入冗余类别），MOVE 通过联合多模态识别与选择性验证扩展有效解决了这些问题。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning faces a fundamental challenge: new classes may emerge after deployment, while models are trained with a fixed label space. Existing approaches typically detect unknown nodes and use LLMs to generate candidate class descriptions, but they do not determine whether existing classes are insufficient to cover these nodes or whether a generated class is reliable enough to expand the class space. Our empirical study reveals three challenges: multimodal information beyond individual modalities is required for unknown-node identification, LLM-generated class descriptions may not fully capture multimodal class characteristics, and directly adding candidate classes can introduce redundant categories. Based on these observations, we propose MOVE, a multimodal open-world class verification and expansion framework. MOVE identifies nodes that cannot be assigned to existing classes by jointly considering visual tokens, textual attributes, and graph context, leverages a multimodal LLM to generate candidate classes, and selectively expands the class space only when candidates are consistently supported by multimodal evidence without introducing unnecessary categories. Experiments demonstrate that MOVE achieves an average improvement of 11.87\% across unknown recognition, open-domain annotation, and downstream graph learning tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Coupling Perception and Reasoning in Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00277)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Hailin Zhang,Xunkai Li,Yu Liu,Kairui Yang,Muyan Huang,Xuaner Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCORE 提出一种联邦多模态图基础模型适配框架，通过共享低维隐状态同时表示编码器与GNN的更新，在共享状态空间中进行联邦演化，从而保持感知与推理适配的兼容性，显著缩小编码器-GNN配对差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦GNN适配方法多只更新图侧模块而冻结多模态编码器，仅改变信息传播方式、固定信息提取内容。作者通过实证发现编码器与GNN适配并非相互独立：编码器适配受图关系影响，跨客户端模块交换揭示出二者更新存在显著的配对敏感性。因此需要一种能将感知（编码器）与推理（GNN）联合适配并保持兼容的联邦框架。

Method: 提出 FedCORE 框架，将编码器与GNN的更新通过共享的低维隐状态（core）统一表示，从多模态信号与结构信号中联合优化该核心，并直接在共享状态空间中进行联邦演化，从而保持感知与推理适配之间的兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明，FedCORE 将编码器-GNN配对差距从30.6降至5.9，相较于独立联合适配实现了80.7%的下降。

Conclusion: 通过在共享低维状态空间中联合演化编码器与GNN更新，FedCORE 有效解决了联邦多模态图基础模型适配中感知与推理模块的配对不兼容问题，显著缩小配对差距，验证了共享核心表示对联合适配的重要性。

Abstract: Federated multimodal graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to adapt pretrained multimodal models to decentralized graph data, where each client owns a private multimodal graph and cannot share raw information. These models typically combine a multimodal Encoder that extracts semantic evidence from heterogeneous modalities and a graph neural network (GNN) that performs relational reasoning over graph structures. However, existing federated GFM adaptation methods mainly update graph-side modules while keeping the multimodal Encoder frozen, limiting adaptation to \emph{how information is propagated} while fixing \emph{what information is extracted}. Through empirical studies, we reveal that Encoder and GNN adaptations are not independent: Encoder adaptation is affected by graph relations, while cross-client module swapping reveals substantial pairing sensitivity between separately parameterized Encoder and GNN updates. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{FedCORE}, a federated adaptation framework that represents Encoder and GNN updates through a shared low-dimensional latent state. FedCORE jointly optimizes this core from multimodal and structural signals and performs federated evolution directly in the shared state space, preserving compatibility between perception and reasoning adaptations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedCORE reduces the Encoder--GNN pairing gap from $30.6$ to $5.9$, corresponding to an $80.7\%$ reduction over independent joint adaptation.

</details>


### [169] [Stable and Counterfactually Robust Physical World Models from Imposed Structure and Learned Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00280)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨世界模型需要硬编码多少通用物理结构，才能同时保证能量耗散兼容性、对物理参数干预的正确响应、长达百倍训练时长的稳定性及抗扰动能力，并在三类物理系统上验证了结构化模型显著优于无结构的等容量模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型从轨迹数据中学习系统演化，但其模仿的系统遵循的物理定律既未被完整提供，也无法被可靠遵守。模型可能凭空产生能量、在长程推演中漂移或发散，并在面对改变的物理定律查询时错误地沿用训练时观测到的定律。因此需要研究：为保证四类关键性质（第二定律兼容的耗散、对物理参数干预的正确响应、达到训练时长一百倍的稳定性、抗扰动鲁棒性）同时成立，多少通用物理结构必须硬编码进模型，多少系统特定的物理可从数据中学习。

Method: 施加的通用结构包括：动力学由学习到的能量经一个固定可逆算子取梯度生成；能量被限制在约束（confining）类中；一个单向端口只能耗散能量而不能注入能量；驱动通道已知；被干预的参数通过一个可分离映射进入系统。模型学习能量泛函、本构关系、耗散率及各耦合项。在电磁腔、粒子网格（PIC）和浅水流体三个系统上验证，使用约九千参数的模型。

Result: 约九千参数的模型以单位斜率恢复本构函数；用单一组权重将守恒世界与耗散世界区分开四个数量级；能将符号、幅值、速率和重力的变化迁移到未见过的取值，而同等容量但无此结构的模型表现仅为随机水平或更差。非线性本构关系被恢复且曲率得到保留，对留出干预的预测比收敛的线性模型好 2–17 倍。

Conclusion: 只需硬编码少量通用物理结构（基于能量梯度的可逆算子、约束能量类、单向耗散端口、已知驱动通道及可分离介入映射），模型即可从数据中学习系统特定物理，并在耗散兼容性、干预响应、长程稳定性和鲁棒性上同时达成目标，显著优于无结构的等容量模型。

Abstract: A world model learns to forecast how a physical system evolves from recorded trajectories, yet the systems it imitates obey physical laws that are neither fully supplied nor reliably respected. The model may create energy, drift or diverge over long rollouts, and answer a changed law query using the law observed during training. We ask how much general physical structure must be hard coded into a world model, and how much system-specific physics can then be learned from data, for four properties to hold simultaneously: second law compatible dissipation, correct responses to interventions on physical parameters, stability out to one hundred times the training horizon, and robustness to disturbances. The imposed structure is general: dynamics are generated from the gradient of a learned energy through a fixed reversible operator, the energy is restricted to a confining class, a one way port can remove energy but never inject it, the drive channel is known, and the intervened parameter enters through a separable map. The model learns the energy functional, constitutive relations, dissipation rate, and couplings. Across an electromagnetic cavity, a particle in cell grid, and a shallow-water fluid, models with roughly nine thousand parameters recover constitutive functions with unit slope, separate conserving from dissipating worlds by four orders of magnitude using a single set of weights, and transfer changes in sign, magnitude, rate, and gravity to unseen values, where equal-capacity models without the same structure perform at chance or worse. A nonlinear constitutive law is recovered with its curvature preserved and predicts a held-out intervention $2$-$17\times$ better than a converged linear model.

</details>


### [170] [Signal-Noise Factorization Isolates Nuisance Variation into Removable Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00751)
*Sakin Kirti,Joel Zylberberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出强化信号-噪声分解（SNF）与信号-信号分解（SSF）的正则化器，发现SNF正则化能提升CIFAR-100及含医学图像腐蚀的BloodMNIST上的性能，而SSF正则化无益；SNF通过将噪声隔离到独立子空间，使腐蚀方向可被显式估计并投影消除，带来显著准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期理论工作指出表征几何的两大基本性质——信号-噪声分解（SNF，分离信号与噪声）与信号-信号分解（SSF，分离任务相关与任务无关信号）——塑造深度网络的推理能力。作者希望将这两项理论性质转化为可实际强化的正则化器，探究它们如何影响表征几何与模型性能，并为生物医学等含分布外图像失真的应用提供改进手段。

Method: 作者构建了分别强化SNF与SSF性质的正则化器，在CIFAR-100分类任务上与L2正则化的基线网络进行对比；进而以五级严重程度经MedMNIST-C腐蚀处理的BloodMNIST数据集评估其在生物医学场景下的效果；最后分析不同正则化强度下网络的干扰子空间，验证SNF正则化是否将噪声表征在独立于类别信号的不同子空间，并在留出数据上估计主要腐蚀方向、从表征中投影剔除，以检验对准确率的提升。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上，强化SNF的正则化改善了模型性能，而强化SSF未带来改善；在经MedMNIST-C五级腐蚀的BloodMNIST上，SNF正则化带来更大的性能增益。分析显示，SNF正则化模型将噪声表征在与类别相关信号分离的独立子空间中；利用这种显式几何，可在留出数据上估计占主导的腐蚀方向并将其投影出表征，从而获得显著的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 强制执行信号-噪声分解的正则化器能在推断时包含分布外图像失真的计算机视觉任务中带来实质性提升。该工作也揭示了塑造表征对性能的影响机制：将干扰变量与类别变量隔离，比保持类别变量自身的分解化表征更为重要。

Abstract: Recent theoretical work identified fundamental properties of representation geometry that shape inference ability of deep neural networks. These include signal-noise factorization (SNF), the ability to segregate signal from noise, and signal-signal factorization (SSF), the ability to segregate task-specific and task-irrelevant signals. Here, we built regularizers that reinforce these two properties during training. We compared networks trained with these regularizers to $L_2$-regularized baseline networks on the CIFAR-100 classification task to understand how our regularizers shape representation geometry and impact performance on a well-known computer vision baseline. Enhancing SNF via regularization improved model performance but enhancing SSF did not. Motivated by biomedical applications, we investigated how our regularizers affected performance on the BloodMNIST dataset treated with MedMNIST-C corruptions at five severity levels, and found even larger performance gains using the SNF regularizer. To understand the mechanism by which SNF-regularization produces improved performance, we analyzed the nuisance subspaces across regularization regimes, finding that the SNF-regularized models represent noise in distinct subspaces, separate from class-relevant signal. Because this geometry is explicit, the dominant corruption-induced directions can be estimated on held-out data and projected out of the representations. This manipulation led to a substantial gain in accuracy. These results show that regularizers that enforce signal-noise factorization can produce substantial improvements on computer vision tasks that contain out-of-distribution image distortions at inference time. They also highlight how shaping representations affects model performance: isolating nuisance variables from categorical ones is more important than maintaining factorized representations of categorical variables.

</details>


### [171] [Scalable Multi-Task Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00758)
*Allen Tran,Jia Wan,Nathan Kallus,Aurélien Bibaut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务逆强化学习方法，在同一环境下利用低秩结构融合多个具有不同奖励的智能体的数据，从而放宽覆盖假设、实现对新环境下多任务的可扩展评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆强化学习通过学习可迁移奖励，支持在修改后的环境下对智能体进行反事实评估。但目标环境会改变智能体的状态占用分布，因此对状态覆盖提出了严格要求。作者希望放松这种覆盖要求，并提升多个任务在新环境下评估的计算可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种多任务逆强化学习方法，在同一环境下汇集多个具有不同奖励的智能体的数据，并假设奖励矩阵满足低秩结构。各任务无需访问所有状态（只要其他任务覆盖即可），且计算量随秩而非任务数增长。

Result: 提供了奖励恢复和在新环境下策略学习的有限样本保证。实验表明，该方法对有限覆盖具有鲁棒性，能在各任务支持集内外恢复奖励，以低于基线的遗憾迁移到目标环境，且随任务数增加，其相对逐任务方法的计算优势不断扩大。

Conclusion: 通过低秩多任务共享，方法有效缓解了逆强化学习中的覆盖约束，实现了对新环境下多任务的可扩展、可证明且鲁棒的反事实评估。

Abstract: By learning transferable rewards, inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) enables counterfactual evaluation of agents under modified environments. Such transfer places strict requirements on coverage since target environments affect agents' state occupancy. We propose a multi-task IRL method that pools data across multiple agents with different rewards in the same environment under a low-rank assumption. In addition to alleviating coverage requirements, so each task need not visit every state as long as others do, the method offers scalable evaluation of multiple tasks under new environments as computationally intensive planning scales with rank rather than the number of tasks. We provide finite sample guarantees on reward recovery and on policy learning in new environments. Experiments show our method is robust to limited coverage, recovers rewards on and off of each task's support, transfers to target environments at lower regret than baselines, with its computational advantage over per-task methods widening as tasks grow.

</details>


### [172] [Bellman-Certified Rounding for Sparse Policy Deployment in MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00325)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限MDP中将连续随机策略舍入为稀疏二元策略时的回报保真问题，提出了基于贝尔曼求解的可复用包络与秩二有理表示下的加权曲率积分方法，显著提升了舍入前的认证覆盖率和舍入后的界-损失比精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续策略优化会在许多状态上产生微小更新，但实际部署往往只允许少数完整的状态级改变；策略相关的状态访问分布使各行编辑相互耦合，且长时域使全局曲率界过于保守，因此需要更紧的舍入保证。

Method: 通过 $2d+2$ 次贝尔曼求解推导出可复用的包络，为舍入前提供统一保证与候选专属保证；进一步利用每次交换的秩二有理表示，沿实际轨迹进行加权曲率积分，得到局部更紧的界。

Result: 证明了当预算增长时线性维度依赖不可避免，且精确全局曲率阈值判定是困难的；候选专属界将结构化套件上的舍入前认证覆盖率从 48.2% 提升到 74.1%；在 $\gamma=0.95$ 时，局部积分将耦合实例上的界-损失比中位数从 402.3 降至 2.08。

Conclusion: 本文为有限MDP的策略稀疏化提供了理论保证，说明候选专属包络与局部曲率积分能大幅提升舍入认证的覆盖率和紧致性，同时揭示了维度依赖与曲率阈值计算的内在难度。

Abstract: Continuous policy optimization may spread an update across many states, even when deployment permits only a few complete state-level changes. We study how much discounted return can be retained when continuous row mixtures are rounded to sparse binary policies in finite MDPs. Policy-dependent visitation couples the row edits, while long horizons make global curvature bounds conservative. From $2d+2$ Bellman solves, we derive reusable envelopes that support uniform and candidate-specific guarantees before rounding. A rank-two rational representation of each exchange further permits weighted curvature integration along the realized trajectory. We prove that linear dimension dependence is unavoidable when the budget scales, and that exact global curvature thresholding is hard. Candidate-specific bounds raise pre-rounding certification coverage from $48.2\%$ to $74.1\%$ on the structured suite. At $γ=0.95$, local integration lowers the median bound-to-loss ratio from $402.3$ to $2.08$ on coupled instances.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond Diagonal State Space Models: Exact Non-Abelian Group Tracking, Solvability Barriers, and Geometric Physical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00329)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非交换状态空间模型（NC-SSM）及其 SO(3)/SO(N) 变体，将状态转移从可交换的对角/仿射群提升到李群 SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)，并通过欧拉-罗德里格斯映射与凯莱变换保证严格等距，从而显著提升在非可解群（如 A_5、S_5）及长程关联记忆任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性状态空间模型（Mamba、S4D、LRU）受限于转移矩阵可交换性（A_t A_t'=A_t' A_t）及可解仿射变换群（Aff_D，导出长度≤2），导致堆叠对角网络在非可解单群（如 A_5）上因模拟非阿贝尔交换子需要指数级电路深度而出现严重优化退化。作者旨在突破这一代数结构限制。

Method: 提出三类模型：NC-SSM、实正交对应体 SO(3)-SSM 及任意维 Cayley-SSM，将状态转移提升到紧李群 SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)。关键组件包括：闭合形式的欧拉-罗德里格斯映射与有理凯莱变换实现精确保范等距（||U_t||=1）；纯霍普夫纤维化 Bloch 投影读出（S^3/{±1}≅S^2≅SO(3)）消除符号歧义；真四元数并行前缀扫描（T=2048 时 9.06x 加速）；以及恒等门控李群 SSM 消除稀疏句法相位漂移。

Result: 在 14 个实验场景中：(1) NC-SSM 在 S_3、D_4、Q_8 及单群 A_5 上实现 100% 跟踪，而 3 层深对角基线坍缩至 6.60%（p=8.81e-4）；(2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM 打破 Klein 1884 年在对称群 S_5 上的上限（50.92% vs 对角 5.25%，p=0.0015，相对 SO(3) 方差降低 7.8 倍）；(3) SO(3)-SSM 在 300 步内保持黎曼流形（漂移<3.12e-6，优势>580,000 倍），航位推算误差 0.04 度并支持主动切向去噪；(4) NC-SSM 在 Dyck-2 上达 74.36%，深度 AST 作用域跟踪 30.26%（p=0.0081）；(5) 消融证实严格等距是无损长程关联记忆的数学必要条件。

Conclusion: 严格等距（保范）是非阿贝尔状态转移实现无损长程关联记忆的数学必要条件；将状态转移提升到紧李群（SU(2)/SO(3)/SO(N)）能从根本上突破传统对角 SSM 的可解性限制，并在群跟踪、黎曼流形保持与句法长程任务上取得显著且统计可靠的优势。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, S4D, and LRU, are bounded by transition matrix commutativity (A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t) and solvable affine transformation groups (Aff_D of derived length <= 2). Consequently, stacked multi-layer diagonal networks face severe optimization degradation on non-solvable simple groups such as A_5 due to the exponential circuit emulation depth required to simulate non-abelian commutators. We propose Non-Commutative State Space Models (NC-SSM), their real-orthogonal counterpart SO(3)-SSM, and arbitrary-dimension Cayley-SSM, lifting state transitions to compact Lie groups SU(2), SO(3), and SO(N). Via closed-form Euler-Rodrigues maps and rational Cayley transforms, NC-SSM achieves exact norm-preserving isometry (||U_t|| = 1). We introduce pure Hopf-fibration Bloch projective readouts (S^3/{+-1} =~ S^2 =~ SO(3)) to eliminate sign ambiguity, true quaternion parallel prefix scans (9.06x speedup at T=2048), and Identity-Gated Lie SSMs to eliminate sparse syntax phase drift. Extensive benchmarks across 14 experimental regimes show: (1) NC-SSM achieves 100% tracking on S_3, D_4, Q_8 and simple group A_5, where a 3-layer deep diagonal baseline collapses to 6.60% (p = 8.81e-4); (2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM breaks Klein's 1884 ceiling on symmetric group S_5 (50.92% vs diagonal 5.25%, p = 0.0015, delivering 7.8x variance reduction over SO(3)); (3) SO(3)-SSM preserves Riemannian manifolds across 300 steps (< 3.12e-6 drift, > 580,000x advantage), achieving 0.04 deg dead-reckoning error and active tangent denoising; (4) NC-SSM achieves 74.36% on Dyck-2 and 30.26% on deep AST scope tracking (p = 0.0081); and (5) ablation confirms strict isometry is mathematically necessary for lossless long-range associative memory.

</details>


### [174] [Platonic Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00929)
*Junghwan Park,Woojin Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"柏拉图任务向量"概念，将不同模型中相似的任务更新视为一个共享的、与模型无关对象的"影子"，并设计"通用任务描述符"这一架构无关的矩阵对象来捕获任务的功能效应，实现跨异构模型的、无需每图标签的任务知识迁移与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一任务的不同模型会收敛到相似行为，但它们各自的参数更新缺乏公共坐标系，导致基于权重空间的任务算术只能局限于单一模型内部，无法在缺乏结构对应关系的不同架构之间迁移。作者假设这些模型特定的更新是一个共享的、与模型无关对象的影子，希望找到并操作这一公共对象，从而打破跨架构迁移的限制。

Method: 作者引入通用任务描述符（Universal Task Descriptors）：形状与架构和嵌入维度无关的矩阵，记录任务的功能效应，并支持矩阵加法与取反。迁移时通过在任务的未标注探测图像和类名提示上编辑目标模型，使其复现该描述符（无需逐图标签）。实现有两种方式：其一，将描述符分解为图像嵌入上的位移场，用一次最小二乘求解得到线性算子，折叠进目标模型最后一层作为权重编辑；利用线性性，一组算子可通过带符号求和实现任意组合与任意强度。其二，在相同目标上训练低秩适配器，可触及所有层并联合拟合组合，代价是每次编辑需一次优化。

Result: 异构模型仅部分共享该对象，模型特定残差的范数与共享分量相当；尽管如此，跨模型迁移仍能保留目标模型自身描述符增益的74%–80%。在六个模型家族、八个分类任务及一个音频-文本设置上的实验表明，任务知识可通过两种实现方式在异构模型间迁移和组合。

Conclusion: 任务知识可以在异构模型之间迁移与组合，验证了"柏拉图任务向量"作为共享、模型无关对象的存在性与可操作性。通用任务描述符提供了一种不依赖架构和嵌入维度、无需逐图标签的跨模型任务算术框架，为跨架构的知识复用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Models specialized for the same task converge to similar behavior, yet the parameter updates that produce it share no common coordinate system, so weight-space task arithmetic stays confined to a single model and cannot cross architectures without a structural correspondence. Drawing on Plato's allegory of the cave, we hypothesize that these model-specific updates are shadows of one shared, model-agnostic object, which we call the platonic task vector. To make it operational for models that pair an image or audio encoder with a text encoder, we introduce Universal Task Descriptors: matrices whose shape is independent of architecture and embedding dimension, which record a task's functional effect and support addition and negation as matrix operations. Transferring a descriptor into a target means editing the target until it reproduces the descriptor on the task's unlabeled probe images and class-name prompts, requiring no per-image labels. We realize this edit in two ways. First, the descriptor factorizes into a shift field on image embeddings, so a single least-squares solve yields a linear operator that folds into the target's last layer as a weight edit; by linearity, a bank of such operators admits any composition at any strength as a signed sum. Second, a low-rank adapter trained on the same objective reaches every layer and fits compositions jointly, at the cost of one optimization per edit. Heterogeneous models share this object only partially, with a model-specific residual comparable in norm to the shared component, yet cross-model transfer still retains 74-80 percent of the gain of the target's own descriptors. Experiments across six model families, eight classification tasks, and an audio-text setting show that task knowledge transfers and composes across heterogeneous models under both realizations.

</details>


### [175] [The Weakest Link: Distilling LLM Reasoning with Worst-Case Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00332)
*Matthieu Zimmer,Xiaotong Ji,Tu Nguyen,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理蒸馏建模为受最坏情况约束的强化学习问题，通过一种无增广的约束MDP奖励变换，在最大化任务奖励的同时，对轨迹每个前缀的教师对数似然施加硬约束，从而在数学推理与代码生成任务上显著扩展了准确率-保真度帕累托前沿，并取得最高的严格推理成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM推理蒸馏存在根本张力：(1) 纯追求可验证任务奖励（如GRPO）会导致奖励黑客行为——学生以错误的中间逻辑得到正确最终答案；(2) 用对教师的软散度正则（如KL蒸馏）会稀释任务性能，且其平均性质允许学生在某一步出现严重逻辑违规、却用其他步骤的高教师一致性来补偿。作者认为这种"平均化"与推理的本质错位：思维链的有效性取决于其最薄弱环节。

Method: 将推理蒸馏形式化为受约束强化学习问题：在轨迹每个前缀的教师对数似然上施加最坏情况约束的前提下最大化任务奖励。为避免对偶拉格朗日求解器的高昂成本，以及Saute等状态增广方法对测试时教师的依赖，作者推导出一种无增广的约束MDP，其奖励变换保留硬约束语义，得到低方差的策略梯度分解（单步项与长期项），并证明在惩罚极限下几乎必然满足最坏情况约束。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，该方法显著扩展了准确率-保真度帕累托前沿；在匹配纯RL的高最终答案正确率的同时，大幅降低了教师约束违规，从而在所有评估设置中取得最高的严格推理成功率。

Conclusion: 通过以最坏情况（而非平均）教师一致性约束来规范推理蒸馏，能够有效抑制奖励黑客行为、保持任务性能，并显著提升严格意义上的推理成功率，为高效部署小模型提供了更对齐推理本质的蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Distilling the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) into smaller students is a central challenge for efficient deployment. Current approaches face a fundamental tension: optimizing purely for verifiable task rewards (e.g., via GRPO) leads to reward hacking, where students arrive at correct final answers through flawed intermediate logic, while regularizing with soft divergence penalties against a teacher (e.g., KL-based distillation) dilutes task performance and, critically, allows the student to compensate for severe logical violations at one step with high teacher agreement at others. We argue that this averaging is fundamentally misaligned with the nature of reasoning: a chain-of-thought is only as valid as its weakest link. Motivated by this observation, we formulate reasoning distillation as a constrained reinforcement learning problem in which the task reward is maximized subject to a worst-case constraint on the teacher log-likelihood along every prefix of the trajectory. To avoid the prohibitive cost of dual Lagrangian solvers and the test-time teacher dependence of state-augmented methods such as Saute, we derive an unaugmented constrained MDP whose reward transformation preserves the hard-constraint semantics, admits a low-variance policy gradient decomposition into single-step and long-term terms, and provably satisfies the worst-case constraint almost surely in the penalty limit. Through extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks, we demonstrate that our method significantly expands the accuracy-fidelity Pareto front. By matching the high Final Answer Correctness of pure RL and drastically reducing teacher constraint violations, we ultimately achieve the highest rigorous Reasoning Success Rate across all evaluated settings.

</details>


### [176] [Benchmarking System One decision models against trained classifiers and language models for automated decision gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00346)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在匹配条件下比较了三类"系统一"决策模型（决策型、监督分类器、生成式语言模型）用于自动决策门控的表现，发现模型类别的优劣取决于具体条件，并据此提出条件依赖的设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化系统需要把分支决策交给模型，且要求模型既能声明选项又能给出可设阈值的概率。已有的监督分类器和生成式语言模型各有局限，研究希望系统评估"带类型的决策模型"（系统一模型）相比这两类既有方案在真实决策任务上的表现，为工程化部署提供依据。

Method: 构建统一评测框架（一个 harness），在匹配条件下向来自六个模型家族的八个决策模型检查点（含托管模型 Jev）和两个生成式对照模型发送相同语义请求，并在相同的工作流（workflow）、意图（intent）和社会科学（social-science）条目上对监督分类器和零样本分类器打分。考察维度包括：有无任务自带标签时的准确率、五种风险水平下的接受率、选项键似然的读取、温度标定与多选项下的校准误差、留出阈值检测分布外请求、是/否选项交换的翻转率，以及"意图训练的一阶段 + 升级到 Jev"的级联成本-准确率。

Result: 模型类别排名取决于条件：有任务自有标签时，小型训练分类器在意图上最准确，在工作流上与最佳决策模型无显著差异；无标签时，除编码器型检查点外的所有决策模型在工作流和意图上都超过零样本蕴含分类器。通过选项键似然，更大的生成式模型在工作流和意图上与 Jev 持平，且在 5% 风险下接受更多工作流决策；微调后的决策检查点相比未调骨干在意图准确率上有所提升。用少量选项拟合的存储温度在多选项时抬高校准误差；留出的 5% 分布内风险阈值仍让 Jev 接受 0.310 的分布外请求。交换 yes/no 使 Jev 每百条翻转 50.5 条答案，而微调检查点降低了其骨干在社会科学条目上的翻转。以意图训练的一阶段模型升级到 Jev 的级联，在 Jev 准确率下仅需其满 GPU 利用率时 0.43 的成本。

Conclusion: 不同决策模型类别各有所长，且没有单一类别在所有条件下胜出；部署自动决策门时应遵循条件依赖的设计规则，例如在有标签时优先小型训练分类器、在无标签时倾向决策模型或更大的生成式模型，并可用级联一阶段模型降低托管大模型的推理成本，同时需注意选项敏感性、校准和分布外稳健性等隐患。

Abstract: Software that hands branching decisions to a model needs a declared option and a probability it can threshold. Typed decision models, also called System One models, return such probabilities without generating text, while supervised classifiers and generative language models are the established alternatives. Under matched conditions, one harness sends eight decision-model checkpoints from six families, including the hosted model Jev, and two generative comparators the same semantic requests, and scores supervised and zero-shot classifiers on the same workflow, intent and social-science items. The ranking of the model classes depends on the conditions. With the task's own labels, small trained classifiers are the most accurate on intents and not significantly different from the best decision models on workflows. Without labels, every decision model except the encoder-based checkpoints exceeds a zero-shot entailment classifier on workflows and intents. Read through option-key likelihoods, a larger generative model is level with Jev on workflows and intents and accepts more workflow decisions at five percent risk, and fine-tuned decision checkpoints gain intent accuracy over their untuned backbones. Stored temperatures fitted on few options raise calibration error with many options, and a held-out threshold for five percent in-scope risk still lets Jev accept 0.310 of out-of-scope requests. Swapping yes and no flips 50.5 answers per hundred for Jev, while fine-tuned checkpoints cut their backbones' social-science flips. An intent-trained first stage escalating to Jev matches its accuracy at 0.43 of its cost at full graphics-processor utilization. The results yield condition-dependent design rules for automated decision gates.

</details>


### [177] [Counterfactual Generation via Flow Matching: Coupling-Sensitive End-to-End Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01193)
*Yunrui Guan,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流匹配的反事实生成方法，结合样本分割的双重鲁棒训练目标与拟合条件结果模型得到的源-目标耦合，并采用高斯平滑插值下的分数校正随机采样器实现有限步生成；理论核心是给出耦合敏感的KL界（误差受源-目标位移矩控制，与关键维度近线性相关），并建立速度场与分数场的有限样本非参数保证，最终得到端到端反事实生成保证，实验验证了耦合依赖理论及随机采样器在有限预算下优于确定性ODE采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实生成旨在利用事实分配机制下的观测数据，在假设干预或决策下采样结果。现有生成模型方法通常依赖速度场的全局一致正则性假设且缺乏端到端的统计保证，无法刻画条件结果模型与耦合估计带来的误差传播，也无法在有限步离散化下给出可靠的理论支撑。

Method: 1) 提出流匹配框架，将样本分割的双重鲁棒训练目标与从拟合条件结果模型中抽取的源-目标学习耦合相结合；2) 引入基于高斯平滑插值的分数校正随机采样器，实现有限步生成；3) 理论分析：建立常数步长Euler离散化的耦合敏感KL界，误差由所选耦合下源-目标位移的矩控制，而非速度场全局一致正则性，且与环境维度近线性相关；4) 对学习的速度场与分数场建立有限样本非参数保证，分解近似、耦合替换、nuisance估计、泛化与蒙特卡洛误差；5) 将采样器分析与统计界结合，得到端到端反事实生成保证；6) 在合成与半合成图像基准上验证。

Result: 理论结果：常数步Euler离散化的KL误差由源-目标位移矩控制，具有近线性的维度依赖；速度场与分数场的有限样本非参数界将各类误差来源显式分离；综合得到端到端反事实生成保证。实验：合成与半合成图像基准支持耦合依赖理论，并显示在有限离散化预算下，随机采样器可优于对应的确定性ODE采样器。

Conclusion: 所提出的流匹配反事实生成方法通过双重鲁棒目标与数据驱动的源-目标耦合，在理论与实验上均表现良好。耦合敏感的KL界表明误差不依赖速度场的全局一致正则性，且维度依赖近线性；端到端理论保证与实验结果共同说明该方法在有限步生成设定下具有优势，随机采样器在有限预算下优于确定性ODE采样器。

Abstract: Counterfactual generation seeks to sample outcomes under a hypothetical intervention or decision using observational data collected under the factual assignment mechanism. We develop a flow-matching approach that combines a sample-split, doubly robust training objective with a learned coupling between observed source outcomes and target outcomes drawn from a fitted conditional outcome model. To enable finite-step generation, we leverage a score-corrected stochastic sampler based on a Gaussian-smoothed interpolation. Our main theoretical contribution is a coupling-sensitive KL bound for constant-step Euler discretization: the error is controlled by moments of the source--target displacement under the chosen coupling, rather than by global uniform regularity of the velocity field, and has near-linear dependence on the ambient dimension. We also establish finite-sample non-parametric guarantees for the learned velocity and score fields when both the conditional outcome model and the source-target coupling are estimated from data. These bounds separate approximation, coupling-replacement, nuisance-estimation, generalization, and Monte Carlo errors and, combined with the sampler analysis, yield an end-to-end guarantee for counterfactual generation. Experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic image benchmarks support the coupling-dependent theory and show that, at finite discretization budgets, the stochastic sampler can outperform the corresponding deterministic ODE sampler.

</details>


### [178] [Deep Learning for Anomaly Detection in Railway Systems: A Structured Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00363)
*Ammar Bouketta,Smail Niar,Hamza Ouarnoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇针对铁路系统异常检测的深度学习方法的系统性综述，提出统一分类法，梳理各类模型架构与学习范式，并分析数据、评估与部署方面的挑战，最终给出面向决策的选型框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代铁路系统的安全可靠运行日益依赖于数据驱动的监测与智能故障检测；随着机车车辆与基础设施多源异构传感器数据的大量积累，深度学习成为铁路异常检测的有效范式，但相关方法庞杂、缺乏统一梳理，亟需结构化系统性总结以指导方法选择与工程部署。

Method: 本文采用结构化综述方法，构建统一分类法，从异常位置、数据表示与表现形式、感知模态、时间特性四个维度组织现有方法；将卷积、循环、注意力架构、自编码器、生成对抗网络与Transformer等方法归纳为基于分类、基于预测、基于重构和混合学习四类范式；并系统考察数据中心挑战、评估实践、性能指标及边缘-云架构、计算约束、硬件感知优化等实际部署问题。

Result: 综述将现有基于深度学习的铁路异常检测方法系统归类，厘清了不同架构与学习范式之间的对应关系，并提出了一个决策导向框架，将异常特征、数据属性与运行约束映射到合适的检测范式和部署配置，为选择与部署铁路异常检测深度学习方法提供了结构化参考。

Conclusion: 本文为铁路异常检测的深度学习解决方案提供了结构化的选型与部署参考，同时指出现有方法在可靠性与可扩展性方面仍存在开放挑战，为未来构建可靠、可扩展的智能监测系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and reliable operation of modern railway systems increasingly relies on data-driven monitoring and intelligent fault detection. Deep learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for railway anomaly detection, driven by the growing availability of heterogeneous sensor data from rolling stock and infrastructure. This paper presents a structured survey of deep learning-based anomaly detection approaches for railway systems. The surveyed methods are organized using a unified taxonomy covering anomaly location, data representation and manifestation, sensing modality, and temporal characteristics. Existing approaches, including convolutional, recurrent and attention-based architectures, autoencoders, generative adversarial networks, and transformers, are structured into classification-based, prediction-based, reconstruction-based, and hybrid learning paradigms. The survey also examines data-centric challenges, evaluation practices, performance metrics, and practical deployment aspects, including edge-cloud architectures, computational constraints, and hardware-aware optimization. Finally, a decision-oriented framework links anomaly characteristics, data properties, and operational constraints to suitable detection paradigms and deployment configurations. This work provides a structured reference for selecting and deploying deep learning solutions for railway anomaly detection and highlights open challenges toward reliable and scalable intelligent monitoring systems.

</details>


### [179] [IQS-BO: In-Context Query Selection for Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01269)
*Luca Geminiani,Nadja Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IQS-BO，一种基于 Prior-data Fitted Network（PFN）的贝叶斯优化方法，通过监督学习在单次前向传播中直接预测每个候选点最大化目标函数的概率，从而无需数值最大化采集函数即可完成查询决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化（BO）每一步都需要重新拟合代理模型并数值最大化采集函数，计算开销大。现有 in-context 方法中，PFNs4BO 虽摊销了代理模型但保留了数值采集函数优化，FIBO 则完全固定决策规则且无代理。作者希望既保留决策的灵活性，又能通过监督学习实现全 in-context 的查询决策，避免强化学习或数值优化带来的问题。

Method: 提出 IQS-BO，一个 PFN 模型，在单次前向传播中预测候选集合中每个点最大化目标的概率，并证明该目标的最小化对应事件“该候选为集合中最大”的后验概率。模型可端到端预训练用于全 in-context BO，也可接收固定概率代理模型的预测作为额外输入、仅摊销决策步骤。此外提出一种混合先验用于预训练 PFN，结合 GP 样本与具有扭曲输入、孤立窄谷或平台等难以被平稳核建模的函数。

Result: IQS-BO 的查询成本仅为基于采集函数方法的一小部分，在合成与真实世界基准上匹配或优于标准 GP-BO 及现有 in-context 方法。在混合先验上预训练可进一步提升优化性能。

Conclusion: IQS-BO 通过监督学习实现查询决策，显著降低计算成本，并在性能上达到或超过现有方法；提出的混合先验能够更好地覆盖 GP 代理难以建模的目标函数，从而提升预训练模型在优化任务上的表现。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimisation (BO) is a powerful framework for the optimisation of expensive black-box functions, but typically requires refitting a surrogate and maximising an acquisition function at every evaluation step. In-context approaches based on Prior-data Fitted Networks (PFNs) amortise part of this cost by pre-training transformers on functions drawn from synthetic priors. PFNs4BO amortises the surrogate but still relies on a numerically maximised acquisition function, while FIBO performs BO fully in-context by sampling optimiser locations from a learned density, which fixes the decision rule and admits no surrogate. Learned acquisition functions score a finite candidate set with a trained network, but, lacking a label for the query, learn the score by reinforcement learning on previously solved tasks. We propose IQS-BO, a PFN that learns the query decision by supervised learning on synthetic priors. In a single forward pass, IQS-BO predicts the probability that each candidate maximises the objective over the set, and we show that the minimiser of its objective is the posterior probability of this event. The model can be pre-trained without a surrogate for fully in-context BO, or take the predictions of a fixed probabilistic surrogate as additional input, amortising only the decision step. Our method proposes queries at a fraction of the cost of acquisition-based methods, while either matching or outperforming standard BO with Gaussian processes (GPs) and available in-context methods on synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Finally, we propose a mixture prior for pre-training PFNs which combines samples from GPs with functions exhibiting warped inputs, isolated narrow optima, or plateaus that are poorly modeled by stationary kernels common in GP surrogates. We show that pre-training on this prior can lead to improved optimisation performance.

</details>


### [180] [Manifold-Constrained Initial Noise Optimization for Efficient Generative Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00365)
*Jinho Chang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZeNOVA，一种无需梯度的初始噪声对齐方法，通过退火软值引导、流形约束的超球面 Langevin 动力学与 Metropolis-Hastings 跳转，在图像和视频生成模型上稳定且高效地实现黑盒奖励对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏与流图模型使确定性少步生成成为可能，催生了直接优化高斯初始噪声的奖励对齐方法。但现有初始噪声优化多依赖一阶梯度，在黑盒奖励场景下要么不可用，要么不稳定、低效，亟需一种稳定高效的免梯度初始噪声对齐方案。

Method: 提出 ZeNOVA，采用梯度无关方式对齐初始噪声，核心包含三项技术：退火软值引导（annealed soft-value guidance）用于缓解黑盒场景下主要挑战；流形约束的超球面 Langevin 动力学（manifold-constrained hyperspherical Langevin dynamics）以利用高斯先验的几何结构；Metropolis-Hastings 跳转（Metropolis-Hastings jumping）提升优化稳定性与采样效率。

Result: 在图像与视频生成模型上的大量实验表明，ZeNOVA 在将初始噪声推向更高奖励方面显著优于所有评估的零阶基线方法，且稳定性大幅提升，同时有效利用高斯先验的几何性质，验证了其对多种黑盒奖励对齐任务的实用适用性。

Conclusion: ZeNOVA 为黑盒奖励场景下的初始噪声对齐提供了一种稳定、高效且免梯度的解决方案，成功解决了现有算法依赖一阶梯度所导致的不可用、不稳定和低效问题，展现出良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Recent advances in distillation and flow-map models have enabled deterministic one- or few-step generation for high-quality data, facilitating a new branch of reward alignment approaches that directly optimize the initial noise from a Gaussian distribution. However, most existing initial-noise optimization methods rely on first-order gradient information, which is either inapplicable or suffers from instability and inefficiency in black-box reward scenarios. Here, we introduce ZeNOVA, a stable and efficient initial noise alignment method in a gradient-free manner. Specifically, we address existing algorithms' major challenge in black-box scenarios through annealed soft-value guidance, manifold-constrained hyperspherical Langevin dynamics, and Metropolis-Hastings jumping. Extensive experiments on image and video generative models show that ZeNOVA outperforms all evaluated zeroth-order baselines by optimizing the initial noise toward higher rewards substantially more stably while exploiting the geometry of the Gaussian prior, demonstrating its practical applicability to various black-box reward alignment.

</details>


### [181] [Clifford Sheaf Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01322)
*Kotaro Kamiya,Joel Nicholls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Clifford 层神经网络（CSNN），一种基于胞腔层（cellular sheaf）的几何图等变网络，以 Clifford 代数作为茎（stalk）上的代数结构，并通过 K-term sandwich 构造限制映射，从而得到半正定的层拉普拉斯算子，无需 versor 约束即可实现阶次混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为几何图设计等变的层神经网络时，茎上代数取 Clifford 代数后，限制映射的朴素等变选择是 versor 共轭；但其因必须是代数同态而表达力过弱。因此需要突破代数同态约束，寻找既能保持等变性、又能保证正半定性与阶次混合能力的限制映射族。

Method: 放弃代数同态，采用 K-term sandwich 作为限制映射，并由此构造层拉普拉斯算子。文章沿三个轴刻画所得限制映射族：耦合哪些阶（grade）、覆盖自同态空间的多少比例、以及条件数好坏；用项数 K 调控表达力。

Result: K-term sandwich 覆盖自同态空间的一半；在 Cl(3,0,0) 中它恰好对应与中心伪标量（pseudoscalar）可交换的映射。项数控制表达力。CSNN 是其中 reversion 成员，按构造即为一阶模型，且处于该族的阶次混合角落，可视为面向图级等变回归的层构造。

Conclusion: CSNN 通过 K-term sandwich 构造出一种等变、正半定且可实现阶次混合的层神经网络，摆脱了 versor 约束与代数同态的表达力瓶颈，并系统刻画了该限制映射族的性质，为图级等变回归提供了新型层构造工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Clifford Sheaf Neural Network (CSNN), an equivariant sheaf neural network for geometric graphs that places a Clifford algebra on each stalk of a cellular sheaf and transports multivector features along edges. The canonical choice of restriction map for sheaves with algebra-valued stalks is algebra homomorphism. Adding the constraint of equivariance, the naive choice becomes versor conjugation. However, versor conjugation is expressively weak, so we drop algebra homomorphism and arrive at the K-term sandwich. The resulting sheaf Laplacian is positive semidefinite by construction, needs no versor constraint, and still mixes grades. Our main contribution characterizes the resulting family of restriction maps along three axes: which grades a map couples, how much of the endomorphism space it reaches, and how well it is conditioned. The K-term sandwich spans half of the endomorphism space, and in Cl(3, 0, 0) it corresponds to the maps that commute with the central pseudoscalar. The number of terms controls expressivity. CSNN is the reversion member, a first-order model by construction and the grade-mixing corner of this family, developed as a sheaf construction for graph-level equivariant regression.

</details>


### [182] [MoRA: MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00367)
*Yushuai Sun,Zikun Zhou,Lin Gao,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoRA，一种面向 MoE 模型的结构化专家剪枝框架，通过学习路由器偏置（Router Bias Learning）和专家近似（Expert Approximation）机制，在去除 25% 和 50% 路由专家的同时保持模型性能，并在多个基准上超越现有剪枝算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型虽能通过稀疏激活实现参数扩展，但部署仍需将完整专家池加载进内存。结构化专家剪枝可降低内存占用，但现有方法存在两大问题：一是专家排序标准与模型性能对齐不佳，二是有效的专家子集搜索计算开销过大；同时，现有方法普遍忽视了保留专家之间存在的路由行为冗余。

Method: 提出 MoRA 框架，包含两部分：(1) 为每个专家引入可学习的路由器偏置，通过最小化语言建模损失和路由多样性正则项来优化偏置，使路由概率分布更加尖锐，从而识别对性能关键的专家，同时鼓励选择路由偏好多样的专家；(2) 引入专家近似机制作为剪枝后的增强手段，利用剩余专家通过仿射变换近似被剪枝专家的输出，进一步提升剪枝后模型的性能。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite 和 Moonlight-16B-A3B 三个 MoE 模型上，对每个 MoE 层去除 25% 和 50% 的路由专家，在九个零样本基准上的广泛实验表明，MoRA 优于现有的最先进剪枝算法。

Conclusion: MoRA 通过路由器偏置学习与专家近似，有效解决了 MoE 结构化剪枝中专家排序标准不对齐、子集搜索开销大以及路由行为冗余被忽视的问题，实现了更优的剪枝性能，代码将开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable parameter scaling with limited per-token computation by activating only a small subset of experts for each token, but deploying them still requires loading the complete expert pool into memory. Structured expert pruning can effectively reduce the memory usage by removing experts. However, existing pruning methods either use expert ranking criteria that are not well aligned with model performance or rely on effective expert subset searching that is computationally expensive. Moreover, these methods typically overlook the routing-behavior redundancy among the retained experts. In this paper, we propose MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation (MoRA), a framework for structured MoE expert pruning. We introduce a learnable router bias for each expert and optimize these biases by minimizing the language-modeling loss and a routing-diversity regularizer. The learned router biases sharpen the routing probability distributions to identify experts critical to model performance while encouraging the selection of experts with diverse routing preferences. In addition, we introduce an expert approximation mechanism as a post-pruning enhancement. It leverages the remaining experts to approximate the outputs of pruned experts by affine transformation, further improving the performance of the pruned model. We evaluate MoRA on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, removing 25\% and 50\% of the routed experts in each MoE layer. Extensive experiments on nine zero-shot benchmarks show that MoRA outperforms state-of-the-art pruning algorithms. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [183] [Tight Transition Time Bounds for Separable Logistic Regression at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01459)
*Haodong Wen,Kaiyue Wen,Jiaye Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性可分数据上逻辑回归在梯度下降（大步长 η）下的过渡时间，证明了最坏情况过渡时间为 Θ((log η)^{min{n-2,d-2}})，推翻了此前"与 η 无关"的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降用大步长训练逻辑回归时会出现"稳定边缘"（Edge of Stability）现象：损失先震荡后转为单调下降的稳定阶段。此前工作仅在 d=2 维给出 η→∞ 时的紧界 Θ(1)，并猜想任意 d≥2 维下过渡时间与 η 无关。本文旨在检验并推翻该猜想，给出任意维度下与 η 相关的精确最坏情况界。

Method: 针对固定样本量 n≥2 与足够小间隔 γ 的情形，作者对维度 d 与样本量 n 做双重归纳，控制"对梯度贡献最大的样本会随迭代反复切换"这一关键难点，从而建立过渡时间的上界，并构造匹配的困难实例证明下界，最终得到紧的 Θ((log η)^{min{n-2,d-2}}) 界，且该界对 d≥2 一致成立。

Result: 证明了最坏情况过渡时间为 Θ((log η)^{min{n-2,d-2}})，均匀地对所有 d≥2 成立；这一结果直接推翻了此前"过渡时间与 η 无关"的猜想，说明过渡时间随步长 η 呈对数多项式的量级增长。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了大步长梯度下降在逻辑回归稳定边缘现象中的过渡时间紧界问题，揭示了过渡时间对步长 η 的对数多项式依赖，并通过归纳与困难实例构造给出了匹配的上下界，修正了以往对 η 无关性的错误猜想。

Abstract: We study logistic regression on linearly separable data under gradient descent with a large constant stepsize $η$. Such dynamics may exhibit a characteristic Edge of Stability phenomenon, in which the loss initially oscillates before transitioning to a stable phase of monotone decrease. Existing work provides a tight $Θ(1)$ bound in dimension $d=2$ as $η\to \infty$ and conjectures a bound independent of $η$ in arbitrary dimensions $d\geq 2$. In this paper, we disprove this conjecture by showing that, for every fixed sample size $n\geq 2$ and sufficiently small margin $γ$, the worst-case transition time is $$Θ\!\left((\logη)^{\min\{n-2,d-2\}}\right)$$ uniformly over $d\geq2$. The key challenge in establishing a tight bound is that the sample contributing most strongly to the gradient can change repeatedly across iterations. To address this issue, we control such changes by induction on dimension and sample size, and construct matching hard instances.

</details>


### [184] [When Do Attention-Head Ablations Support Causal Claims? Projection-Level Confounds, Floor Effects, and Matched Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00373)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出注意力头消融实验的因果推断极易脆弱，除非干预语义、评估指标与对照方法均经过严格验证；研究发现常见的一些实现方式（如投影后置零）与修正后的置零几乎不相关，二元准确率会掩盖作用，而正确的干预位置、连续非饱和指标和匹配留出对照是可靠解释的前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力头消融（将某头置零并观察任务表现变化）是推断语言模型组件因果责任的常用方法，但该方法在实现语义、指标选择和统计控制上的有效性尚未经过系统检验，导致因果推断可能并不可靠。

Method: 以 GPT-2 small 和 DistilGPT2 为对象：对比"投影后置零"与"投影前置零（修正后）"两种干预语义的相关性及所选重要头集合的重叠度；对比二元准确率与 gold-token 对数概率两种指标；采用发现集/留出集划分，并抽取 1,000 组随机头与层匹配头作为对照，通过 Spearman 相关与 Monte Carlo 检验评估排序稳定性与显著性。

Result: 投影后置零与修正后的投影前置零几乎不相关（Pearson r = 0.057），且两者的 top-5 重要头完全不相交；二元准确率会在地板/天花板处掩盖效应，而 gold-token 对数概率保持梯度区分；修正后的逐头效应排序在发现集与留出集间高度稳定（Spearman rho = 0.974），top-5 头显著超过两种对照分布（Monte Carlo p = 0.001），但 GPT-2 上的任务特异性证据不稳健；DistilGPT2 复现保留了干预语义与匹配对照的相关结论。

Conclusion: 单头消融本身不足以支持因果论断；可靠的解释要求正确的干预位置、非饱和的连续指标以及匹配的留出对照，否则推断结果可能误导。

Abstract: Attention-head ablation, zeroing a head and measuring the resulting change in task performance, is a common method for inferring which components of a language model are causally responsible for a behavior. We show using GPT-2 small that this inference can be fragile unless the intervention semantics, evaluation metric, and controls are carefully validated. A natural post-projection implementation of "zeroing a head" is nearly uncorrelated with a corrected pre-projection ablation (Pearson r = 0.057) and selects a completely disjoint top-5 set of important heads. We also show that binary accuracy can hide effects at behavioral floors and near ceilings, whereas gold-token log-probability remains graded. Using a discovery/held-out split and 1,000 matched random-head and layer-matched-head control draws, the corrected per-head effect ranking is highly stable across splits (Spearman rho = 0.974), and the top-5 selected heads significantly exceed both control distributions (Monte Carlo p = 0.001). However, evidence for task specificity is not robust on GPT-2. Replication on DistilGPT2 preserves the intervention-semantic and matched-control findings. These results show that single-head ablation does not by itself justify a causal claim; defensible interpretation requires correct intervention placement, a non-saturated continuous metric, and matched held-out controls.

</details>


### [185] [Langevin-Informed Transfer Learning: Replacing Target Samples by Black-Box Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01522)
*Vladimir R. Kostic,Karim Lounici,Hélène Halconruy,Timothée Devergne,Michele Parrinello,Massimiliano Pontil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LITL 框架，利用黑盒反馈从有偏/静态样本中恢复目标 Langevin 动力学，学习目标无穷小生成元的谱结构并估计投影漂移，附有有限样本理论保证与多种实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与机器学习系统由具有低维慢变结构的随机动力学支配，但用于识别和解释这些动力学的目标轨迹往往不可得，只能获取探索底层流形的有偏或静态样本。因此需要一个能在此分布偏移下、仅凭黑盒反馈恢复目标 Langevin 动力学的通用框架。

Method: 提出 Langevin-Informed Transfer Learning（LITL），通过 Dirichlet 表示学习来学习目标无穷小生成元的主导谱结构及投影漂移，实现谱形式的动力学重建与慢流形梯度场估计；同时引入球面变体以适用于学习系统中常见的归一化潜表征的定向控制，并建立 Sobolev 范数下特征值、特征函数及投影漂移估计的有限样本保证。

Result: 理论层面给出特征值、特征函数及投影漂移估计在 Sobolev 范数下的有限样本界，保证这些量及其一阶导数的泛化。实验层面，LITL 从有偏分子模拟中恢复物理过渡时间尺度、从生成模型静态样本构建动力学结构、重建物理系统的球面对称性，并在黑盒反馈下实现对已训练神经网络潜表征的事后定向控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明谱算子学习可作为分布偏移下恢复随机动力学的实用框架，为机器学习和物理科学领域拓展了应用空间。

Abstract: Many scientific and machine learning systems, from molecular dynamics to diffusion models and beyond, are governed by stochastic dynamics with low-dimensional structure, evolving on slow timescales. However, target trajectories, used to identify and interpret such dynamics, are often inaccessible: only biased or static samples that explore the underlying manifold are available. We introduce Langevin-Informed Transfer Learning (LITL), a framework for recovering target Langevin dynamics from biased source samples using only black-box feedback. LITL learns the leading spectral structure of the target infinitesimal generator and the projected drift through Dirichlet representation learning, enabling kinetic reconstruction in spectral form and slow-manifold gradient field estimation. We further introduce a spherical variant well suited to steering normalized latent representations commonly used in learning systems toward desired objectives. We establish finite-sample guarantees for eigenvalue, eigenfunction, and projected drift estimation in Sobolev norms, thereby ensuring generalization of these quantities and their first-order derivatives. Empirically, LITL recovers physical transition timescales from biased molecular simulations, builds kinetic structure from static samples of generative models, reconstructs spherical symmetries of physical systems, and enables post-hoc latent steering of trained neural networks under black-box feedback. Together, these results position spectral operator learning as a practical framework for recovering stochastic dynamics under distribution shift and unlock applications across machine learning and the physical sciences.

</details>


### [186] [A First Glance at Jev for Network Traffic Classification: Accuracy, Processing Time, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00376)
*Shenghe Xu,Lifan Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We evaluate Jev on ten dataset-defined application labels in CESNET-QUICEXT-25 using only the first ten packets' sizes, directions, and inter-packet times. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first empirical study of general-purpose decision models, represented here by Jev, for application classification of network flows. Across 52,000 records from 26 collection weeks following the training period, 40 fixed labeled examples raise Jev's accuracy from 9.80% to 28.42%. Random Forest and Extra Trees trained on 8,000 records achieve 69.95% and 66.80% and outperform Jev in every week. Increasing Jev's context to 150 examples yields 34.50% on the first test week. On a paired 100-record subset, Jev with 40 examples achieves 29% accuracy at a median request time of 0.750 s, versus 37% and 6.036 s for the generative language model OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol with high reasoning effort through Azure; Jev also incurs lower API charges. The paired subset does not establish an accuracy advantage for either service, and the timing reflects different service configurations. Thus, labeled examples substantially improve Jev, but the tested Jev configurations remain less accurate than trained tree ensembles; unequal supervision budgets and fixed configurations prevent attributing the gap to a single cause.

</details>


### [187] [Exact Distinguishability in Non-Markovian Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01527)
*Kabir Murjani,Nisarg Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非马尔可夫环境（建模为正规决策过程 RDP）中，在固定行为策略下收集的数据能否区分两个候选模型的问题，提出 PEC 算法以线性时间判定观测等价性，并证明先验工作的可区分性假设在多数测试环境中失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 RDP 的离线保证依赖于行为策略上的可区分性假设，但缺乏验证该假设的手段；当假设被违反时，不同的模型可能对数据给出同样好的解释，导致离线学习无法识别真实环境。

Method: 作者形式化研究了固定行为策略下数据能否区分两个候选 RDP：证明了观测等价的候选模型之间后验几率在任意样本量下都保持等于先验几率（即使策略访问每个自动机状态）；在 Lean 4 中对两个关键结果做了形式化验证；随后给出观测等价性的精确刻画，并设计 PEC 算法，其运行时间与乘积自动机的大小成线性关系，用于判定该等价性。

Result: 证明了观测等价候选模型的后验几率恒等于先验几率；在 Lean 4 中形式化验证了相关定理；PEC 算法以线性时间判定观测等价性。在四个测试环境中，先验工作的可区分性假设有三个失败，而 PEC 识别出的实验在每个案例中都恢复了该假设。

Conclusion: 观测等价性是 RDP 离线学习中必须显式验证的关键障碍，而 PEC 提供了一种线性时间、可实践的方法来判定它，并可用于识别能恢复可区分性假设的实验。

Abstract: Non-Markovian environments are often modeled as Regular Decision Processes (RDPs), where dynamics depend on the interaction history through a finite automaton. Existing offline guarantees for RDPs rely on a distinguishability assumption on the behaviour policy but provide no means of verifying it. When the assumption is violated, distinct models may explain the data equally well. We study when data collected under a fixed behaviour policy can distinguish two candidate RDPs. We prove that the posterior odds between observationally equivalent candidates remain equal to the prior odds at every sample size, even when the policy visits every automaton state, and verify both results formally in Lean 4. We then characterize this equivalence exactly and derive PEC, an algorithm that decides it in time linear in the size of the product automaton. The distinguishability assumption of prior work fails on three of our four test environments, and the experiment identified by PEC restores it in each case.

</details>


### [188] [In-context Learning of Single-index Targets: Comparing Kernel and Feature Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01712)
*Haotian Gu,Yizhou Xu,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用副本方法分析两种单层注意力架构（核学习器与特征学习器）在非线性情境学习中的记忆与泛化误差，给出相图与上下文长度标度，揭示架构选择与数据集如何共同决定非线性 ICL 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ICL 理论多集中于线性目标函数，对非线性情形的理解不足。作者旨在通过比较同一族单指标任务上的两种单层注意力架构，系统研究预训练规模、任务池多样性及上下文长度等因素如何影响非线性情境学习的优劣。

Method: 在单指标任务族上对比两种单层注意力架构：核学习器先用固定非线性特征映射处理输入再用线性注意力，特征学习器则对原始输入施加注意力后用可学习的非线性读出层。作者运用副本方法（replica method）推导两者的记忆误差与泛化误差，并显式保留预训练数据量、任务池多样性、训练与推理上下文长度的影响，最后与数值实验对照验证。

Result: 理论预测在广泛参数范围内与数值实验高度吻合。分析得到相图，刻画了随预训练数据量、任务多样性与上下文长度变化时两种架构各自的优势区间，并发现两者呈现定性不同的上下文长度标度关系。

Conclusion: 架构选择与数据集特性共同决定非线性情境学习的行为：预训练数据量、任务多样性和上下文长度的不同组合会使核学习器与特征学习器各有优劣。该研究厘清了非线性 ICL 中架构与数据的交互机制。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables a pretrained model to infer a task from demonstrations without updating its parameters. While much of the existing theory focuses on linear target functions, in this paper we study nonlinear cases by comparing two one-layer attention architectures on the same family of single-index tasks. A kernel learner first maps inputs through a fixed nonlinear feature map and then applies linear attention, whereas a feature learner applies attention to the original input, followed by a learned nonlinear readout. We derive predictions for their memorization and generalization errors using the replica method, retaining the effects of pretraining size, task-pool diversity, and training and inference context lengths. The resulting predictions closely match numerical experiments across a broad range of regimes. Our analysis yields phase diagrams that characterize when each architecture is advantageous as the amount of pretraining data, task diversity, and context lengths vary. We further identify qualitatively different context-length scalings for the two learners. Together, these results clarify how architectural choices interact with the dataset and govern nonlinear in-context learning.

</details>


### [189] [Fusion techniques of time frequency-based images to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00380)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Mohammed Aledhari,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究用EEG时频图像预测rTMS治疗抑郁症的疗效，提出蒙太奇与混合两种融合方法并训练轻量CNN。结果显示，在允许同一患者片段跨训练/测试集泄漏的10折交叉验证下准确率虚高（主数据集99.90%），而在严格的受试者不相交交叉验证下几乎所有配置性能崩溃（AUC 0.31–0.54，置信区间含0.5），说明此前高精度结果源于数据泄漏，而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症可导致自杀与自伤，而预测rTMS治疗结局对临床医生极为困难。用EEG预测rTMS反应受限于高受试者间变异性及单域分析特征有限，因此需要更丰富的特征提取与融合方法，并验证预测是否真实可靠。

Method: 提出蒙太奇（montage）与混合（blending）两种融合技术，从EEG导出的时频（TF）图像中提取更丰富特征，并训练轻量自定义卷积神经网络（CNN）。采用主数据集（15例）与次数据集（46例），进行两类实验：(1) 片段级10折交叉验证（同一患者片段可同时出现在训练与测试中）；(2) 严格的受试者不相交交叉验证（同一患者所有片段只在该患者所属折中）。并测试四种时频方法、六种融合机制、十四种模型架构，辅以患者级置换检验。

Result: 片段级10折交叉验证：Montage CWT_ST融合在主数据集达99.90%准确率、次数据集91.90%。但在严格受试者不相交交叉验证下性能崩塌：除一个配置外，所有配置在两队列上AUC介于0.31与0.54之间，且每个95%置信区间均含0.5；最佳独立方法的患者级置换检验p=0.703。最佳受试者级结果为Montage CWT_ST在主队列，AUC 0.874±0.183、准确率82.7%。

Conclusion: 片段级交叉验证中允许同一患者片段跨训练/测试集会造成数据泄漏，导致看似极高的准确率，无法反映真实泛化能力。在严格的受试者不相交评估下，基于EEG时频图像与CNN的rTMS疗效预测几乎无预测力，说明该预测任务极为困难，需更谨慎的评估协议与更有效的方法。

Abstract: Depression is a mental condition that can lead to suicide and self-harm. Predicting the outcome of depression treatment is one of the most difficult tasks for clinicians. Among various treatment options, repetitive Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (rTMS) is a widely used non-invasive method. Predicting rTMS response using Electroencephalogram (EEG) data is difficult because of high inter-subject variability and limited features from single-domain analysis. We introduce two fusion techniques, montage and blending, to overcome these limitations and extract richer features from EEG-derived Time-Frequency (TF) images. We then propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained on fused TF representations. \textcolor{black}{We use a primary dataset of 15 patients and a secondary dataset of 46 patients. We run two sets of experiments. The first set uses segment-level 10-fold cross-validation. In this setup segments from the same patient can appear in both training and testing. The Montage CWT\_ST fusion reaches 99.90\% accuracy on the primary dataset and 91.90\% on the secondary dataset. The second set uses strict subject-disjoint cross-validation. All segments of a patient stay in one fold and no patient appears in both training and testing. Performance collapses. We test four time-frequency methods, six fusion mechanisms, and fourteen model architectures. With one exception, every configuration on both cohorts falls between AUC 0.31 and 0.54 and every 95\% confidence interval contains 0.5. A patient-level permutation test on the best standalone method returns $p = 0.703$. The best subject-level result is Montage CWT\_ST on the primary cohort, which reaches AUC $0.874 \pm 0.183$ and 82.7\% accuracy.

</details>


### [190] [Inferring Multi-Timescale Neural Dynamics with Switching Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01786)
*Lulu Gong,Yongxu Zhang,Shreya Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多时间尺度切换线性动力系统（MTS-SLDS），用于从连续或脉冲神经观测中识别随行为状态变化的潜在时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经活动常表现出多重时间尺度，且随行为状态和任务条件变化。传统基于自相关拟合的方法难以扩展到高维群体记录，在动力学随行为变化时也不可靠；标准状态空间（隐动力系统）模型虽能建模高维神经群体活动，但其标准形式与推断方法未显式考虑多重时间尺度，无法保证准确恢复底层时间结构。

Method: 提出多时间尺度切换线性动力系统（MTS-SLDS）。该方法结合：(1) 多滞后矩初始化（multi-lag moment initialization），在多个观测滞后上捕捉时间结构；(2) 状态条件化的 Laplace-EM 推断（regime-conditioned Laplace-EM），降低不同不确定状态下动力学统计量的混合。特征时间尺度可直接从学习到的潜在转移矩阵的特征值中提取。适用于高斯观测和泊松脉冲观测。

Result: 在合成数据和神经数据实验（高斯观测与泊松脉冲观测）中，MTS-SLDS 在多个数据集上都能准确恢复时间尺度和切换结构。

Conclusion: MTS-SLDS 提供了一种可靠框架，可在高维神经群体记录中识别与状态相关（regime-specific）的多重潜在时间尺度，克服了传统自相关方法与标准状态空间模型在多重时间尺度恢复上的局限。

Abstract: Neural activity often exhibits multiple timescales that can vary with behavioral states and task conditions. Identifying these timescales from neural recordings is important for better understanding neural computation and function. However, traditional approaches based on autocorrelation fitting are difficult to scale to high-dimensional population recordings and can become unreliable when neural dynamics change with behavior. State-space models have been a powerful framework for modeling high-dimensional neural population activity through latent dynamical systems, but standard formulations and inference methods do not explicitly account for multiple timescales and therefore do not guarantee accurate recovery of the underlying temporal structure. Motivated by these questions, we introduce the Multi-Timescale Switching Linear Dynamical System (MTS-SLDS), a framework for identifying regime-specific latent timescales from continuous or spiking neural observations. MTS-SLDS combines a multi-lag moment initialization, which captures temporal structure across multiple observation lags, with \textit{regime-conditioned} Laplace-EM inference, which reduces mixing of dynamical statistics across uncertain regimes. Characteristic timescales can then be extracted directly from the eigenvalues of the learned latent transition matrices. In synthetic and neural experiments with Gaussian and Poisson spike observations, MTS-SLDS accurately recovers timescales and switching structure over multiple datasets.

</details>


### [191] [OmniMed-Jev: Calibrating LVLM Confidence for Trustworthy Medical Multimodal Decisions via System One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00381)
*Luyao Tang,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniMed-Jev，一个将医疗决策表示为对候选集的条件化概率分布（Choice/Noul/Score）的多模态医学模型，使模型输出的置信度与真实准确率高度一致，显著降低校准误差与可靠性误差，但其泛化性证据有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学模型不仅取决于正确性，还取决于其报告的置信度是否与真实准确率匹配。现有通用多模态医学模型将诊断、发现或细胞计数等有界决策输出为生成文本，导致报告的概率反映的是下一个 token 而非决策本身，置信度不可信。

Method: 受 Jev 等决策原生接口启发，作者提出 OmniMed-Jev：将每个医疗决策表示为对运行时提供候选集的 Choice（互斥类别）、Noul（发现的有无）或 Score（有界有序值）决策，并输出该候选集上的完整概率分布；模型在相同骨干网络、数据和训练计划下训练，并与生成式基线进行接口受控比较。

Result: 在接口受控对比中，OmniMed-Jev 报告的概率与实际正确率一致性显著更高：校准误差降低最多一个数量级，可靠性误差降低最多两倍，而点预测性能与生成式基线相当；计数任务是生成式基线唯一保持领先的任务族。

Conclusion: 将决策分布作为模型输出并非仅是格式变化，而是让报告的数字成为名副其实的概率的关键。结果支持显式决策建模能使报告置信度在评估任务内有意义，但这并不构成临床就绪的证据；此外该对比无法将接口设计与相关训练差异分离，作者已在结果中同时说明。

Abstract: Medical models are judged not only on correctness, but on whether reported confidence matches actual accuracy. Generalist multimodal medical models have expanded what a single model can perceive, yet they still express bounded decisions such as diagnoses, findings or cell counts as generated text, so the reported probability reflects the next token rather than the decision itself. Motivated by decision-native interfaces such as Jev, we introduce OmniMed-Jev, which represents each medical decision as a Choice, Noul or Score decision over a runtime-supplied candidate set and returns a full distribution over that set: mutually exclusive classes, binary presence of a finding, or a bounded ordered value. The design is omni in three respects: it accepts diverse imaging modalities, covers different prediction tasks, and expresses them through one candidate-conditioned probability model, so heterogeneous outputs become comparable probabilities rather than task-specific strings. In an interface-controlled comparison against a generative baseline trained on the same backbone, data and schedule, OmniMed-Jev's reported probabilities track observed correctness far more closely, reducing calibration error by up to an order of magnitude and reliability error by up to two, while point-prediction performance remains comparable; counting is the one family where the generative baseline stays ahead. Making the decision distribution the model's output is not a format change but what turns reported numbers into probabilities that mean what they say. These results support explicit decision modeling as a way to make reported confidence meaningful within the evaluated tasks, and they are not evidence of clinical readiness: the comparison cannot separate the interface from associated training differences, which we state alongside the results. Code is available at github.com/lytang63/OmniMed-Jev.

</details>


### [192] [EvoGen-Harness: Learning Where and How to Evolve Image-Generation Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00383)
*Jiabin Luo,Yinan Liu,Chunlei Meng,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvoGen-Harness 框架与 Trace 方法，在冻结的文本到图像系统中同时演化多个生成器外部职责（提示、路由、工作流等），用失败归因引导搜索，显著超越单维度适配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像系统的提升方法通常只优化单一维度（提示、路由或工作流），限制了修正生成失败的空间；而允许多个外部职责共同演化虽扩大了适配空间，却面临"视觉反馈只知道失败、不知道该在哪演化、如何高效探索"的难题。

Method: 提出生成器无关的多职责演化框架 EvoGen-Harness，以及 Trace（Trajectory-Relative Attribution and Coordinated Evolution）方法：跨随机执行聚合证据、以失败归因作为搜索先验聚焦候选更新、逐步对残余失败重新归因以协调各职责的演化；同时引入 No-Patch 与留出验证机制，避免不必要或有害的更新。

Result: 在 GenEval2、T2I-CompBench++ 和 WISE 上，EvoGen-Harness 相较最强基线分别提升 +0.2633、+0.0720 和 +0.0752；同时达到 87.9–91.4% 的归因召回率、94.8% 的 No-Patch 准确率，以及仅 1.9% 的性能回退。

Conclusion: 结果表明，归因引导的多职责协同演化能够大幅提升冻结文本到图像系统，超越单维度适配方法的局限，为无需修改生成器参数的 T2I 系统优化提供了有效新路径。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image (T2I) systems can be improved without modifying generator parameters by adapting the external system around frozen generators. However, existing approaches typically optimize a predefined dimension, such as prompts, routing, or workflows, restricting the space in which generation failures can be corrected. Allowing multiple generator-external responsibilities to evolve provides a broader adaptation space, but introduces a new challenge: visual feedback reveals what failed, but not where persistent evolution should occur or how this space should be explored efficiently. We introduce EvoGen-Harness, a generator-agnostic framework for multi-responsibility image-generation harness evolution, together with Trace (Trajectory-Relative Attribution and Coordinated Evolution). Trace aggregates evidence across stochastic executions, uses failure attribution as a search prior to focus candidate updates, and progressively re-attributes residual failures to coordinate evolution across responsibilities, while No-Patch and held-out validation prevent unnecessary or harmful updates. Across GenEval2, T2I-CompBench++, and WISE, EvoGen-Harness improves over the strongest evaluated baselines by +0.2633, +0.0720, and +0.0752, respectively, while achieving 87.9-91.4% attribution recall, 94.8% No-Patch accuracy, and only 1.9% regression. These results demonstrate that attribution-guided multi-responsibility evolution can substantially enhance frozen T2I systems beyond single-dimension adaptation.

</details>


### [193] [Sharp Non-Asymptotic Analysis of the Penalized Challenger in $β$-EB-TCI for Bernoulli Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01951)
*Nam Nguyen,Tuan Quang Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于固定置信度最佳臂识别的Top-two算法（以β-EB-TCI为代表）的精确非渐近行为，证明了在经验领先者稳定为真实最佳臂且采样比例接近β后，停止时间为T_β^*(μ)log(1/δ)（加上低阶集中项），并据此得到高概率下界与尖锐的期望样本复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-two算法简单有效，但其精确的非渐近（finite-time）行为尚不清楚。作者希望刻画β-EB-TCI（Jourdan等人的经验最佳top-two规则，挑战者通过伯努利运输成本加对数计数惩罚选择）的停止时间与采样复杂度，并解决经验领先者何时永久正确的控制难题，进而获得对所有具有唯一最佳臂的伯努利实例的非渐近、尖锐的理论保证。

Method: 以Bernoulli bandit为模型，分析β-EB-TCI算法。核心证明思路：在经验领先者已成为真实最佳臂且其采样比例稳定接近β的状态下，证明停止时间为T_β^*(μ)log(1/δ)加上低阶集中项，并证明每个挑战者都被线性次采样。利用该结构控制经验领先者永久正确的时刻，得到非渐近高概率界。若算法满足有限均值充分探索条件，进一步推出尖锐的期望样本复杂度；所有臂均值两两不同时可借助Jourdan等人的充分探索结果得到无守卫Bernoulli规则的尖锐期望结果；另加入仅贡献O(√(Kt))次拉取的温和强制探索规则，得到对任意臂数的自包含期望样本复杂度定理。

Result: 1) 证明在经验领先者稳定后，停止时间为T_β^*(μ)log(1/δ)（含低阶集中项），且每个挑战者都被线性次采样。2) 对原始无强制探索算法，主要剩余困难是控制经验领先者何时永久正确。3) 得到对所有具有唯一最佳臂的伯努利实例的非渐近高概率界。4) 在有限均值充分探索条件下得到尖锐期望样本复杂度；对臂均值两两不同的情况给出无守卫Bernoulli规则的尖锐期望结果。5) 加入温和强制探索（至多O(√(Kt))次拉取）后，得到任意臂数下唯一最佳臂假设的自包含期望样本复杂度定理。6) 识别了试图通过单一指标比较论证处理相等次优臂均值的证明策略的局限性。

Conclusion: 本文为β-EB-TCI等Top-two算法建立了精确的非渐近停止时间与样本复杂度刻画，核心困难在于控制经验领先者永久正确的时刻，并辅以充分探索或温和强制探索条件，最终获得尖锐的期望样本复杂度结果，同时指出单一指标比较方法在处理相等次优均值时的局限。

Abstract: Top-two algorithms are simple and effective for fixed-confidence best-arm identification, but their sharp non-asymptotic behavior is still not well understood. We study this problem for Bernoulli bandits through $β$-EB-TCI, the empirical-best top-two rule of Jourdan et al., whose challenger is chosen using a Bernoulli transportation cost with a logarithmic count penalty. We prove that, after the empirical leader has become the true best arm and its sampling fraction stays close to $β$, the stopping time is $T_β^{\star}(μ)\log(1/δ)$ up to lower-order concentration terms. We also show that, in this regime, every challenger is sampled linearly often. Thus, for the original algorithm without forced exploration, the main remaining difficulty is to control when the empirical leader becomes permanently correct. These results imply a non-asymptotic high-probability bound for all Bernoulli instances with a unique best arm. If the algorithm satisfies a finite-mean sufficient-exploration condition, the bound further yields the sharp expected sample complexity. In particular, this gives the sharp expectation result for the unguarded Bernoulli rule when all arm means are pairwise distinct, using the sufficient-exploration result of Jourdan et al. Finally, if we add a mild forced-exploration rule that contributes only $O(\sqrt{Kt})$ pulls up to time $t$, we obtain a self-contained expected sample-complexity theorem for any number of arms under the unique-best-arm assumption. We also identify a limitation of proof strategies that try to handle equal suboptimal means through a single index-comparison argument.

</details>


### [194] [The Curvature of Regret in Contextual Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01980)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Alice E. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性优化的决策聚焦学习（DFL）中优化器的不连续性难题，证明该点态非光滑行为在数据分布上平均后会变成局部二次型，并给出曲率的闭式解（一个支撑在法锥墙面上的矩阵值测度），随后提出只需一次投影的曲率近似并证明其弱收敛，最终应用于决策感知的场景生成，在电池套利任务上取得30.8%的遗憾值改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策聚焦学习用于线性优化时，优化器的不连续性（微小成本误差可能使决策不变或跳到另一顶点）导致梯度信息难以获取，阻碍了可微学习方法的直接应用。作者旨在刻画并利用平均意义下的曲率结构，从而为DFL提供可处理的理论与算法工具。

Method: 作者先证明点态非光滑行为在数据分布上取平均后成为局部二次型，推导出其曲率的闭式形式——一个支撑在法锥（normal fan）墙面上的矩阵值测度，该测度仅依赖可行集，数据分布仅以权重形式进入。进而提出一种可处理的曲率近似，只需一次向可行集的投影即可计算，并证明该近似弱收敛于真实总体曲率。

Result: 实验验证了二次型法则与弱收敛法则的有效性；在电池套利的预期成本线性优化场景生成任务中，所提方法相较均匀分配取得30.8%的遗憾值改进。

Conclusion: 通过引入数据分布平均下的二次曲率结构及其可计算近似，本文缓解了线性优化DFL中优化器不连续带来的困难，为决策聚焦学习提供了新的理论和实用手段，并在实际能源优化任务中展现出显著性能提升。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning for linear optimization is complicated by the discontinuity of the optimizer, where small cost errors may leave the decision unchanged or move it to a different vertex. We show that this non-smooth pointwise behavior becomes locally quadratic after averaging over the data distribution, and we derive the curvature in closed form, specifically, a matrix-valued measure supported on the walls of the normal fan. This measure depends only on the feasible set, with the data distribution entering only as a weight. We then offer a tractable approximation for this curvature, computable with just one projection to the feasible set. We prove that the approximation weakly converges to the true population curvature. We offer one application of our findings, a decision-aware scenario generation method for expected-cost linear optimization. Our experiments test the quadratic and weak convergence laws and show a 30.8% regret improvement over uniform allocation on battery arbitrage.

</details>


### [195] [T2SPO: Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00388)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Maoqi Liu,Feiran Li,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2SPO 通过将过去成功的交互轨迹转化为逐步反馈，为 LLM Agent 的策略优化提供辅助信用分配；具体做法是用成功轨迹构建"状态—剩余距离"样本，训练一个 TabPFN 回归器来估计新 rollout 中每一步距成功的剩余距离，并用相邻步距离变化作为额外奖励，叠加在任务级奖励之上，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上稳定优于 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多交互式任务的奖励仅在最终结果中体现，几乎不指示哪些中间决策真正推动了任务进展，导致强化学习过程中步骤级信用分配困难、学习信号稀疏。而成功的训练轨迹本身蕴含了哪些中间状态有助于成功的监督信息，可以用来为后续交互补充更细粒度的反馈，从而弥补仅靠稀疏结局奖励这一不足。

Method: 提出 Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization（T2SPO）。首先从过去的成功轨迹中推导出"剩余距离目标"，并将其与沿途访问的状态表示配对，形成示例样本。以这些样本为条件，使用预训练的 TabPFN 回归器估计新 rollout 中每个状态"到成功的剩余距离"。然后，通过比较相邻状态间距离估计的变化，为 agent 的每一步生成辅助信用（auxiliary credit），并与任务级监督奖励共同用于策略优化。随着训练进行，新完成的轨迹会不断刷新回归器的上下文，从而在不更新其参数的情况下纳入新经验。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上，使用 1.5B 和 7B 语言模型的实验表明，T2SPO 相比于 GRPO 一致地提升了整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: T2SPO 证明了利用历史成功轨迹为步骤级信用分配提供监督是有效的：通过在参数冻结的 TabPFN 回归器上动态估计剩余距离并生成逐步辅助奖励，能稳定地改善 LLM Agent 在仅含稀疏结局奖励的交互式任务上的策略学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables large language model (LLM) agents to learn multi-step behaviors through interaction with their environments. However, rewards in many interactive tasks reflect only the final outcome, providing limited guidance on which intermediate decisions advance the task. Successful training trajectories contain intermediate states that can provide supervision for subsequent interactions. We introduce Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization (T2SPO), a method that uses past interaction trajectories to provide step-level feedback for policy learning. T2SPO derives remaining-distance targets from successful trajectories and pairs them with representations of the states visited along the way. Conditioned on these examples, a pretrained TabPFN regressor estimates the remaining distance to success at each state of a new rollout. Changes in this distance estimate across consecutive states yield auxiliary credit for agent steps alongside task-level supervision. As training proceeds, newly completed trajectories refresh the estimator's context, incorporating new experience without updating its parameters. Experiments with 1.5B and 7B language models on ALFWorld and WebShop show that T2SPO consistently improves overall task success over GRPO.

</details>


### [196] [Muon meets Tamed Langevin: Momentum Preconditioning beyond Convex and gradient-Lipschitz Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02158)
*Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对势能既非凸且梯度非全局Lipschitz的矩阵空间Gibbs分布采样问题，提出一类非二次动能，构造带动量预处理的欠阻尼Langevin系统。该系统中动能梯度对动量起到光滑的谱驯化（spectral taming）作用，保证目标测度不变性、加权全变差距离下的指数收敛，并使其离散化具有时间一致的矩界，从而确保采样算法稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样方法的势能通常要求凸性或全局梯度Lipschitz，而许多实际Gibbs分布（如矩阵空间上的分布）不满足这些条件，导致欠阻尼Langevin动力学不稳定或无法保证收敛。作者希望放松这些假设，设计在非凸、非Lipschitz势能下仍稳定且收敛的采样算法。

Method: 引入一族非二次动能，构造带动量预处理的欠阻尼Langevin系统；利用动能梯度实现对动量的光滑谱驯化（spectral taming），替代对势能梯度的修改；随后分析该动力学的目标测度不变性、加权全变差距离下的指数收敛性，并研究其Euler-Maruyama离散化的时间一致矩界。

Result: 证明在势能非凸且梯度非全局Lipschitz的松弛假设下：动力学保持目标Gibbs测度不变；在加权全变差距离下以指数速率收敛至平衡；对应的Euler-Maruyama离散化在无需修改势能梯度的情况下具有关于时间一致的矩界，从而保证采样算法数值稳定。

Conclusion: 通过动量预处理与非二次动能引入的光滑谱驯化，有效克服了势能非凸、非Lipschitz带来的困难，获得一个具有理论收敛保证和数值稳定性的矩阵空间Gibbs采样框架。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sampling from Gibbs distributions on matrix spaces whose potential energies are neither convex nor globally gradient-Lipschitz. We introduce a family of non-quadratic kinetic energies that lead to a new underdamped Langevin system with momentum preconditioning, in which the gradient of the kinetic energy acts as a smooth spectral taming of the momentum. We prove that, under these relaxed assumptions on the potential, the resulting dynamics leaves the target Gibbs measure invariant, and we establish exponential convergence to equilibrium in a weighted total variation distance. Finally, we show that the corresponding Euler-Maruyama discretization admits moment bounds that are uniform in time, without any modification of the potential gradient, which ensures the stability of the resulting sampling algorithm.

</details>


### [197] [FERPO: Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02198)
*Sebastian Sanokowski,Alireza Sarmadi,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FERPO，一种无需对 critic 关于动作求导的 on-policy 最大熵强化学习算法，通过熵与 KL 正则构造目标动作分布，并用 forward-KL 目标拟合 actor，以自归一化重要性采样估计，在 MuJoCo Playground 和 ManiSkill 上取得竞争性性能和采样效率提升，actor 更新快于 REPPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 critic 动作梯度的连续控制在线强化学习方法中，critic 通常只被训练来预测回报，准确的回报预测并不保证准确的动作导数，可能导致策略更新不可靠。因此希望在不依赖 critic 对动作求导的情况下完成策略改进。

Method: 提出 FERPO（Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization），一种 on-policy 最大熵强化学习算法。它仅用 critic 的值、不对 critic 关于动作求导：先由熵与 KL 散度正则化的策略改进目标推导出最优目标动作分布；再通过最小化 forward-KL 目标（用 rollout 策略采样的动作进行自归一化重要性采样 SNIS 估计）将 actor 拟合到该目标分布。KL 正则限制目标分布偏离 rollout 策略，从而保证重要性权重表现良好；forward-KL 目标鼓励覆盖多个高价值模态以促进探索，区别于可能偏向单一模态的 reverse-KL。

Result: 在 MuJoCo Playground 和 ManiSkill 上的实验与消融表明 FERPO 具有竞争性性能和采样效率增益；计算基准测试显示其 actor 更新比 Relative Entropy Pathwise Policy Optimization（REPPO）更快。

Conclusion: FERPO 通过摆脱对 critic 动作梯度的依赖，结合熵正则与 forward-KL 拟合，可提供可靠且高效的策略更新，在连续控制基准上取得竞争性采样效率与更快的 actor 更新，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Several state-of-the-art methods for online reinforcement learning in continuous control improve policies using action gradients of a learned critic. However, critics are typically trained to predict returns, and accurate value predictions do not necessarily yield accurate action derivatives, potentially leading to unreliable policy updates. We propose Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization (FERPO), an on-policy maximum entropy reinforcement learning algorithm that performs policy improvement using critic values without differentiating the critic with respect to actions. FERPO derives an optimal target action distribution from a policy-improvement objective regularized by entropy and Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. We then fit the actor to this target by minimizing a forward-KL objective, estimated using self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS) with actions drawn from the rollout policy. By limiting the target distribution's deviation from the rollout policy, the KL regularization helps keep these importance weights well behaved. In contrast to reverse-KL objectives, which can favor a subset of the target distribution's modes, the forward-KL objective encourages coverage of multiple high-value modes and thereby promotes exploration. Experiments and ablations on MuJoCo Playground and ManiSkill show competitive performance and sample-efficiency gains. Computational benchmarks also demonstrate faster actor updates than Relative Entropy Pathwise Policy Optimization (REPPO).

</details>


### [198] [Interpretable Synthetic Medical Tabular Data Generation for Clinical Decision Support Using Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00391)
*Michael Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模糊认知图（FCM）的可解释、因果驱动且注重隐私的合成医疗表格数据生成框架，在仅用CPU的条件下，于UCI医疗基准数据集上达到与TVAE和高斯Copula相媲美甚至更优的分类性能与保真度，并具备更强的隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床数据因隐私、伦理或数据可用性限制难以获取，而现有概率模型与深度生成模型往往缺乏可解释性、无法保留临床上有意义的依赖关系，难以胜任安全关键型应用，因此需要一种透明、因果明确且可信的合成数据生成方法。

Method: 将临床特征用语言可解释的模糊集合描述，通过模糊集合交集计算FCM边权重以编码特征间依赖；合成患者记录通过随机初始化的语言激活向量在FCM中迭代传播直至收敛，再经去模糊化得到临床一致的数值。该方法原生支持混合数据类型及健康记录中常见的领域约束。

Result: 在UCI医疗基准数据集上的TSTR评估显示：Heart Disease数据集上准确率最高达0.81、AUROC最高达0.90，与TVAE和高斯Copula持平或更优，且仅用CPU；KS Complement最高0.91、Correlation Similarity最高0.95，统计一致性良好；DCR Baseline Protection得分持续高于TVAE，隐私保护充分。

Conclusion: 基于因果、可解释的模糊建模为CBMS中可信赖的合成数据生成提供了一种计算高效且透明的深度生成模型替代方案。

Abstract: Synthetic medical tabular data generation has become essential for developing and validating computer-based medical systems (CBMSs) when real clinical data is restricted due to privacy, ethical, or data availability limitations. Existing probabilistic and deep generative models often lack interpretability and fail to preserve clinically meaningful dependencies, limiting their suitability for safety-critical applications. This paper proposes a novel application of Fuzzy Cognitive Maps (FCMs) in a framework for synthetic medical tabular data generation with explicit causality and privacy preservation. Clinical features are described using linguistically interpretable fuzzy sets, and inter-feature dependencies are encoded as FCM edge weights computed from fuzzy set intersections. Synthetic patient records are generated by propagating randomly initialized linguistic activation vectors through the FCM until convergence, followed by defuzzification to produce clinically coherent numerical values. The approach natively handles mixed data types, and domain constraints common in health records. Experimental evaluation on UCI medical benchmark datasets demonstrates competitive performance under a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) protocol. The proposed method achieves accuracy of up to 0.81 and AUROC of up to 0.90 on the Heart Disease dataset, matching or exceeding TVAE and Gaussian Copula baselines while running exclusively on CPU. Fidelity metrics including KS Complement (up to 0.91) and Correlation Similarity (up to 0.95) confirm strong statistical coherence, and DCR Baseline Protection scores consistently exceed those of TVAE, confirming adequate privacy guarantees. These results demonstrate that causally grounded, interpretable fuzzy modeling offers a computationally efficient and transparent alternative to deep generative models for trustworthy synthetic data generation in CBMSs.

</details>


### [199] [Forking: Sudden Overfitting Under Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00394)
*Shanbin Yu,Shaoyang Guo,Haoran Zhao,Danni Yu,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 NanoGPT 自动研究中发现的"分叉"(forking)现象：在数据重放且模型含过度编码的 n-gram 记忆分支时，训练损失与验证损失在 epoch 边界出现尖锐分离，形状如叉子。该现象在受控 vanilla NanoGPT 与 DeepSeek 风格 + Engram 模型中均可复现，源于重复更新锐化训练中见过的续接、压制未见续接，n-gram 模块的弱交互子空间放大该效应，低频上下文贡献最大，大训练预算与拥挤表则抑制它。本文贡献有二：揭示了继 grokking、double descent 之后又一深度学习奇观；警示自动研究智能体所产技巧可能带来意外且有害的副产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 NanoGPT 自动研究中意外发现一种此前未被关注的新现象——分叉，希望理解其成因、复现条件，并以此提醒研究者对自动研究智能体产出的大量"看似有用"的结果保持警惕。
