Method: 在受控的 vanilla NanoGPT 设置下系统研究分叉现象，并在 DeepSeek 风格模型搭配 Engram 模块中复现；通过机制分析考察重复更新如何锐化训练续接、压制未见续接，以及 n-gram 模块产生的弱相互作用、上下文特定子空间对效应的放大作用；同时检验低频上下文、训练预算与表拥挤度的影响，并在短预算、高重复的 SFT 与类 RL 设定下观察分叉。

Result: 分叉现象可稳定复现；机制上，重复更新使训练中见过的续接概率被锐化，未见续接被压抑且损失随遍历次数增长；n-gram 模块的弱交互上下文特定子空间放大该效应；低频上下文对训练/验证差距贡献最大；更大的训练预算与高度拥挤的表可抑制分叉；分叉同样出现在短预算、高重复的 SFT 与类强化学习设定中。

Conclusion: 分叉是继 grokking 与 double descent 之后深度学习中的又一奇特现象，同时是自动研究智能体所提技巧带来的意外且不愉快的副产品，提示研究者对自动生成结果保持审慎。

Abstract: This paper studies forking, a generalization failure discovered in NanoGPT autoresearch. Under data replay, models with an over-encoding n-gram memory branch show a sharp separation of training and validation loss at epoch boundaries, resembling the shape of forks. We study this phenomenon in a controlled vanilla NanoGPT setting and reproduce it in a DeepSeek-style model with Engram. Mechanistically, repeated updates sharpen the continuations observed in training while suppressing the probability of unseen continuations, whose loss grows with each pass. The n-gram module creates weakly interacting context-specific subspaces, amplifying this effect. Low-frequency contexts contribute most of the gap, whereas larger training budgets and heavily crowded tables suppress it. We also observe forking in short-budget, heavily repeated SFT and RL-like regimes. The contributions of this paper are twofold: (1) Forking reveals yet another curious phenomenon in deep learning, in addition to grokking and double descent. (2) Forking is an unexpected and unpleasant by-product of tricks proposed by autoresearch agents. While these agents produce an enormous number of results that seem useful, we should always be careful with their results.

</details>


### [200] [Specificity-Aware Diffusion Steering via Variance-Reduced Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00395)
*Luran Wang,Linrui Ma,Hannes Stärk,Regina Barzilay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种特异性感知的扩散模型推理期引导方法，将问题建模为目标分布设计问题，通过基于重叠的目标构造“正分布占优”区域的目标路径，并配以方差最小化局部提议的序列蒙特卡洛采样器与固定噪声优化，从而在抑制负样本区域的同时最小化对正分布的扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理期引导可在不重新训练的情况下让预训练扩散模型满足新约束，但特异性感知生成困难：排斥负参考分布时，在正负分布重叠区域也会侵蚀正分布，因此核心挑战是抑制负质量的同时最小限度扭曲正分布。

Method: 将特异性感知引导建模为目标设计问题，从基于重叠的目标函数导出目标分布；目标分布仅在“正参考分布相对负参考分布足够占优”的区域保留正分布，从而赋予特异性以似然比解释。为从对应的时间相关目标路径采样，提出方差最小化局部提议的序列蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样器，并进一步引入结合正负分数场雅可比-向量积的固定噪声优化流程。

Result: 在合成任务、类对比生成、文本到图像任务及肽-MHC（p-MHC）结合体任务上的实验表明，相较于负引导基线，该方法更有效地抑制不需要的区域、减少模式偏移，并通过降低 SMC 权重坍缩来提升采样稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过基于重叠的目标分布设计与方差最小化的 SMC 采样，实现了在不牺牲正分布质量的前提下有效抑制负区域，显著改善了特异性感知生成中的模式偏移与采样稳定性，并提供了代码开源实现。

Abstract: Inference-time steering enables pretrained diffusion models to satisfy new constraints without full retraining. However, specificity-aware generation is difficult: repelling samples from a negative reference distribution can also erode the positive distribution where the two overlap. The key challenge is to suppress negative mass while minimally distorting the positive distribution. We address this problem by formulating specificity-aware steering as a target-design problem and deriving a target distribution from an overlap-based objective. The resulting target keeps the desired reference distribution only in regions where it is sufficiently preferred over the undesired reference distribution, giving a likelihood-ratio interpretation of specificity. To sample from the corresponding time-dependent target path, we develop a Sequential Monte Carlo sampler with a variance-minimized local proposal. We further introduce a practical fixed-noise optimization procedure with the Jacobian--vector products with the desired and undesired score fields. Experiments on synthetic task, class-contrastive generation, text-to-image tasks and peptide-MHC (p-MHC) binder show that the proposed method suppresses undesired regions more effectively, reduces mode shift, and improves sampling stability by decreasing the SMC weight collapse compared with negative-guidance baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/WangLuran/Specificity-Aware-Diffusion-Steering

</details>


### [201] [NEUROTOKEN: Joint Source and Directional AAD with Envelope Decoding via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00397)
*Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对鸡尾酒会问题中的听觉注意力解码（AAD），提出用"给定EEG的注意包络条件似然"取代传统单一统计量，并实现为三头共享EEG前端的网络NEUROTOKEN，其中条件流匹配头ATTUNEFLOW通过积分速度-残差似然比对候选声源打分；配合QUADTRACK与ENV-FLOW两种推理集成，在5秒窗口上显著提升源级AAD准确率并降低跨被试方差，同时以严格试次分离协议揭示传统方向AAD的高准确率被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AAD文献割裂：方向性AAD只分类方向但不映射到具体声源流；回归式源级AAD依赖单一Pearson相关，在真实设备所需的1-5秒窗口内本质上噪声大；包络重建缺乏原生的AAD判据。作者认为正确对象应是被注意包络在给定EEG下的条件似然，而非任意单一统计量。

Method: 提出NEUROTOKEN：单一网络、三个头共享一个EEG前端，其中条件流匹配头ATTUNEFLOW通过积分速度-残差似然比对候选打分；并以两种推理时集成——QUADTRACK（四种互补统计量）与ENV-FLOW（z标准化后的QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW）——吸收各统计量的失效模式。

Result: 在KU Leuven、DTU、NJU三数据集5秒窗口上，ATTUNEFLOW将逐段源级AAD较最强非生成基线提升9%-16%，并将跨被试方差缩小约3倍；试次级融合在其中两个数据集超过93%。并行复现表明，经典95-97%的方向AAD在严格试次分离协议下下降17%-45%。

Conclusion: 以条件似然为核心、辅以多统计量集成的NEUROTOKEN框架在短窗口源级AAD上显著优于现有基线，并揭示了方向AAD被高估的场景，说明基于似然的表述是解决鸡尾酒会问题、推动下一代助听器与脑机接口落地的关键方向。

Abstract: Identifying which speaker a listener is attending to in a noisy room -- the cocktail-party problem -- is the missing ingredient for next-generation hearing aids and brain-computer interfaces: it tells the device whose voice to amplify. Auditory attention decoding (AAD) reads this answer from EEG, but the literature splits into disconnected pieces: directional-AAD classifies side but does not map side to stream; regression-based source-AAD ranks candidate streams by a single Pearson correlation that is intrinsically noisy at the 1-5 s windows real devices need; and envelope reconstruction has no native AAD rule. We argue the right object is not any single statistic but the conditional likelihood of the attended envelope given EEG, and we make this practical with NEUROTOKEN: a single network whose three heads share one EEG front-end, with a conditional flow-matching head (ATTUNEFLOW) that scores candidates by an integrated velocity-residual likelihood ratio. Two inference-time ensembles -- QUADTRACK (four complementary statistics) and ENV-FLOW (z-normalised QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW) -- absorb per-statistic failure modes for free. On KU Leuven, DTU, and NJU at 5 s, ATTUNEFLOW lifts per-segment source-AAD by 9%-16% over the strongest non-generative baseline and shrinks across-subject variance by ~3x; trial-level fusion exceeds 93% on two of three datasets. In parallel reproductions we show that canonical 95-97% direction-AAD numbers collapse by 17%-45% under a strict trial-disjoint protocol, clarifying both the true ceiling and why a likelihood-based formulation is needed.

</details>


### [202] [WIPSNet: Deep Learning for Paediatric Wheeze Detection from Overnight Impedance Pneumography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00398)
*Felix Oury,Harley Day,Karina Mayoral,Ville-Pekka Seppä,Sejal Saglani,Reiko J. Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 WIPSNet——一种基于堆叠连续小波变换（CWT）尺度图的 3D ResNet 模型，用于从整夜阻抗呼吸描记（IP）信号中分类夜间喘息，AUC 达 0.783，显著优于现有临床指标 EVI 及多种基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整夜阻抗呼吸描记（IP）用于监测儿科呼吸健康，但其现行临床读出指标 Expiratory Variability Index（EVI）将整段记录压缩为单一标量，信息损失严重，夜间喘息分类 AUC 仅为 0.633。因此需要一种能充分利用整夜长时间序列中丰富时频信息的建模方法，以提升喘息检测性能。

Method: 提出 WIPSNet：先将整夜 IP 信号的连续小波变换尺度图沿体积维度堆叠，形成三维时频表示，再用 3D ResNet 架构学习时空特征并进行夜间喘息分类。在包含 15 名患者、60 个夜晚、281 小时的数据集上进行评估，并与 EVI、状态空间模型（Mamba）及两种现代睡眠分期架构进行对比。

Result: WIPSNet 取得 AUC 0.783 ± 0.026，优于 EVI（0.633）、Mamba 及两种睡眠分期架构。性能在对应 32 分钟时间上下文的体积深度处达到峰值，表明多尺度时间聚合对建模夜间呼吸动态至关重要。

Conclusion: 结构化时频表示与 3D 卷积架构相结合，为从长时、不规则生理时间序列中学习提供了有效途径，有望改善儿科夜间呼吸健康的自动监测。

Abstract: Overnight impedance pneumography (IP) is used to monitor paediatric respiratory health. Its current clinical readout, the Expiratory Variability Index (EVI), compresses each IP recording into a single scalar and achieves an AUC of 0.633 for night-level wheeze classification. We introduce Wheeze Impedance Pneumography Scalogram Network (WIPSNet), a 3D ResNet operating on stacked continuous wavelet transform scalograms of overnight IP signals. On a 15-patient cohort (60 nights, 281 hours), WIPSNet achieves an AUC of $0.783 \pm 0.026$, outperforming EVI, a state-space model (Mamba), and two modern sleep-staging architectures. Performance peaks at a volumetric depth corresponding to 32 minutes of temporal context, suggesting that multi-scale temporal aggregation is important for modelling nocturnal respiratory dynamics. Overall, these results indicate that structured time-frequency representations combined with 3D convolutional architectures provide an effective approach for learning from long, irregular physiological time series.

</details>


### [203] [Metacognitive Reasoning in Energy Based Models using Instance Based Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00399)
*Tailia Malloy,Prateek Kumar Rajput,Serge Lionel Nikiema,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MERITED 框架，将基于实例的学习理论（IBLT）与基于能量的模型（EBM）结合，使 AI 系统能依据自身不确定性动态分配计算资源，实现元认知控制下的推理努力调节，并开源了一个 191M 参数的推理 EBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的 AI 系统无法在生成输出前估计自身的不确定性，也无法动态分配计算资源，导致元认知过程难以实现。经典 transformer 架构的替代方案 EBM 虽然支持可解释的不确定性建模与动态计算资源分配，但尚未直接解决"根据不确定性决定计算分配"这一元认知任务。IBLT 则提供了从经验中建模类人决策的方法，此前已被用于预测人类元认知推理。

Method: 提出 MERITED（MEtacognitive Reasoning with Instance-based Learning Theory and Energy Dynamics）框架：以 IBLT 为理论基础，控制 EBM 中分配的计算工作量，从而在保持计算高效的同时实现基于不确定性的元认知推理努力控制。具体包括训练并开放权重一个 191M 参数的推理 EBM，以及实现基于 IBL 模型的动态计算分配。

Result: 该工作取得两项主要成果：一是训练并开放共享了 191M 参数的推理 EBM；二是实现了 MERITED 框架下利用 IBL 模型进行动态计算分配。

Conclusion: MERITED 框架在 IBLT 的基础上，能够控制 EBM 的计算工作量，实现基于不确定性的元认知推理努力控制，同时保持计算高效，为 AI 系统具备类人元认知资源分配能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metacognition involves reasoning about cognitive processes themselves. An example is in resource allocation where we choose how much time and effort to put into a reasoning task before we begin based on our confidence. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems that rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot estimate their uncertainty about an output without first responding, and cannot dynamically allocate resources to producing an output, making this type of metacognitive process difficult. A recently proposed alternative to classic transformer architectures that addresses these two concerns is the Energy Based Model (EBM) which allows for interpretable uncertainty modeling and dynamic allocation of compute resources. While EBMs can allow for control of these two processes, the actual metacognitive task of determining compute allocation based on uncertainty is not directly addressed. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides an approach to modeling human-like decisions from experience that has previously been applied to predicting human metacognitive reasoning. In this paper we introduce a framework for MEtacognitive Reasoning with Instance-based Learning Theory and Energy Dynamics (MERITED). Grounded in IBLT, this framework allows for control of the computational effort allocated in an EBM to allow for metacognitive control over reasoning effort based on uncertainty while remaining computationally efficient. This work has two main contributions, the training and open weight sharing of a 191M parameter reasoning EBM, and an implementation of the MERITED framework for dynamic compute allocation using an IBL model.

</details>


### [204] [Representation Transitions Reveal Emerging Safety Risks in Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00400)
*Haoyu Wang,Wei Zhao,Yedi Zhang,Christopher M. Poskitt,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DART，一种运行时防御框架，通过监测智能体内部表征的累积迁移来检测多轮攻击，并利用去噪后的对比安全方向与定向提醒进行干预，在多模型、多基准上显著降低攻击成功率，且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮攻击可将单个可允许的动作组合成有害结果，使孤立评估动作或状态的防御失效。作者发现此类攻击会在智能体内部表征中留下可检测信号：有害行为表现为跨上下文更新的累积表征迁移，且可在同一信号中定位触发上下文。但朴素聚合会受到良性表征漂移的干扰，因为对比安全方向不必对良性迁移赋零值。

Method: 提出 DART 运行时框架：(1) 识别并监测表征迁移及其触发上下文；(2) 通过去噪对比安全方向，将良性流量锚定为零并移除其主变异方向，以消除良性表征漂移的混淆（无运行时额外成本）；(3) 在检测到有害表征迁移时，以定向提醒方式进行干预。

Result: 在六个模型和两个多轮基准上，DART 将 MT-AgentRisk 上的攻击成功率从 84% 降至 25%，平均误报率 12% 下捕获所有攻击；将 ASEval 上从 97% 降至 52%，良性非拒绝成本分别为 8% 和 0%。在 MT-AgentRisk 上，DART 在全部六个模型上均优于最先进的多轮防御 ToolShield（后者同样协议下仅达 55%）。去噪至关重要：ASEval 上未去噪监测器仅捕获 7%–40% 攻击，而去噪后捕获 60%–85%。同一监测器无需修改即可覆盖单轮间接注入，每个受监测步骤仅增加 0.14–0.56 秒开销且无需辅助模型。

Conclusion: DART 利用表征迁移信号实现高效的多轮攻击检测与归因，去噪机制有效缓解良性漂移干扰，在保持极低运行时开销和不依赖辅助模型的情况下，成为计算密集型推测性防御的轻量补充，并在多基准与多模型上取得显著优于现有方法的防御效果。

Abstract: Multi-turn attacks on agentic systems can compose individually permissible actions into harmful outcomes, challenging defenses that assess actions or states in isolation. We show that such attacks leave a detectable signature in the agent's internal representations: harmful behavior emerges as an accumulated representation transition across context updates, whose triggering context can be identified from the same signal. We further find that naive aggregation is confounded by benign representation drift, as a contrastive safety direction need not assign zero to benign transitions. We address this by denoising the direction, anchoring benign traffic at zero and removing its leading variation directions, with no runtime cost.
  These findings motivate DART, a runtime framework that detects and attributes representation shifts and intervenes with targeted reminders. Across six models and two multi-turn benchmarks, DART reduces attack success from 84% to 25% on MT-AgentRisk, catching every attack at a mean false-alarm rate of 12%, and from 97% to 52% on ASEval, at costs in benign non-refusal of 8% and 0%, respectively. On MT-AgentRisk, it outperforms ToolShield, the state-of-the-art multi-turn defense, on all six models: under the same protocol, ToolShield reaches only 55%. Denoising is critical: on ASEval, the undenoised monitor catches only 7%-40% of attacks, while the denoised monitor catches 60%-85%. The same monitor covers single-turn indirect injection without modification and adds only 0.14-0.56 s overhead per monitored step without requiring an auxiliary model, making it a lightweight complement to computation-heavy speculative defenses.

</details>


### [205] [The Conflict Between Logic and Memory: Learning Higher-Order Interactions in Shallow MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00403)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐层MLP在合成任务中"拟合训练样本"与"恢复生成标签的规则"之间的差异，通过控制交互阶数和干扰输入，发现优化器（SGD、Adam、Muon）、干扰权重学习和偏置干预显著影响网络能否学到高阶规则，揭示了浅层网络实现高阶规则的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络能够在训练集上拟合良好却未必学到真实的标签生成规则。作者希望系统性地量化这种"拟合"与"规则恢复"之间的分离，并考察优化器选择、干扰（nuisance）输入、以及偏置等因素如何影响浅层网络对高阶交互规则的恢复能力。

Method: 使用单隐层MLP，构造可控制交互阶数、并含独立干扰输入的合成任务。首先建立基准性质：纯奇偶校验不含低阶预测边际、存在精确贝叶斯后验、并可由宽度为k的ReLU网络在干净隐变量上表示。随后设计两组实验：(1) 匹配阶2到4的扫描实验，比较SGD、Adam、Muon的峰值测试精度；(2) 混合阶任务，对比冻结连接到干扰输入的第一层权重、仅测试时移除干扰输入的干预效果。此外还通过偏置干预考察目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示的联系，并在信号压缩（signal-only）情境下测试SGD与Muon对5至11阶规则的泛化能力。

Result: 在阶2到4的匹配扫描中，三种优化器均在阶2达到100%峰值测试精度；阶3时SGD、Adam、Muon分别达到96.25%、50.87%、76.82%，Muon在阶4达到99.21%。在混合阶任务中，仅冻结连接到独立干扰输入的第一层权重，便将AdamW的第10轮精度从44.73%提升至95.07%；而仅在测试时移除相同输入仅提升到48.38%，说明干扰权重学习对训练结果的影响远超其对预测的即时作用。在信号压缩（signal-only）情境下，SGD和Muon都能学到5到8阶规则，且SGD在9到11阶的峰值精度更高。

Conclusion: 优化器的选择和干扰（nuisance）权重的学习过程共同约束了浅层网络所能实现的高阶规则：不同优化器在相同阶数上表现差异显著，干扰输入的学习会改变训练结果而不仅仅是影响预测，偏置干预揭示了目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示之间的关联。总体表明，优化动态与干扰学习是决定网络能否从数据中恢复真实高阶规则的关键因素。

Abstract: A network can fit its training examples while failing to recover the rule that generated their labels. We examine this separation in single-hidden-layer multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), using synthetic tasks that control interaction order and the presence of nuisance inputs. We establish elementary benchmark properties: pure parity contains no predictive lower-order marginals, admits an exact Bayes posterior, and can be represented on clean latent inputs by a width-$k$ ReLU network. Experiments then identify distinct optimization outcomes. In a matched order-2--4 sweep, SGD, Adam, and Muon all reach 100\% peak test accuracy at order two; at order three they reach 96.25\%, 50.87\%, and 76.82\%, respectively, while Muon reaches 99.21\% at order four. In a separate mixed-order task, freezing only the first-layer weights connected to independent nuisance inputs raises AdamW's epoch-10 accuracy from 44.73\% to 95.07\%. Removing the same inputs only at test time raises it to 48.38\%. Thus, nuisance-weight learning changes the training outcome beyond its immediate effect on prediction. Bias interventions expose a connection between target symmetry and shallow ReLU representations. In a compact signal-only regime, both SGD and Muon learn orders five through eight, with higher SGD peak accuracy at orders nine through eleven. Together, the results show how optimization and nuisance learning constrain the higher-order rules realized by a shallow network.

</details>


### [206] [EchoPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Pruning via Virtual Context Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00412)
*Jiawei Lin,Saibo Geng,Thomas Bourgeat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoPress 是一种无需训练的方法，通过复用标准预填充中已计算的信息来近似 KVzip 的重构注意力分数，从而在保持任务准确率的同时大幅降低 KV 缓存剪枝的压缩开销与预填充时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KVzip 通过逐块提示模型重构上下文来估计 KV 对的重要性，压缩质量高，但需要额外的前向传播；已有的学习式近似虽能降低成本，却依赖模型专属训练。作者希望找到一种既具备 KVzip 级压缩质量、又无需额外训练且开销低的替代方案。

Method: 作者分析 KVzip 如何识别重要的缓存信息，发现可用预填充阶段已算好的查询（query）与键（key）来近似其重构分数。基于此提出 EchoPress：对每个请求仅重构第一个分块，用其校准其余上下文的的重要性分数，从而以训练无关的方式逼近重构注意力。

Result: 在 LongBench 与 RULER 上，使用 Qwen3-8B 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，在 50% 到 90% 的驱逐比例下，EchoPress 的任务准确率与 KVzip 相当；同时压缩开销降低 1.7–19.6 倍，总预填充时间最多降低 2.9 倍。

Conclusion: EchoPress 证明可以利用标准预填充中已存在的信息高效近似重建分数，以训练无关的方式达到与 KVzip 相近的压缩质量，同时显著降低计算开销，为长上下文推理的 KV 缓存剪枝提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: KV cache pruning reduces long-context inference memory usage by evicting less important key-value pairs. KVzip estimates importance through context reconstruction: prompting a model to repeat the context chunk by chunk. This achieves strong compression quality at the cost of additional forward passes. Learned approximations reduce this cost but require model-specific training. We analyze how KVzip identifies important cached information and show how to approximate its reconstruction scores using information already computed during prefill. These findings motivate EchoPress, a training-free method that approximates reconstruction attention using queries and keys from standard prefill. For each request, it reconstructs only the first chunk to calibrate importance scores for the remaining context. Experiments on LongBench and RULER with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct show that EchoPress matches KVzip in task accuracy across eviction ratios from 50% to 90%, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 1.7-19.6 and total prefill time by a factor of up to 2.9. Code is available at https://github.com/ljwljwljwljw/kvpress/tree/echo-press.

</details>


### [207] [Do Better Scores Mean Better Physics? Physics-Grounded Explanations for Sim2Real Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00415)
*Somyajit Chakraborty,Xizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文考察机器学习代理模型在NACA4418翼型绕流中的预测误差与物理相关流动统计量（尤其是脉动能量）之间的一致性，发现较低的流场误差未必对应较低的双分量脉动能误差，建议除聚合预测分数外报告互补的物理诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型可加速物理模拟，但"预测误差更低"并不必然意味着"物理相关流动统计量的误差更低"。作者旨在检验这一脱节现象，并区分"敏感性"与"独立的物理重要性证据"。

Method: 结合NACA4418翼型绕流的CFD成对模拟与实验PIV测量数据；采用保均值输入干预法，从观测流场历史的选定区域移除速度脉动；对比四种神经算子，并设置等面积随机移除作为对照；另通过输出衰减压力测试检验基准误差与全域求和脉动能之间的一致性。

Result: 从最富能量的10%有效观测单元中移除脉动，对预报的改变大于等面积随机移除，但由于掩膜未按移除的脉动能量匹配，该对比只反映敏感性而非物理重要性的独立证据；单独而言，CNO在速度场误差上更低，但其双分量脉动能误差在两个分析子集上均显著高于参考模型；输出衰减压力测试同样显示基准误差与全域求和脉动能之间存在分歧。

Conclusion: 这些单一基准的结果表明，除聚合预测分数外应报告互补的物理诊断量；研究结果并不能确立反事实意义上的物理正确性。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates accelerate physical simulation, but lower prediction error need not coincide with lower error in physically relevant flow statistics. We examine this question for flow around a NACA4418 airfoil using paired computational-fluid-dynamics simulations and experimental particle-image-velocimetry measurements. A mean-preserving input intervention removes velocity fluctuations from selected regions of observed flow histories. Across four neural operators, removing fluctuations from the most energetic 10% of valid observed cells changes forecasts more than equal-area random removal. Because the masks are not matched for removed fluctuation energy, this contrast measures sensitivity, not independent evidence of physical importance. Separately, a CNO has lower velocity-field error but substantially higher two-component fluctuation-energy error than the reference on both analysis subsets. An output attenuation stress test also demonstrates disagreement between benchmark errors and domain-summed fluctuation energy. These single-benchmark results motivate reporting complementary physical diagnostics alongside aggregate prediction scores; they do not establish counterfactual physical correctness.

</details>


### [208] [Source Identification Is Not Fitness Testing: Measuring the Limits of Synthetic-Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00417)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型生成数据反复训练导致的退化问题，检验来源溯源是否可靠以及是否能帮助筛选更好的训练数据。结果发现：原始生成文本的生成器归属准确率达98.7%，但经过改写后大幅下降；生成文本与人类文本的区分仍近乎完美。两种筛选规则（基于来源信息 vs. 基于参考模型评分）选出的样本不同，但未检测到它们对模型退化的稳定差异，说明"数据来自哪里"与"数据是否适合训练"是两个独立的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反复用模型生成数据训练会导致后续模型退化。一种应对思路是利用数据的来源（provenance）来决定复用哪些生成样本。作者希望检验：来源信息能否被可靠恢复，以及来源信息是否真正有助于筛选出更好的训练数据。

Method: 采用金融风险文本作为数据集。首先对生成段落进行生成器归属识别，并在改写（改述与风格重写）后重复测试；同时评估生成文本与人类文本的检测性能。随后进行三轮"生成-再训练"循环，对比两种生成样本筛选规则：一种基于来源信息，另一种基于独立参考模型给出的评分。通过计划比较（planned comparison）检验两种规则对模型退化程度的影响是否有稳定差异。

Result: 生成器归属在原始段落上准确率为98.7%，改述后降至53.1%，风格重写后进一步降至29.0%；而生成文本与人类文本的检测在对测试的人类对照集上仍接近完美。两种筛选规则选出了不同的样本，但计划比较未能检测到两者在模型退化程度上的稳定差异。

Conclusion: 实验表明"识别数据来源"与"识别哪些数据对训练有用"是两个独立的问题。该实验将来源身份、面向准则（criterion-facing）的筛选与递归训练结果三者分离：无论是来源评分还是所测试的面向准则的代理指标，均未被证明确立为对未来递归行为具有充分性。

Abstract: Repeated training on model-generated data can degrade later models. One possible response is to use provenance when deciding which generated examples to reuse. We test both how reliably that provenance can be recovered and whether it helps identify better training data. Using financial-risk text, we first identify the source of generated passages and then repeat the test after rewriting them. Generator attribution is 98.7% accurate on the original passages but falls to 53.1% after paraphrasing and 29.0% after style rewriting. Generated-versus-human detection remains close to perfect against the tested human comparison set. We then compare two ways of selecting generated examples over three rounds of generation and retraining. One uses source information. The other uses a score from a separate reference model. The two rules select different examples, but the planned comparison does not detect a stable difference in the degradation of the resulting models. The results show that identifying where data came from and identifying which data are useful for training are separate problems. The experiment therefore separates source identity, criterion-facing selection, and recursive training outcome: neither the provenance score nor the tested criterion-facing proxy is established as sufficient for future recursive behaviour.

</details>


### [209] [CommunityKV: Efficient Long-Context Decoding via Graph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00418)
*Joe McKenna,Anastasios Alexandridis,Nathan Susanj,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommunityKV 将稀疏注意力建模为社区检测问题，利用预填充阶段已有的 QK^T 分数构建 token 图并划分社区，以恒定时间将新 token 归入社区，从而在无需训练和大规模重划分的情况下高效地为长上下文解码实现稀疏检索，最高带来 1.71 倍端到端生成吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 扩展到长上下文受限于自注意力的二次计算成本以及键值缓存线性增长带来的内存传输压力。稀疏注意力虽能缓解该问题，但现有方法要么需要大规模训练，要么在免训练设定下依赖语义粗糙的启发式规则或难以在解码中高效更新的昂贵聚类，亟需一种无需训练、语义连贯且可高效更新解码的稀疏检索方案。

Method: 提出 CommunityKV 框架：在标准预填充阶段，利用已计算出的 QK^T 分数构造 token 图，将该图划分为社区（community），以此检索语义连贯的 token 组；同时设计局部更新规则，使新生成 token 能在恒定时间内被分配到相应社区，从而在流式解码过程中实现稀疏检索而无需全局重新划分。

Result: 在 Qwen3 与 Llama-3.1 模型上，于三个长上下文基准上进行评测。采用每个查询头一个图（one graph per query head）的配置，CommunityKV 的端到端生成吞吐达到稠密注意力的 1.25 倍；采用查询组图聚合（query-group graph aggregation）时可达 1.71 倍，且准确率保持可比。

Conclusion: CommunityKV 表明，将稀疏注意力形式化为社区检测问题，能够在不依赖额外训练的前提下，利用已有的注意力分数构建语义连贯的 token 社区，并通过恒定时间的局部更新实现流式解码下的高效稀疏检索，在保持准确率的同时显著提升长上下文生成吞吐。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention and the linear growth of key-value cache memory transfer. Sparse attention mitigates this by retrieving only relevant tokens, but current approaches either require large-scale training or, within the training-free regime, rely on semantically coarse heuristics or expensive clustering that is difficult to update efficiently during decoding. We introduce CommunityKV, a framework that formulates sparse attention as a community detection problem. CommunityKV constructs a token graph from the $QK^T$ scores already computed during standard prefill, and partitions the graph into communities to enable retrieval of semantically coherent token groups. A local update rule assigns newly generated tokens to communities in constant time, enabling sparse retrieval throughout streaming decoding without global re-partitioning. We evaluate CommunityKV on Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 models across three long-context benchmarks. With one graph per query head, CommunityKV delivers up to $1.25\times$ the end-to-end generation throughput of dense attention, while query-group graph aggregation yields up to $1.71\times$ with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [210] [The Life Cycle of a Massive Activation: Stochastic Birth, Weight-Decay-Driven Growth, and Competitive Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00423)
*S. Aaron McClendon,Jorge Gallego-Feliciano,Antonios Saravanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文追踪了Transformer中大规模激活（massive activations）在训练过程中的出现、增长与固化，发现其尺度主要由权重衰减（weight decay）因果性地调控，并通过AdamW预条件与权重衰减之间的平衡模型解释了激活峰值的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活与注意力汇（attention sinks）相关，但其尺度在训练过程中如何被调控仍未完全理解。作者希望通过训练轨迹分析与受控干预，厘清大规模激活的涌现、增长和固化机制。

Method: 结合训练轨迹分析、受控干预、消融实验及优化器测量。具体包括：跨随机种子分析承载汇的通道；改变权重衰减系数的受控续训；构建AdamW预条件增长与权重衰减相抗衡的平衡模型；扫描衰减系数λ观察峰值时间与峰值幅度的标度律。

Result: 承载汇的通道随随机种子变化但每次运行内早期即稳定；长时训练中周围通道侵蚀、汇集中到少数冗余载体上；梯度衰减遵循汇token的集合均方根幅度而非单通道；去除权重衰减使全局激活尺度持续上升，保留衰减则在恒定学习率下尺度下降；峰值时间随λ近似对数线性偏移，峰值幅度近似按λ^(-1/2)标度；预条件维持大通道群体对抗衰减。

Conclusion: 大规模激活的生命周期可由规模调控的训练动力学解释，权重衰减是调控激活幅度的训练期杠杆，AdamW预条件增长与权重衰减之间的平衡是峰值形成的关键机制。

Abstract: Massive activations, residual-stream coordinates with magnitudes far larger than typical activations, are associated with attention sinks in transformers, but how their scale is regulated during training remains incompletely understood. Combining training-trajectory analyses and controlled interventions, we trace their emergence, growth, and consolidation. Sink-carrying channels vary across random seeds but stabilize early within each run. Over longer training, surrounding channels erode and the sink concentrates onto a few redundant carriers. Across ablations, gradient attenuation follows the sink token's collective root-mean-square magnitude rather than any single channel, making collective scale central to understanding their effects. Our central result is that weight decay causally controls the turnover of global activation scale. In controlled continuations, removing decay near the peak allows this scale to keep rising, whereas retaining it produces decline even at constant learning rate. We develop a balance model for the rise and peak of massive-activation magnitude, in which AdamW-preconditioned growth opposes weight decay. Sweeping the decay coefficient $λ$ shifts peak timing approximately log-linearly and yields peak magnitudes scaling approximately as $λ^{-1/2}$, consistent with this balance. Optimizer measurements further show that preconditioning sustains the large-channel cohort against decay even when raw maintaining forces are too small to do so. Together, these findings connect the observed life cycle to scale-regulating training dynamics and establish weight decay as a training-time lever on activation magnitude.

</details>


### [211] [XOR-Trellis: Ultra-Low-Complexity Dequantization and Curvature-Aware Hadamard-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00432)
*Xiaofan Que,Nir Elkayam,Spandan Pyakurel,Shuokai Pan,Dibakar Gope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型权重的超低位压缩，提出两套互补技术：一个硬件高效、高并行的低复杂度网格解量化器，以及一个在原坐标空间直接反映模型敏感度的曲率感知网格路径优化目标，从而在无需 Hadamard 非相干处理的情况下实现高质量超低位网格量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格编码量化（TCQ）能以超低位宽对大语言模型权重进行高维压缩，避免了传统矢量量化所需的指数级大码本。但实际部署存在两大难题：一是解量化重建的并行吞吐量不足，容易成为推理瓶颈；二是为保持量化精度通常需要昂贵的非相干变换（如 Hadamard 变换），带来额外开销。本文旨在同时解决解量化速度与量化精度两个问题。

Method: 提出两项互补技术：(1) 超低复杂度网格解量化器，采用结构化的硬件高效状态到值的映射，在保持网格搜索多样重建选择的同时实现低复杂度和高并行度重建；(2) 将离散网格路径优化重新表述为曲率感知（curvature-aware）目标，直接在原坐标空间中反映模型敏感性，替代依赖 Hadamard 基的非相干处理。

Result: 两项技术结合后，实现了高质量的超低位网格量化，同时具备廉价、高并行的运行时重建能力，并且完全摆脱了对 Hadamard 基非相干处理流程的依赖。

Conclusion: 通过结构化解量化器与曲率感知路径优化的协同，可以在不牺牲量化精度的前提下，显著降低解量化复杂度和提升并行吞吐量，并去除非相干变换开销，使 TCQ 成为大语言模型超低位权重量化的实用部署方案。

Abstract: Trellis-coded quantization enables high-dimensional compression of large language model (LLM) weights at ultra-low bit widths without the exponentially large codebooks required by conventional vector quantization. Practical deployment, however, presents two challenges: reconstructing compressed weights at sufficient parallel throughput to avoid making dequantization an inference bottleneck, and maintaining quantization accuracy without costly incoherence transformations. We address these challenges with two complementary techniques. First, we introduce an ultra-low-complexity trellis dequantizer that uses a structured, hardware-efficient state-to-value mapping while preserving diverse reconstruction choices for trellis search. Second, we reformulate discrete trellis path optimization with a curvature-aware objective that reflects model sensitivity directly in the original coordinate space. Together, these techniques enable high-quality ultra-low-bit trellis quantization with inexpensive, highly parallel runtime reconstruction and without relying on Hadamard-based incoherence processing.

</details>


### [212] [Every Batch Is Its Own Validation Set: Leave-One-Out Gradient Matching for Online Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00436)
*Hongyu Chen,Xinyi Luo,Ming Zhao,Lin Tang,Zihan Xu,Jing Li,Yuxuan Wang,Haoran Deng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了样本内梯度匹配选择器之所以难以超越全批量训练的原因（对角线上的协方差惩罚使目标偏向噪声最大的样本），并提出去掉对角线、以留一法构造无偏估计的 LOOM 方法：按梯度信噪比加权选子集，无需留出数据即可超越全批量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线批量选择（online batch selection）旨在微调语言模型时挑选最有用的候选批量。匹配候选批量梯度的选择器因无需留出数据而颇具吸引力，但实际却很少能胜过全批量训练。作者试图解释这一现象，并给出一个无需留出数据且真正能超越全批量训练的方法。

Method: 作者指出：样本内梯度匹配让每个样本成为自身目标的一部分，从而在梯度 Gram 矩阵对角线上引入了协方差惩罚（使训练误差乐观的同一来源），导致选择偏向最噪声的样本，并使得全批量成为该目标能达成的最优解。修正方法为去掉对角线——对每个样本而言，其余候选构成数据分布的独立样本，于是匹配目标变为对总体梯度的更新误差的无偏估计。该留一法目标的最小化解按每个样本的梯度信噪比（SNR）加权；当逐样本 SNR 异质性足够大时，半个批量即可给出比全批量更低误差的更新（文中给出精确条件）。基于此构造的 LOOM 在普通反向传播过程中以 Adam 预条件器度量计算 Gram 矩阵，贪婪地选择加权子集，并具有 $(1-e^{-\gamma})$ 的保证，全程不使用留出数据。

Result: 在四个微调任务、七种 1.5B 到 8B 参数的骨干网络上，LOOM 相比全批量训练在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上分别提升 2.3 和 2.4 分；相较每一种样本内梯度匹配器平均高出 2.4 分；相较验证集引导的 GREATS 和 OPUS 高出 1.6–2.0 分；并且能以不到其基础速率五分之一的比率选出注入的标签噪声。

Conclusion: LOOM 通过移除样本内梯度匹配目标中的对角线协方差项，将选择目标转化为关于总体梯度的无偏更新误差估计，并按梯度信噪比加权样本。实验表明，该方法无需任何留出数据即可稳定超越全批量训练及现有梯度匹配和验证引导的批量选择方法，同时显著回避噪声标签。

Abstract: Online batch selection fine-tunes a language model on the most useful part of each candidate batch. Selectors that match the gradient of the candidate batch are attractive because they need no held-out data, yet they rarely beat training on the whole batch. We show why. In-sample gradient matching uses every example as part of its own target, so its objective credits each example with its own gradient noise. This is the covariance penalty that makes training error optimistic, now sitting on the diagonal of the gradient Gram matrix: it steers selection toward the noisiest examples and makes the full batch the best solution the objective can reach. The fix costs nothing. For each example, the other candidates form an independent sample of the data distribution, so removing the diagonal turns the matching objective into an unbiased estimate of the update's error with respect to the population gradient. The minimizer of this leave-one-out objective weights examples by their gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and whenever per-example SNR is heterogeneous enough, half of a batch yields a lower-error update than the whole batch; we give the exact condition. We build \method{} on this principle. It computes the Gram matrix in the metric of the Adam preconditioner during the ordinary backward pass, selects a weighted subset greedily with a $(1-e^{-γ})$ guarantee, and uses no held-out data. Across four fine-tuning tasks and seven backbones from 1.5B to 8B parameters, LOOM improves on full-batch training by 2.3 and 2.4 points on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, exceeds every in-sample gradient matcher by 2.4 points and the validation-guided GREATS and OPUS by 1.6--2.0, and selects injected label noise at under a fifth of its base rate.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Heavy-Tailed Memory Traces in Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00010)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出长程语言智能体依赖外部记忆作为世界模型，但现有评估只看任务成功率或 token 成本，忽略了记忆使用的"形状"。作者通过尾部审计发现记忆检索呈现重尾分布（语义 LLM 策略呈截断幂律），并提出 Core–Tail World Model（CTWM），用单一指数 τ 分配 prompt 预算并保留摘要化尾部，实现 token 节省与尾部预测误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统仅以任务成功或 token 成本评判，忽视了有限上下文与重复检索下记忆使用的结构性问题：记忆会集中在少数核心状态，而稀有状态落入长尾，导致预测误差在尾部累积。作者希望揭示这一"重尾"现象，并将其转化为可操作的记忆控制信号。

Method: 首先进行保守尾部审计（conservative tail audit），比较随机游走与语义 LLM 策略产生的检索分布形状（对数正态 vs. 截断幂律）。随后提出 CTWM，一种基于排序的记忆控制器：用单一指数 τ 按排名分配 prompt 预算，同时保留摘要化的尾部，以平衡核心覆盖与长尾表征。

Result: 审计显示记忆集中具有可复现性但依赖策略：随机游走产生对数正态兼容的检索痕迹，语义 LLM 策略产生最强的截断幂律兼容核心–尾部痕迹。CTWM 在 Synthetic Graph World 上保持完整状态与转移覆盖，prompt token 减少 5.9%，下半部分尾部预测误差降低 13.6%；在 ALFWorld 上获得一致的 token 节省，在 LongMemEval 上 token 减少 24.48% 且聚合准确率持平。

Conclusion: 重尾记忆痕迹不仅是有限检索的诊断特征，也是实现 token 高效智能体世界模型的实用控制信号。通过主动利用核心–尾部结构，CTWM 能以更少的 token 维持覆盖并降低尾部预测误差。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents increasingly rely on external memory as a frozen world model, yet current memory systems are usually judged only by task success or token cost. We argue that the missing object is the shape of memory use: under finite context and repeated retrieval, agent memory can concentrate on a small core while leaving rare states in a long tail where prediction errors accumulate. We study this effect through a conservative tail audit and find that concentration is reproducible but policy-dependent. Random-walk agents produce log-normal-compatible retrieval artifacts, whereas semantic LLM policies yield the strongest truncated-power-law-compatible core--tail traces. Motivated by this audit, we propose Core--Tail World Model (CTWM), a rank-based memory controller that allocates prompt budget with a single exponent $τ$ while retaining a summarized tail. On Synthetic Graph World, CTWM preserves full state and transition coverage, reduces prompt tokens by 5.9%, and lowers bottom-half tail prediction error by 13.6% relative to a graph-memory baseline. The same paired comparison gives consistent token savings on ALFWorld and a 24.48% token reduction on LongMemEval with aggregate accuracy parity. These results suggest that heavy-tailed memory traces are not only a diagnostic of finite retrieval, but also a practical control signal for token-efficient agent world models.

</details>


### [214] [When Do Causal World Models Help Modular LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00012)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体模块化系统中的因果世界模型问题，提出FedCausalCompose框架，证明观测性世界模型存在不可消除的干预误差，并发现因果结构仅在接口可统计识别且以行动时可用的形式呈现时才能帮助智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益通过模块化系统（订单、支付、库存、发货等）运作，一个模块的动作会改变另一模块的有效状态转移。标准世界模型通常拟合观测轨迹，但这并非干预时规划所需的量：轨迹只能显示"支付先于发货"，却无法识别支付是否授权发货、库存是否起中介作用、或是否存在隐藏触发因素同时解释两者。这种观测数据与干预需求之间的鸿沟是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出FedCausalCompose，一个面向模块化LLM智能体的因果世界模型框架，其中局部动作提供跨模块接口的干预响应证据。理论上证明：在存在未阻断后门路径时，观测世界模型会产生不可消除的干预误差；接口恢复能力随干预响应覆盖度提升；在覆盖度和局部机制误差可控时，oracle因果组合能超越非因果下界。随后在结构化工具环境与对话/叙述两类诊断性智能体设置中检验这些预测。

Result: 理论结果：观测世界模型在后门路径未阻断时存在不可消除的干预误差；接口恢复随干预响应覆盖度单调改善；oracle因果组合可突破非因果下界。实验发现：因果接口在结构化工具环境中帮助最大（API签名暴露前置条件和下游效应）；而对话和叙述环境常忽略原始边列表，除非添加一个简短的注意力锚使因果信息在决策时具备相关性。

Conclusion: 论文识别出LLM智能体中因果世界模型生效的具体条件：当跨模块接口既能被统计识别、又以智能体行动时可用的形式呈现时，因果结构才能提供帮助。缺一不可——仅有可识别性而表达形式不可用，或形式易用但结构不可识别，均无法发挥因果结构的作用。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act through modular systems, such as order, payment, inventory, and shipment services, where actions in one module change which transitions are valid in another. Standard world models usually fit observational traces, but this is not the quantity needed for intervention-time planning: a trace may show that payment precedes shipment without identifying whether payment authorizes shipment, inventory mediates the effect, or a hidden trigger explains both. We study this gap through FedCausalCompose, a causal world-model framework for modular LLM agents in which local actions provide intervention-response evidence for cross-module interfaces. We first show that observational world models incur an irreducible interventional error under unblocked back-door paths, that interface recovery improves with intervention-response coverage, and that an oracle causal composition can beat the non-causal lower bound when coverage and local mechanism errors are controlled. We then test the resulting prediction in diagnostic agent settings. Causal interfaces help most in structured tool environments, where API signatures expose preconditions and downstream effects. In contrast, dialogue and narrative environments often ignore raw edge lists unless a short attention anchor makes the causal information decision-relevant. These results identify a concrete condition for causal world models in LLM agents: causal structure helps when cross-module interfaces are both statistically identifiable and presented in a form the agent can use at action time.

</details>


### [215] [From Proposal to Verified Effect: Praxa, an Evidence-Bound Harness for Governed AI Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00015)
*Stefan G. Creadore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Praxa，一个将 LLM 智能体从"提出动作"到"服务上线"的权威-效果转换过程显式化的 agent harness，通过确定性准入、代理执行、外部读回、对账与受审发布等状态建模。作者报告了四项证据通道，但均不构成对安全性、生产率或优越性的强证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 智能体能够提出并执行动作，但"提议（proposal）、权威（authority）、派发（dispatch）、可验证的外部效果（verified external effect）、服务推广（serving promotion）"是彼此不同的声明，缺乏清晰的机制将其显式区分与验证，导致难以对智能体行为的安全性与生产效果进行可信评估。

Method: 提出 Praxa 这一 agent harness，通过确定性准入（deterministic admission）、代理执行（brokered execution）、外部读回（external read-back）、对账（reconciliation）和受审发布（reviewed promotion）等机制，把权威到效果的转换过程显式表示并使其可测试。并开展四类证据评估：仓库本地单元/Workerd 测试与覆盖率、provider 支持的 Terminal-Bench 试点、协调代理（coordination-proxy）开发对比，以及部署的源码/配置证据审计。

Result: 四项证据通道结果：1) 固定版本下仓库本地审计通过 1,027/1,027 单元测试和 89/89 Workerd 测试，插桩全部 363 个预期源文件并满足四项覆盖率下限，但无独立复现；2) Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 试点中，基线组与可靠性层组各通过 12 个精选任务各 17/36 严格试验，可靠性层额外消耗 37.49% 输入与 50.73% 输出 token，不证明优越性；3) 调试后的协调代理对比中，基线与候选各完成 180/180 试验，准确率相同、全密封崩溃恢复、零受保护违规，候选少用 37.11% token、降低 33.84% 端点成本、少 11.63% 步骤，但不证明质量/延迟/生产行为提升；4) 部署源码/配置证据显示有界反射、召回记账、内存编译与工具健康路径，但无生产结果提升。

Conclusion: Praxa 的贡献是一种"证据受限"（evidence-bound）的架构，使权威到效果的转换显式化且可测试。当前证据尚不足以证明对抗安全性、生产安全性、通用专家优越性、自主递归优化或用户收益。

Abstract: Large-language-model agents can propose and execute actions, but proposal, authority, dispatch, verified external effect, and serving promotion are different claims. We present Praxa, an agent harness that represents these states explicitly through deterministic admission, brokered execution, external read-back, reconciliation, and reviewed promotion. We report four evidence lanes. First, an author-run repository-local audit at a pinned revision passed 1,027/1,027 unit tests and 89/89 Workerd tests, instrumented all 363 expected source files, and met four coverage floors; raw per-test transcripts and independent reproduction are unavailable. Second, in a provider-backed Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 pilot across 12 curated tasks, baseline and reliability-layer arms each passed 17/36 strict trials. The reliability layer used 37.49% more input and 50.73% more output tokens, so the pilot does not support superiority. Third, in a post-debug, two-order coordination-proxy development comparison, baseline and a source-authored candidate each completed 180/180 trials with equal measured accuracy, full hermetic crash recovery, and zero protected violations. The candidate used 37.11% fewer tokens, 33.84% lower estimated endpoint cost, and 11.63% fewer steps; this does not establish improved quality, latency, or production behavior. Fourth, deployed source/configuration evidence shows bounded reflection, recall accounting, memory compilation, and tool-health paths, but no production outcome lift. Praxa's supported contribution is an evidence-bound architecture that makes authority-to-effect transitions explicit and testable. Current evidence does not establish adversarial security, production safety, general specialist superiority, autonomous recursive optimization, or user benefit.

</details>


### [216] [What Do Rationales Communicate? A Message-Intervention Study in Role-Specialized QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00018)
*Jiameng Zhang,Hongqiu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种信息干预诊断方法，固定在推理器到验证器之间传递的证据与候选答案，仅改变传递的推理依据，以检验角色分工问答流水线中"推理依据消息"的实际作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色分工的问答流水线日益将推理器输出的理据传递给验证器，但尚不清楚这条消息究竟带来什么：更准确的答案、更可靠的支撑性判断，还是一个全新的失败面。作者希望通过受控诊断厘清理据传递的真实价值与风险。

Method: 提出"消息干预诊断"（message-intervention diagnostic）：固定证据和候选答案不变，只改变跨推理器-验证器边界传递的理据。在400个MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA样本上，以DeepSeek同时作为生成器和验证器，对比忠实理据、无理据、同义改写及被篡改（corrupted）理据对支撑性判断和最终答案的影响，并辅以人工审计、跨模型与任务边界检验。

Result: 忠实理据相比无理据几乎不提升答案准确率；被篡改理据则强烈改变支持性判断。盲验证提示下，无害改写仅引起0–2.5%的支持度偏移，而篡改理据引起10–22%偏移；显式理据检查提示将同一模式放大到34–55%。最终答案变化较小（2–30%），且仅2.9–35.3%的篡改支持翻转伴随答案改变。人工审计中16/42个有效篡改为"篡改过度信任"（corruption-overtrust）案例，9/10个被模型接受的篡改理据被盲评人类拒绝或标记为不明确。跨模型与任务边界检验显示该通道有时活跃、被放大、惰性或被折叠进任务标签。

Conclusion: 理据共享应被评估为一种"验证消息机制"，而不仅仅是提升答案准确率的途径；它可能引入新的失败面，需关注其作为验证信号的有效性与脆弱性。

Abstract: Role-specialized QA pipelines increasingly pass rationales from a reasoner to a verifier, but it is unclear what this message actually buys: better answers, stronger support assessment, or a new failure surface. We introduce a message-intervention diagnostic that fixes the evidence and candidate answer while varying only the rationale passed across the reasoner-to-verifier boundary. On 400 MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultiHopQA examples with DeepSeek as generator and verifier, faithful rationales add almost no answer accuracy over no rationale, while corrupted rationales strongly alter support judgments. Under a blind verifier prompt, harmless paraphrases shift support by only 0--2.5%, whereas corrupted rationales shift support by 10--22%; an explicit rationale-checking prompt amplifies the same pattern to 34--55%. Final answers move less (2--30%), and only 2.9--35.3% of corrupted support flips co-occur with answer changes. Human audits show why this matters: 16/42 valid corruptions are corruption-overtrust cases, and blind humans reject or mark unclear 9/10 audited corrupted rationales that the model accepts. Cross-model and task-boundary checks show when the channel is active, amplified, inert, or folded into the task label. Rationale sharing should be evaluated as a verification-message mechanism, not merely as a route to higher answer accuracy.

</details>


### [217] [Measuring the Microtask Eligibility Gap: When Is an Off-the-Shelf SLM Enough for an Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00025)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用小型语言模型（SLM）在围绕前沿大模型规划器的四个微任务上的可用性，发现所有规模（0.6B–8B）的现成 SLM 均无法达到预设的、以廉价非 LLM 基线锚定的资格阈值，且量化（4-bit）会造成规模相关的损害但并不能改变结论；失败可区分为能力不足与解码阈值可调两类，最终建议将 SLM 置于达标基线之后作为辅助组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent 架构越来越倾向于用小型语言模型处理大模型规划器周边的微任务（如自动审批 shell 命令、写记忆、选工具、重排历史轮次），但缺少对这些 SLM 是否真正达到从业者定义阈值的严格评估。作者希望回答：现成 SLM 能否满足预设阈值？失败原因是什么？量化是否改变结论？

Method: 构建包含 4 个微任务的基准，每个任务配有固定 prompt、自动指标，以及锚定于廉价非 LLM 基线的预设阈值 τ 和基于置信区间的资格判定规则（仅当置信下界超过 τ 才算通过）。在 Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B 的最佳配置（FP16、贪心解码、单一冻结 prompt、无微调）上进行扫描；对 T1/T3/T4 采用基于 logprob 的决策阈值诊断（T2 用上下文长度/级联探针），将失败分为能力缺陷与可通过调整解码阈值解决的问题（4 种状态）。进一步测试 4-bit 量化（RTN/GPTQ/AWQ），在可重构硬标签任务 T1/T3 上做正式认证、T2/T4 上做诊断/窗口稳健性评估；并在 Llama-3.x（12/12 不合格）、锚点 τ 扫描、3 组中性释义 prompt（0/112）上验证稳健性。

Result: 存在“资格缺口”：16 个配置（4 任务 × 4 模型）中 0 个通过（并经检查原始输出与解析器行为验证）。诊断将失败分为能力缺陷与可调阈值两类。4-bit 量化（RTN/GPTQ/AWQ）造成与模型规模相关的损害，但没有任何配置因此进入合格区间；资格缺口主要跟踪模型规模而非数值精度。结果在 Llama-3.x（12/12 不合格）、锚点选择（τ 扫描）和 prompt 措辞（0/112）上稳健。实践上，将 SLM 置于已达 CI 阈值的基线之后，仅在其失败处使用 SLM：例如 4B 重排器叠加在 BM25 短列表上可超过 BM25（+0.047 [0.020, 0.073]），尽管其自身未通过资格认证。

Conclusion: 现成 SLM 无法自主满足代理微任务的从业者定义阈值，量化不改变这一结论，资格缺口主要由模型规模而非精度决定；失败可细分为能力性不足与解码阈值可调两类。因此建议不要直接用 SLM 担任此类角色，而是将其作为已达标基线的增强组件（如重排器），仅在基线不达标的场景下调用。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly want to run small language models (SLMs) on the microtasks around a frontier large language model (LLM) planner: auto-approving shell commands, writing memory, selecting tools, ranking past turns. We ask whether off-the-shelf SLMs meet practitioner-defined thresholds and, when they fail, why, and whether quantization changes the answer. We build a benchmark of 4 such microtasks with fixed prompts and automatic metrics, each with a pre-specified threshold $τ$ anchored to a cheap non-LLM baseline and a CI-aware eligibility rule (a configuration passes only if its confidence bound clears $τ$). Sweeping Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B at their best (FP16, greedy, one frozen prompt, no tuning), we find an eligibility gap: 0 of 16 (4 tasks $\times$ 4 models) configurations pass (verified by checking the raw outputs and parser behavior). A logprob decision-threshold diagnostic (T1/T3/T4; T2 via a context-length/cascade probe) separates the failures into capability deficits and failures that can be addressed by changing the decoding threshold (4 regimes). Quantization to 4-bit (RTN/GPTQ/AWQ) does damage that depends on model size and moves no configuration into eligibility (certified on the reconstructable hard-label tasks T1/T3, diagnostic/windowed robustness on T2/T4), so the gap tracks model size more than precision; it replicates on Llama-3.x (12/12 ineligible) and is robust to the anchor choice (a $τ$-sweep) and to prompt wording (0/112 eligible across the original plus 3 neutral paraphrases per cell). The practical implication: place SLMs behind a baseline that meets the CI-backed threshold, and use the SLM only where the baseline fails to meet the threshold; e.g. a 4B re-ranker over a BM25 shortlist beats BM25 ($+0.047$ [0.020, 0.073], without itself certifying eligibility).

</details>


### [218] [Characterizing a Configuration Where Inference-Time PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting Is Inert: Evidence from Three Reasoning LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00047)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRM剪枝片段嫁接（PPFG）在多个模型与基准上被证明与独立并行思维链基线统计等价，该机制对推理结果无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行思维链中的多样性坍缩促使研究者设计基于过程奖励模型（PRM）的推理时干预。以往片段嫁接工作仅在附加补偿成分下才报告增益，本文旨在隔离最省成本的跨轨迹步骤级迁移机制（PPFG），并在该特定运行点检验其本身是否真正有效。

Method: 将PPFG作为最省成本的跨轨迹步骤级迁移操作化，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Math-Shepherd上对完整MATH500（n=500，三个种子）测试停滞型与随机型两种变体；通过四桶分类分析322次停滞规则注入事件；采用双向单侧检验（TOST）将奇偶性提升为正向等价；并进行逐事件抽查、幸存兄弟反事实与事后神谕上界分析。

Result: PPFG在所有测量轴上与独立并行思维链基线统计不可区分；仅14%的注入事件真正针对挣扎中的链；随机对照在2.4倍触发率下达到同样奇偶性；该发现复现于三个基础LM、六个基准、第二个PRM及兼容门扫描，12个Qwen/LLaMA单元均达正向等价；注入链剪枝率是匹配步率的2.75倍，但无总体级补偿；事后神谕给出的每次问题选择PPFG的相对增益上界仅+0.13个百分点。

Conclusion: PPFG作为推理时机制在此测试运行点上是无效的，其惰性并非特定于启发式规则；作者贡献了一套用于确立推理时机制无效性的等价检验模板，并强调所有结论仅限定于所测运行点。

Abstract: Diversity collapse in parallel chain-of-thought has motivated inference-time interventions built on a natural design: when a process reward model (PRM) prunes a chain, its high-PRM prefix is extracted and grafted verbatim as an in-context demonstration into a still-decoding sibling. We isolate this mechanism, PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting (PPFG), as the most cost-minimal operationalization of cross-trajectory step-level transfer, and test it at the operating point where prior fragment-grafting work reports gains only under additional compensating ingredients. On Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with Math-Shepherd on full MATH500 (n=500, three seeds), PPFG in both stagnation- and random-targeting variants is statistically indistinguishable from an independent parallel-CoT baseline on every measured axis. We characterize why: a four-bucket classification of 322 stagnation-rule injection events shows only 14% targeted a genuinely struggling chain; the rest landed on chains that had already succeeded, were near completion, or sat on a flat PRM plateau, states a rescue graft cannot change. No compound-gate refinement jointly achieves well-targeted firing and adequate density, and a random control matches the same parity at 2.4x the firing rate, so the inertness is not heuristic-specific. The finding replicates across three base LMs, six benchmarks, a second PRM, and a compatibility-gate sweep; two-one-sided-tests analysis promotes the parity to positive equivalence on all twelve Qwen/LLaMA cells. A per-event spot-check finds injected chains prune at 2.75x the matched-step rate, but a surviving-sibling counterfactual finds no population-level compensation. A hindsight oracle bounds any per-problem gain from choosing PPFG over independent at +0.13 pp. We contribute an equivalence-testing template for establishing inference-time mechanism nulls, with every claim scoped to its tested operating point.

</details>


### [219] [Gradient-Aligned Pair Selection for Personalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00061)
*Ruoming Jin,Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Lei Xu,Haixun Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GAP-DPO，将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐的优化问题，论证偏好对的选择是优化几何的内在组成而非启发式预处理，并通过迭代的效用感知、几何对齐配对选择显著提升个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化 LLM 需要对齐用户特定偏好而非聚合质量；现有 DPO 方法依赖似然极值等启发式准则选择偏好对，使优化与显式用户效用脱节，导致个性化退化。作者旨在从梯度交互的理论视角揭示配对选择如何决定个性化优化的成败，并将其纳入优化几何框架。

Method: 将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐优化问题，分析期望用户效用梯度与 DPO 更新方向的一阶交互；在离策略采样下揭示当偏好边际与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO 更新从纯误差校正信号转变为类强化更新。基于此提出 GAP-DPO：迭代进行效用感知、几何对齐的配对选择，并通过逐轮（epoch-wise）再生控制分布偏移。

Result: 在个性化文本生成基准上，GAP-DPO 相较标准 DPO 变体，在风格保真度、偏好对齐和生成质量上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 梯度对齐可作为个性化偏好优化的统一原则；配对选择是优化几何的内在组成部分，而非启发式预处理步骤。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires aligning generation behavior with user-specific preferences rather than aggregate quality. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) provides a stable framework for preference learning, its effectiveness in personalized settings critically depends on how preference pairs are selected. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic criteria, such as likelihood-based extremes, which decouple optimization from explicit user utility and can lead to degraded personalization. We formalize personalized preference learning as a geometry-aligned optimization problem by analyzing the first-order interaction between gradients of expected user utility and DPO update directions. Our analysis reveals that, under off-policy sampling, the DPO update transitions from a purely error-corrective signal to a reinforcement-like update when preference margins are directionally aligned with utility gradients. This perspective exposes pair selection as a geometric decision that governs whether preference optimization advances or hinders personalization. Motivated by this insight, we propose GAP-DPO (Geometry-Aligned Preference DPO), an iterative algorithm that performs utility-aware, geometry-aligned pair selection while controlling distribution shift via epoch-wise regeneration. Experiments on personalized text generation benchmarks show that GAP-DPO consistently improves stylistic fidelity, preference alignment, and generation quality compared to standard DPO variants. Together, our results establish gradient alignment as a unifying principle for personalized preference optimization and demonstrate that pair selection is an intrinsic component of the optimization geometry rather than a heuristic preprocessing step.

</details>


### [220] [K-Dense BYOK: An Open-Source AI Research Assistant That Runs Locally and Keeps a Hash-Chained Lab Notebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00074)
*Aubrey M. Brueckner,Darshil Patel,Yuhuan He,Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: K-Dense BYOK 是一款免费开源的本地 AI 科研助手，通过项目化文件夹、科学程序库、不可篡改的观察日志等手段提升科研可复现性，并在 20 个跨学科任务上以固定评分表领先两家托管平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聊天助手和通用编码代理在科研场景中最常见的失败是模型过度声称（overclaiming）。作者希望提供一个既保留研究者对模型和数据控制权、又能把 AI 代理的科研产出变得可核查、可复现的工具，从而解决"声称的结果无法验证"这一核心问题。

Method: K-Dense BYOK 采用"自带密钥/模型"的本地运行架构，每个项目即一个普通文件夹，数据、代码、结果与记录均保留在研究者自管的机器上。系统内置：书面科学程序库、引导式工作流模板、研究数据来源目录、可转交任务的审稿人与撰写人角色；维护"活页实验室笔记本"（Living Lab Notebook），条目只能追加不能删除，并链接到论证；通过观察代理的实际行为来记录日志，代理无可写该日志的工具，从而让声明可核查。系统还在固定评分表下，用 20 个跨学科研究提示词与两个托管平台进行对比。

Result: 在 20 个跨学科研究提示词上，按预先固定的评分表，K-Dense BYOK 在科学质量和研究执行两方面均领先两个托管平台。它的交付物是唯一记录所运行软件环境的，且通常附带一条可重新生成结果的命令。使用相同前沿模型的一个托管平台两者皆未提供。这些环境记录是代理写入的文件，而非被观察日志的一部分（观察日志目前尚未捕获软件环境本身）。

Conclusion: K-Dense BYOK 通过让声明"可核查"而非一味"阻止"声明，有效应对模型过度声称这一主要失败模式，在科学质量、研究执行与可复现性上优于托管平台。代码以 MIT 协议开源（https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok）。

Abstract: K-Dense BYOK (bring your own keys) is a free, open-source AI research assistant for scientists in any field that runs on the researcher's own computer. The researcher supplies access to a model of their choice, hosted or running locally, and the application supplies everything else: a place for the work to run, a layer of scientific scaffolding, and a complete record. Each project is an ordinary folder, so the data, the code, the results, and the record stay on a machine the researcher administers and can be read years later without the application. Three things separate it from a chat assistant or a general-purpose coding agent. It ships a library of written scientific procedures, guided workflow templates, catalogs of where research data can be found, and reviewer and writer roles the agent can hand work to. It keeps a Living Lab Notebook whose entries link into an argument and are added to but never erased. And it records what happened by watching what the agent does rather than by taking the agent's word for it, in a log the agent has no tool that can write to. That choice targets the most common failure, model overclaiming, in our earlier benchmark of nine frontier models, by making claims checkable rather than preventing them. On twenty interdisciplinary research prompts, scored under a rubric fixed in advance, K-Dense BYOK led two managed platforms on both scientific quality and research execution. Its deliverables were the only ones that recorded the software they ran in, and the only ones that usually arrived with a command that regenerates the results. One of the managed platforms ran the same frontier model and supplied neither. Those environment records were files the agent wrote, not part of the observed log, which does not yet capture the software environment itself. The code is available under the MIT license at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok.

</details>


### [221] [Scientific Agents: Evaluating Profession-Specific System Prompts on Scientific Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00084)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现：详细的专业化系统提示词（如 AGENTS.md 职业画像）会增加 token 消耗和成本，却未带来一致的准确率提升，在部分任务上甚至更低；其唯一意外优势是在 API 频繁掉线时（长提示更稳定）保持更高的一遍通过率，但这源于提示长度/格式而非专业内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估"专业定制化系统提示词（profession-specific system prompts）是否真的能提升大模型在科学任务上的表现。此前缺少对这类详细职业画像（profiles）在准确率与成本之间权衡的系统性量化研究，因此作者构建开源语料并设计多条件对照实验来检验其实际价值。

Method: 作者构建了 Scientific Agents 开源语料库，含 503 个职业专属 AGENTS.md 画像；用 Gemini 3.8 Flash（经 OpenRouter，运行于 Pi agent harness）进行评测。设置 5 个条件对比：最小基线（"You are a helpful assistant"）、画像首句角色句、通用科学严谨性指南、无关领域画像、以及匹配画像。在 9 个文本类科学基准（4,531 道抽样题、100 个匹配画像）上测试，4,488 项在 API 错误重试后完成全部 5 个条件，采用自动化规则评分。另在 60 道 BioMysteryBench 生物信息学工具题（基线/画像各跑 3 次）上对比求解率。统计用 bootstrap 置信区间，成本按 token 用量估算。

Result: 匹配画像相对基线的平均准确率差为 -0.6 个百分点（固定任务上 95% bootstrap 区间 [-1.5, +0.2]），无任何基准显示统计上明确的提升。匹配画像产生的输出 token 是基线的 1.5–2.3 倍，单次成功调用成本高出 2.2–4.5 倍。在 BioMysteryBench 上，画像条件下平均求解率为 46.7%，基线为 56.7%，差 -10.0 个百分点（95% 区间 [-16.7, -3.3]），主要因画像导致更多 token/时间限制停止。唯一操作层面的意外优势：在 SuperGPQA 上，因 API 频繁掉线，短基线仅有 54.0% 的题目首次通过即为正确答案，而画像为 71.6%；且通用与错配提示也差不多可靠，说明该优势来自提示长度或格式而非领域专业知识。

Conclusion: 对于所测模型与任务，默认加载完整职业画像不能提升准确率，且成本显著更高；选择性检索画像片段或在开放式科学任务中的效果仍有待验证。

Abstract: Detailed profession-specific system prompts raise token use and estimated cost per response without a consistent accuracy gain. We evaluate Scientific Agents, an open-source corpus of 503 profession-specific AGENTS.md profiles, with Gemini 3.8 Flash via OpenRouter in the Pi agent harness. We compare matched profiles with four controls: a minimal baseline ("You are a helpful assistant"), the profile's opening role sentence, a generic scientific rigor guide, and a profile from an unrelated domain. Across nine text-based science benchmarks (4,531 sampled questions, 100 matched profiles), 4,488 items completed all five conditions after API-error retries, scored with automated, rule-based grading. The average profile-baseline accuracy difference is -0.6 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval [-1.5, +0.2] across fixed tasks), and no benchmark shows a statistically clear improvement. Matched profiles produced 1.5-2.3 times as many output tokens and cost 2.2-4.5 times more per successful call. On 60 tool-using BioMysteryBench bioinformatics problems (three runs each for baseline and profile), mean solve rates were 46.7% with the profile and 56.7% at baseline, a difference of -10.0 percentage points (95% interval [-16.7, -3.3]) driven by more frequent token- and time-limit stops under the profile. Longer prompts had one unexpected operational advantage: on SuperGPQA, frequent provider API drops left the short baseline with a correct first-pass answer on only 54.0% of items, against 71.6% with the profile. Generic and mismatched prompts were about as reliable, so this gain comes from prompt length or formatting rather than domain expertise. For the tested model and tasks, loading full profession profiles by default does not improve accuracy and costs considerably more; whether selective retrieval of profile sections or open-ended scientific tasks would change this remains to be tested.

</details>


### [222] [Comedic Fool's Gold: Reward Exploits and Countermeasures in Conversational Humor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00197)
*Sam Larson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对话幽默语言模型训练中的自动奖励设计，发现两类奖励均存在可利用捷径，并通过奖励修订与强化学习训练评估改进效果，但幽默专项提升未达预设目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动奖励设计存在核心难题：奖励信号常被模型通过非目标行为（捷径）利用，导致评分虚高但实际能力未提升。作者希望探究对话幽默场景下奖励的漏洞，并验证反制措施能否在封堵捷径的同时保留奖励预期的幽默行为。

Method: 作者提出两类自动奖励：一是基于嵌入的意外性（surprise）奖励，旨在捕捉可理解的意外感；二是预测观众发笑（audience model）的奖励。通过受控测试检验二者的可攻击性，并引入流畅性过滤器、跨说话人笑声线索归一化等反制措施。随后进行三次强化学习训练，每次使用递进修订后的奖励，比较综合评分与零分会话比例的变化。

Result: 受控测试显示，嵌入型意外奖励对打乱词序的回复与机智回复同样给予高分；流畅性过滤器能识别乱序，但组合后的奖励也误拒部分机智回复，且未通过进一步验证。观众模型预测的笑声易被任一说话人消息中的笑声线索利用；跨说话人归一化可阻断已覆盖的攻击，但未匹配的表达方式仍可被利用。三次强化学习训练中，最终修订版将综合评分提高0.0903、零分会话减少40%，但幽默专项提升仍未达预注册目标。

Conclusion: 研究揭示自动奖励设计的普遍性挑战：反制措施必须在封堵可被利用的捷径与保留奖励本欲鼓励的行为之间取得平衡。单点封堵往往顾此失彼，说明需更系统的奖励评估与设计方法。

Abstract: We investigate automated rewards for training language models in conversational humor, focusing on reward exploits and countermeasures. Two approaches aim to capture understandable surprise and predicted audience amusement. Controlled tests show that an embedding-based surprise reward accepts word-shuffled replies as readily as witty ones. A fluency filter detects the shuffles, but the combined reward also rejects some witty replies and fails further validation. An audience model's predicted laughter is instead vulnerable to laughter cues in either speaker's messages. Normalizing these cues across speakers blocks the covered attacks, although unmatched expressions remain exploitable. Three reinforcement-learning runs evaluate training with successive reward revisions. The final run improves the combined evaluation score by 0.0903 and reduces zero-score sessions by 40%, but its humor-specific improvement remains below our preregistered target. These findings illustrate a broader challenge for automated reward design: countermeasures must block exploitable shortcuts while preserving the behavior the reward was intended to encourage.

</details>


### [223] [EviGraph: Proof-Carrying Selective Recommendation over Temporal Public-Service Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00212)
*Yixi Zhou,Sikun Wang,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EviGraph，通过区分"关键决策要求"与"可保留未解决的信息"来改善公共服务推荐，减少不必要的拒绝回答，并发现过度验证反而会撤回合理推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务推荐需要证据与所请求的服务、范围、日期精确匹配，但将每一个缺失细节都视为决定性的做法会过度保守，导致本可提供的有用推荐被拒绝。因此需要一种机制来区分哪些信息是决策所必需的、哪些可以保持未解决。

Method: 提出 EviGraph 框架：一个语言智能体（language agent）将关键决策要求与时序知识图谱（temporal knowledge graph）中的证据建立关联，再由一个确定性检查器（deterministic checker）判定某一推荐是否得到充分支持。

Result: 在双语香港公共服务基准（含可执行政策参考）上的评估表明，区分关键决策要求与可未解决信息能够显著减少不必要的拒绝回答；而额外的验证反而会撤回原本有支持依据的推荐，且并未提升决策质量。

Conclusion: 可靠的循证导航关键在于明确规定"做决策必须确立什么"，而非简单地堆叠更多验证环节。

Abstract: Public-service recommendations require evidence that matches the requested service, scope, and date. Yet treating every missing detail as decisive can withhold useful recommendations. We introduce EviGraph, which distinguishes critical decision requirements from information that can remain unresolved. A language agent links these requirements to evidence in a temporal knowledge graph, while a deterministic checker establishes whether a recommendation is supported. Evaluation on a bilingual Hong Kong public-service benchmark with executable policy references shows that this distinction reduces unnecessary abstention. Additional verification, however, can withdraw supported recommendations without improving decision quality. These findings suggest that reliable evidence-based navigation depends on specifying what must be established for a decision, rather than simply adding more verification.

</details>


### [224] [Build2SPARQL: A Large-Scale Text-to-SPARQL Benchmark Dataset for Building Knowledge Graph Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00224)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Build2SPARQL，一个通过知识图谱遍历代码生成 SPARQL 查询、由大语言模型仅生成自然语言问题的大规模基准，包含 6,136 条可执行查询与 30,680 个问题，并通过检索增强将三个开源模型的精确匹配准确率从 0.2–20% 提升至 56–65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 楼宇自动化系统日益以 Brick、ASHRAE 223P 等本体表示为语义知识图谱，为 AI 应用提供机器可读基础。将自然语言问题翻译为 SPARQL（text-to-SPARQL）能让楼宇运维人员通过语言代理查询图谱，但缺乏大规模自然语言/SPARQL 基准，限制了该方向的发展。

Method: 提出 KG-grounded（图谱锚定）流水线：SPARQL 查询完全由图遍历代码生成并验证，大语言模型仅负责生成自然语言问题，从而使查询正确性与模型行为解耦。该流水线挖掘六类查询模式（线性链、分支、UNION、聚合、OPTIONAL、属性过滤），并在五个词汇语域下对每个查询进行措辞改写；应用于 201 个楼宇知识图谱（180 个 Brick、21 个 ASHRAE 223P）。

Result: 生成 6,136 条可执行 SPARQL 查询和 30,680 个问题。对 300 个问题的人工双评显示：98.8% 语义保真度、98.8% 自然度、84.0% 操作合理性。在三个开源权重语言模型上的检索增强评测将精确匹配准确率从零样本的 0.2–20% 提升到三样本检索的 56–65%。

Conclusion: Build2SPARQL 提供了一种无需依赖模型行为即可保证查询正确性的大规模基准构建方法，并为楼宇图谱上的 text-to-SPARQL 任务提供了有效评估；检索增强显著提升了开源模型的表现，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Building automation systems are increasingly represented as semantic knowledge graphs (KGs) using ontologies such as Brick and ASHRAE 223P, creating a machine-readable substrate for artificial-intelligence applications. One promising application is translating natural-language questions into SPARQL (text-to-SPARQL), which would let building operators query these graphs through language agents, but progress is limited by the scarcity of large natural-language/SPARQL benchmarks. This paper presents Build2SPARQL, a large-scale benchmark for building KGs generated by a KG-grounded pipeline: SPARQL queries are produced and validated entirely by graph-traversal code, while large language models generate only the natural-language questions, keeping query correctness independent of model behavior. The pipeline mines six query-pattern families -- linear chains, branching, UNION, aggregation, OPTIONAL, and attribute-filtered -- and phrases each query across five vocabulary registers. Applied to 201 building KGs (180 Brick, 21 ASHRAE 223P), it yields 6,136 executable SPARQL queries and 30,680 questions. A two-rater human validation of 300 questions found 98.8% semantic fidelity, 98.8% naturalness, and 84.0% operational plausibility. A retrieval-augmented evaluation across three open-weight language models raised exact-match accuracy from 0.2-20% (zero-shot) to 56-65% (three-shot retrieved).

</details>


### [225] [Robust Is Salient: An Informed Adversary Moves the Optimal Signal onto the Salience Pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00233)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在受限信令通道中引入知情对手模型，证明对抗鲁棒的最优信号与显著性极点完全重合，且这一重合限制了经验评估的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究的显著性极点为何是最优，及其与贝叶斯判别的关系尚未在对抗性环境中得到解释；作者想探究当知情对手共享信令通道时，鲁棒最优信号是否等同于能最好描述真值的信号，并考察二者重合对经验评估的影响。

Method: 作者在强制选择任务（抽象自《香港谋杀案》）中引入一个知情对手：对手知道目标、观察信号，并在说服预算β下为最强错误答案辩护。当β=0时还原原始oracle模型（监听温度τ=1）；随β增大，最优信号从后验最大化选项转移至边缘最大化选项。通过108个确认项和200,000项池进行验证，并测量语言模型在两种对手框架下的选项变化。

Result: 在108个确认项中，对手鲁棒最优与显著性极点完全一致；在200,000项池中二者仅2,748项不同，且分歧恰好位于显著性到贝叶斯坐标未定义处。18.2%的池在有限预算下最优发生转移，每项的临界预算精确。两种对手框架使7个语言模型在108项中的30至77项改变选择，但由于鲁棒目标与显著性目标完全相同，测量无法判定该移动方向，构成结构性限制而非零结果。

Conclusion: 在有知情对手时，从贝叶斯判别完全转向显著性即可实现鲁棒，且二者重合在结构上限制了经验评估；评估对手意识前应廉价地检验鲁棒目标是否与启发式目标重合。

Abstract: When an informed adversary shares the audience of a constrained signalling channel, the signal that best protects the truth is the signal that best describes it. On 108 confirmatory items, the adversary-robust optimum aligns exactly with the salience pole from prior work. Across a 200,000-item pool, the two differ on only 2,748 items --- lying exactly where the prior salience-to-Bayes coordinate is undefined. Where defined, robustness is achieved by moving from Bayesian discrimination entirely to salience. We show this by introducing an adversary to a forced-choice task (abstracted from Deception: Murder in Hong Kong). The adversary knows the target, observes the signal, and argues for the strongest wrong answer using a persuasion budget, $β$. As $β$ grows, the optimal signal shifts from the posterior-maximizing option to the margin-maximizing one; at $β= 0$, the game reproduces the original oracle model with a listener temperature of $τ= 1$. This effect is real: 18.2 percent of the pool has an optimum that shifts under a finite budget, and each item's critical budget is exact. This coincidence structurally limits empirical evaluation. Two adversary framings change the chosen option of seven language models on 30 to 77 of 108 items against an exact no-effect rate. Yet, no measurement can determine whether this movement is toward the adversary-aware optimum or toward salience, because the two options are identical. This is a structural limit, not a null result. The diagnostic check is cheap: before evaluating adversary-awareness, verify whether the robust target coincides with a heuristic target on the evaluation items.

</details>


### [226] [Conflicting Supervision Moves Commitment, Not Capability: A 12.29σ arrangement effect that is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00234)
*Wenhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究数据顺序（约定冲突下的同一批问题）如何影响模型参数中写入的内容，指出学习率调度是决定“顺序效应”大小的关键因素，并证明衰减调度会抑制顺序效应，而恒定学习率在最后一步会放大它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者想探究：当同一批问题以两种互不兼容、但各自正确的约定呈现给模型训练时，数据的排列顺序会在模型参数中“写入”什么。更进一步，作者认为学习率调度不是无关的背景条件，而是决定顺序效应大小的算子，因此想厘清“顺序效应”的真正来源。

Method: 作者推导出一个界，其中“排列方式”和“调度”以分离的相乘因子进入顺序效应——排列只作为“块周期”进入，调度只作为“端点能赋予运行中任一时刻的权重”进入。并通过实验：对同一个语料的十种排序、固定预算、仅路径不同，在仅 lr_scheduler_type 不同的两组调度族下各跑两次，对比恒定学习率与单一余弦调度下的“分配份额”（allocation share）与对比度（contrast floors）。

Result: 在恒定学习率下，内部跨度在分配上为 0.2221、对比度底限为 11.63，且单调取决于排列的阻塞程度；而在单一余弦调度下，同十个臂只占据两个可区分状态（而其自身分辨率本可允许约十个）。十二个臂中 acc_A+acc_B 恒定性在 9.7% 以内，分配份额却在 0.04 到 0.87 之间，因此本文测得的 12.29σ 的排列切换在约定无关指标下恰好为零。该守恒性质在衰减调度族中更清晰，恒定学习率下噪声更大；在提示中标注约定会使切换坍缩并达到并集上限的 87.5%。

Conclusion: “顺序重要”与“顺序不重要”是同一旋钮的两端，学习率调度（尤其是衰减）决定了这一旋钮的位置。模型路径实际写入的是模型所承诺采用的约定，而精确匹配基准无法察觉这一点。衰减调度抑制顺序效应的机制、排列与调度的分离，以及对两半因子的受控测量，是本文的贡献。

Abstract: "Train a model on the same problems written under two incompatible conventions, both correct, and ask what the ordering of that data writes into the parameters. The learning-rate schedule is not a background condition for that question. It is the averaging operator, and it decides the answer. We prove a bound in which the arrangement and the schedule enter the ordering effect as separate multiplied factors: the arrangement only as a block period, the schedule only as how much weight the endpoint can place on any one moment of the run. A decaying schedule cannot put a large step size and an uncontracted remainder at the same moment; a constant one does exactly that at the last step. That decay moderates ordering effects has been reported in pretraining; the mechanism, the separation, and a controlled measurement of both halves are ours. Ten orderings of one corpus, one budget, everything but the path held fixed, run twice under families differing in lr_scheduler_type and nothing else: at a constant rate the interior spans 0.2221 in allocation, 11.63 contrast floors, monotone in how blocked the arrangement is. Under the single cosine every published arm uses, the same ten arms occupy two distinguishable states where their own resolution would allow about ten. "Order matters" and "order does not matter" are the two ends of one knob. What the path writes is which convention the model commits to, and no exact-match benchmark can see it. Across twelve arms acc_A+acc_B is constant to within 9.7% while the allocation share runs 0.04 to 0.87, so the 12.29-sigma arrangement switch this paper measures is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic metric. That conservation is quoted from the decayed family throughout, the constant-rate one being a noisier place to read it. Marking the convention in the prompt collapses the switch and reaches 87.5% of the union ceiling."

</details>


### [227] [Knowing When to Yield: Grounded Arbitration of User Corrections in Text-Based Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00282)
*Yezhou Cheng,Runjia Du,Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: How should an embodied agent respond when a person's correction may be wrong? We formulate grounded correction arbitration as a choice among accepting, rejecting, inspecting the world, and asking the speaker. GAVA implements this interface with observation-bounded evidence, legal probes, and a one-step expected-loss rule. In text-only ALFWorld, 162 checkpoints produce 972 paired true and false interventions. Complete local inspections give GAVA and always verify 100 percent correction accuracy, establishing the evidence contract rather than a comparative advantage. In same-episode execution, GAVA reduces interaction cost against always verify but ties a cost threshold under a perfect speaker. An exploratory training-only object-location prior lowers interaction and declared joint cost on 340 unseen scenarios by 0.490 and 0.420 relative to uniform GAVA. After freezing the policy, costs, baselines, and multiplicity plan, the gains replicate on 77 non-overlapping seen checkpoints, covering 308 scenarios: 0.595 and 0.517, with both 95 percent checkpoint-bootstrap confidence intervals excluding zero. Joint cost also improves over an identical-prior fixed policy, while the matched calibrated no-VOI comparison remains inconclusive. Semantic GAVA makes four factual errors in each cohort, corresponding to 98.8 percent and 98.7 percent accuracy, and all methods complete every task. Results support selective information gathering with semantic priors under declared costs, but do not establish a general advantage of environmental value of information over clarification. The study uses normalized claims, complete symbolic observations, and controlled speakers; it evaluates neither human participants, visual input, nor physical robots.

</details>


### [228] [Rules to Tools: Executable Checks for LLM Agents in Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00313)
*Jingjie Ning,Guojiang Zhao,Chen Xu,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了科学编码智能体在配备"可执行公共要求检查"(R2T)后,与仅提供文本要求相比的程序修复效果与成本。结果显示工具组和文本组在多数任务上打平或互有胜负,差异在统计上不显著,但工具能显著降低模型输出量;差异高度依赖具体任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体需要依据书面要求(方程、边界条件、输出要求)评估和修复程序,但缺少现成的可执行检查来验证其是否满足公共科学要求。作者希望量化"提供可调用检查工具"相对于"仅提供文本要求"对修复成功率、任务依赖性与智能体侧成本的影响。

Method: 作者采用配对实验设计,在共享书面检查的SciCode修复组中,对比"仅文本"组与"工具(可调用检查实现)"组,固定起始程序、模型和预算。使用两类任务ID群组(以及一个8-ID群组、共享定义的SciCode群组、5个开发暴露任务)进行对比,并通过任务簇自举法(task-cluster bootstrap)估计组间差异的95%置信区间;同时测量智能体输出节省与公共CPU使用等成本指标,并设置了一条从源码直接到Python的对照臂(arm)。

Result: 在30任务的两类task-ID群组中,完整修复率文本组为26/30、工具组为29/30,其中3个任务ID工具占优、1个文本占优、11个打平。8-ID群组为13/16对15/16,自举95%置信区间为[-12.5, 43.75]个百分点,差异不显著。共享定义的SciCode群组两组均为13/24。5个开发暴露任务(更换起始程序)为3/10对7/10。工具占优的任务17、77、11中,初始检查仅标记了任务17,任务77和11未报告违规;文本占优的任务37也无初始违规。源码到Python对照臂同样达到15/16。在配对PDE比较中,详细文本23/24、检查24/24,检查组模型输出降低31.2%。智能体输出节省因群组而异,两类task-ID群组公共CPU使用均上升。

Conclusion: 准备好的可执行检查能小幅提升修复成功率,且能大幅降低模型输出(在PDE任务中达31.2%),但总体优势在统计上不显著,效果高度依赖具体任务;部分工具占优任务并未被初始检查标记违规,提示工具的效益可能来自于迭代式反馈而非简单初筛。文本组在部分任务上仍具优势,成本(如公共CPU)因群组而异。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents receive equations, boundary conditions, and output requirements in writing, then must assess the programs they revise. Rules to Tools (R2T) supplies prepared executable checks of public scientific requirements. Matched SciCode repair groups share written checks, starting programs, model, and budgets; the tool group receives a callable implementation. Across two task-ID cohorts, complete repair is 26/30 with text and 29/30 with the prepared checks. Three task IDs favor tools, one favors text, and eleven tie. The eight-ID cohort scores 13/16 versus 15/16, with a task-cluster bootstrap 95% interval of [-12.5, 43.75] percentage points for the difference. The larger shared-definition SciCode cohort ties at 13/24 per group. Five development-exposed tasks with alternate starting programs score 3/10 versus 7/10. The tool group favors tasks 17, 77, and 11; initial checks flag task 17 and report no violation for tasks 77 and 11. Task 37 favors text and has no initial reported violation. A fresh source-through-Python arm also reaches 15/16, matching the dedicated command's aggregate. In a matched PDE comparison, detailed text scores 23/24 and checks score 24/24, with 31.2% lower reported model output for checks. Agent-side output savings vary by cohort, while public CPU use rises in both task-ID cohorts. These results measure task-dependent repair outcomes and agent-side costs with prepared checks.

</details>


### [229] [Predictive Credit: Measuring What Scientific Explanations Add to Experimental Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00314)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套测量"研究智能体解释性文本预测贡献"的协议，并通过配对预测设计在多个基准上检验解释的预测增益，最终未能确证自然解释在测试的捐赠者分辨率下具有预测信用，但发现机制性正面对照可降低点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究智能体在提出实验方案时通常附带解释性文本，但这类自然语言解释是否真正提升了预测性能（即"预测信用"）缺乏系统、可复现的测量手段。作者希望建立一套协议，通过控制干预、预测者与结局不变的配对预测，分离出"描述、匹配解释、捐赠者上下文"等文本成分各自的预测贡献。

Method: 设计配对预测框架：共享干预、预测者与结局，仅改变描述、匹配解释及捐赠者上下文。设置五项检查追踪承诺、交付、预测增益、对齐与已知信号吸收。在336个受控学习的前瞻状态、12个Tox21端点、24个OpenML任务上执行；Tox21预注册ROC AUC区间得分伤害检验、OpenML联合形成/点等价/可重复性规则作为主要判定；并引入机制性正面对照及DeepSeek不同版本（V4 Pro、V4 Flash）回放以考察模型敏感性。

Result: v5冻结信用判定不确证；Tox21预注册区间得分伤害检验未达标（D−M=−.0026，95%区间[−.0174,.0104]）；OpenML联合规则未满足；匹配点精度增益未被确认，Tox21/OpenML种子-捐赠者区间跨零。DeepSeek V4 Pro下匹配与捐赠者卡片使Tox21次级漂移分别降低64.5%和59.1%；V4 Flash回放使匹配点MAE从.01823升至.02020，未通过匹配-捐赠者区间得分等价性；OpenML全卡赋值使名义80%区间加宽21%，覆盖率49.3%（描述与内容为51.4%，占66/144卡）；直接文本Flash交付全部144条注释但无可检测的匹配点精度增益；研究者撰写的机制性正面对照将点MAE相对描述降低2.60个百分点。

Conclusion: 该协议可用于量化研究智能体基准与科学预测中的"预测信用"，但在所测试的捐赠者分辨率下，自然解释的信用仍未得到确证；机制性正面对照的显著增益提示，解释若要产生可测量的预测价值，可能需具备明确机制信息且分辨率足够。

Abstract: Research agents explain planned experiments. We measure predictive credit with paired forecasts sharing an intervention, forecaster, and outcome while varying description, matched explanation, and donor context. Five checks track commitment, delivery, predictive gain, alignment, and known-signal uptake. Across 336 prospective states in controlled learning, 12 Tox21 endpoints, and 24 OpenML tasks, v5's frozen credit decision was inconclusive. Tox21's preregistered ROC AUC interval-score harm test was unmet ($D-M=-.0026$, 95 percent interval [$-.0174$, .0104]); OpenML's joint formation, point-equivalence, and repeatability rule was unmet. Matched point-accuracy gains over description remained unconfirmed, and Tox21/OpenML seed-donor intervals spanned zero. Under requested DeepSeek V4 Pro, matched and donor cards reduced secondary Tox21 drift by 64.5 and 59.1 percent. A DeepSeek V4 Flash replay raised matched point MAE from .01823 to .02020 and missed matched-donor interval-score equivalence. OpenML full-card assignment widened nominal 80 percent intervals by 21 percent, with 49.3 percent coverage versus 51.4 percent for description and content in 66/144 cards. Direct-text Flash delivered all 144 notes without detectable matched point-accuracy gain. A researcher-authored mechanism positive control lowered point MAE by 2.60 percentage points versus description. The protocol measures predictive credit for research-agent benchmarks and scientific forecasting; natural-explanation credit remained unconfirmed at the tested donor resolutions.

</details>


### [230] [ContractRL: Shielded Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Auditable Tool-Call Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00328)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Yina Sa,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContractRL 提出一种受合约约束、由验证器引导的 JSON 顺序修复协议，通过动作掩码、不可变修复历史与预算约束，在语义成功率与生成 token 效率上均优于 Patch-SFT 和全文重新生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工具调用常因少数字段违反模式或执行合约而失败；重新生成完整对象会扩大动作面、难以审计多次修复。需要一种能局部、可追踪、受限地修复 JSON 违规的方法。

Method: 将验证器引导的 JSON 修复建模为有界决策过程。策略每一步观察候选对象、带类型的验证器反馈、JSON Pointer、不可变修复历史与剩余预算；由合约派生的动作掩码在执行前过滤畸形或被禁的 RFC-6902 操作，再由确定性验证器完成状态转移。采用合约约束的群组相对目标覆盖 patch、retry 与 abstention 决策，并在轨迹冻结前将规范目标与语义标签排除在在线状态之外。

Result: 在相同验证器信息下，ContractRL 达到 0.9362 语义成功率、34.4 生成 token（192 案例/种子 × 5 种子），优于 Patch-SFT 的 0.9076、44.9 token 和全文重新生成的 0.9148、137.2 token。策略优化将监督 ContractRL 的语义成功率从 0.9186 提升至 0.9375。三种子配对评估相对 Patch-SFT 语义差为 +0.0396（95% CI [+0.0137,+0.0662]，p=0.0039）。反馈、动作掩码、预算与模式偏移分析显示收益来自局部修正；对抗性与多轮评估刻画了残余失败模式。

Conclusion: ContractRL 通过合约约束的顺序修复与局部修正，在保持可审计性的同时显著提升语义成功率并降低 token 消耗，显著优于 Patch-SFT 和全文重新生成，并识别出仍需改进的失败模式。

Abstract: Structured tool calls often fail after only a small number of fields violate a schema or an execution contract. Regenerating the complete object enlarges the action surface and makes repeated repair difficult to audit. We introduce ContractRL, a contract-constrained sequential repair protocol that models verifier-guided JSON repair as a bounded decision process. At each step the policy observes the candidate, typed verifier feedback, JSON Pointer, immutable repair history, and remaining budget; a contract-derived action mask filters malformed or prohibited RFC-6902 operations before a deterministic validator performs the transition.
