### [264] [OR for AI That Does OR: Routing LLMs up the Escalator inside the OSCAR Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00912)
*Jinzhi Bu,Haixin Tang,Huanan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCAR 是一个由模拟器（Simulator）、编码器（Coder）和审查员（Reviewer）组成的框架，利用经过标注决策样例认证的离线模拟器来验证并持续搜寻更优的优化模型，以远低于闭源模型的成本实现接近满分的高准确率优化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能将业务描述转化为优化模型，但可执行代码可能误解约束或目标，导致求解器返回"错误问题"的最优解；即使解满足既定规则，也可能存在更优方案。对反复使用优化建模的组织而言，需要一套低成本、可本地部署、且能保证模型准确性并能持续改进解的框架。

Method: 提出 OSCAR 框架，通过一个用带标签决策样例认证的离线模拟器来比较候选方案，并在找到可行解后继续搜索更优解（超越可行性）。作者将"寻找下一个经认证的改进"建模为面临未观测难度的序贯决策问题（调用哪些 LLM、何时停止），在简化已知先验设定下给出成本递升策略最优的条件，并对一般菜单推导出先验无关的竞争性保证。

Result: 在五个基准问题上，OSCAR 使用两个可在单 GPU 上本地部署的小型开放权重 LLM，在报告设定下达到 95% 至 100% 的准确率，而这两个模型单次尝试的平均准确率分别仅为 29% 和 48%。在每个问题五次运行中，Codex 与 Claude Code 的平均 token 成本分别是 OSCAR 的 3.1 倍和 5.8 倍。

Conclusion: 企业应维护可行/不可行决策的标注示例，以明确自然语言运营规则；当 LLM 的解释与标注冲突时，OSCAR 以标注为准。OSCAR 支持开放权重模型在本地或云端运行，灵活适配预算与保密需求，其简单运营规则和可调设定使企业能随 LLM 能力与价格变化灵活调整模型选择并获益。

Abstract: Large language models can translate business descriptions into optimization models, but executable code may misrepresent constraints or objectives. A solver can then return an optimal solution to the wrong problem. Even when the solution satisfies the intended operating rules, a better plan may exist. For organizations that repeatedly use optimization modeling, an LLM-based framework should produce accurate formulations at low cost and, ideally, run locally. We study how to verify improvements and allocate attempts across LLMs that differ in price and capability. We develop OSCAR (Optimization modeling by Simulator, Coder, And Reviewer), which uses an offline Simulator certified against labeled decision examples to compare candidates and continues searching beyond feasibility. We model the search for the next certified improvement as sequential decisions under unobserved difficulty: which LLMs to call and when to stop. In a simplified known-prior setting, we give conditions under which cost-ordered escalation is optimal. For general menus, we derive a prior-free competitive guarantee. On five benchmark problems, OSCAR achieves 95% to 100% accuracy at the reported settings using two small open-weight LLMs, each deployable locally on a single GPU. Their single-attempt accuracies average 29% and 48%. In five runs per problem, Codex and Claude Code incur average token costs 3.1 and 5.8 times OSCAR's, respectively. OSCAR supports open-weight models locally or in the cloud, depending on budget and confidentiality requirements. Firms should maintain labeled decision examples of feasible and infeasible decisions to clarify plain-language operating rules. OSCAR follows these labels when an LLM's interpretation conflicts with them. As LLM capabilities and prices change, OSCAR's simple operating rules and adjustable settings help firms adapt their model choices and benefit from these advances.

</details>


### [265] [Finding the Right Fit: Model-Harness Interactions across Agent Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00917)
*Yixuan Li,Yiyun Zhou,Yao Long Teng,Fuchao Yang,Yanchen Deng,Zhiyi Lyu,Xuyu Dong,Feng Chen,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了语言模型与智能体脚手架（harness）配对在不同任务环境下的表现，发现"强模型/强脚手架"并非稳定属性，模型排名会随脚手架切换而反转，主张应将模型、脚手架与任务三者联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估往往孤立地考察模型或脚手架，忽略了二者配对与任务环境之间的交互作用。作者质疑"强模型、强脚手架或强配对在设置改变后是否仍保持强大"，并通过跨脚手架、跨基准的系统实验检验这一假设。

Method: 构建66种配置：四个可配置脚手架（OpenHands、DeepSeek Harness、PI、openJiuwen）分别与五个模型配对，在TUA-Bench、ALE-CLI、Terminal-Bench 4三个基准上评估，另加入原生Codex-GPT与Claude Code-Claude配对。采用跨脚手架模型排名对比、配对稳定性分析、成本-得分分析以及对6204条评分的轨迹进行匹配分析，探究"适配度"差异的成因。

Result: 模型排名在脚手架间发生反转：Terminal-Bench 4上Claude在OpenHands中领先GPT 7.94分，但在PI中落后GPT 30.16分。五个模型中有四个的最优脚手架随基准变化，但存在稳定配对：openJiuwen使Kimi在全部三个基准上得分最高，领先5.61至11.11分。模型自家供应商的脚手架并非可靠最优，高成本也不可靠地带来更高得分；GPT在PI下得分高于DSH，而每任务成本不足DSH的四分之一。轨迹分析显示模型几乎自主发起所有修复，故脚手架能否以模型可用的形式回传失败信息至关重要：GPT适配PI的精简脚手架，而常产生格式错误工具调用的Kimi在openJiuwen中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 模型、脚手架与任务应被联合评估，而非孤立考察。作者开源了脚手架适配器、评估代码及全部6204条已评分轨迹，以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Choosing an agent system means choosing both a language model and the harness through which it acts. We ask whether a strong model, harness, or pairing stays strong when the setting changes. We evaluate 66 configurations: four configurable harnesses (OpenHands, DeepSeek Harness, PI, and openJiuwen) paired with five models on TUA-Bench, ALE-CLI, and Terminal-Bench 4, plus the native Codex-GPT and Claude Code-Claude pairings. Model rankings reverse across harnesses. On Terminal-Bench 4, Claude leads GPT by 7.94 points in OpenHands but trails it by 30.16 points in PI. For four of the five models, the best harness changes from one benchmark to another, yet some pairings hold: openJiuwen gives Kimi its highest score on all three benchmarks, by 5.61 to 11.11 points. A model's own vendor harness is not reliably its best, and higher cost does not reliably buy a higher score. On Terminal-Bench 4, GPT scores higher under PI than under DSH at less than a quarter of the cost per task. Matched trajectories suggest why fit varies. Models start almost all repairs themselves, so much depends on whether the harness hands failures back in a form the model can use. GPT does best with PI's lean scaffold, while Kimi, which often issues malformed tool calls, does best in openJiuwen. We argue that the model, the harness, and the task should be evaluated together, and we release the harness adapters, evaluation code, and all 6,204 scored trajectories at https://github.com/liyix/finding-the-right-fit and https://huggingface.co/datasets/yixuanli97/finding-the-right-fit.

</details>


### [266] [ABDA-NL: A Natural-Language Scenario Explorer for Argument-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00947)
*Shawn Bowers,Martin Caminada,Haoyang Liu,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABDA-NL 为基于论辩讨论的系统 ABDA 增加了一个自然语言接口：用户用自然语言查看结论状态、探究推理原因、探索反事实假设、提问并获得基于参考文档的回答，以及编辑事实/假设/规则；大型语言模型负责语言与形式化之间的桥接，而确定性的 ABDA 引擎仍保持论辩、攻击与接受标签的唯一来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论辩系统 ABDA 基于 ASPIC- 知识库和稳固语义，虽能给出严谨的结论接受/拒绝/未决状态，但其形式化交互门槛较高，普通用户难以理解推理过程、探索替代方案或以自然语言维护知识库。论文旨在通过引入自然语言接口和大型语言模型，降低使用门槛，同时不牺牲系统的确定性与严谨性。

Method: 在 ABDA 之上构建自然语言层：用户可查看各结论的接受、拒绝或未决标签；可打开稳固讨论博弈（grounded discussion game）的交互式呈现以理解原因；通过暂停假设、规则或改变偏好来探索"若何"（what-if）替代方案；针对场景参考文档提问并获得回答；并用自然语言撰写新的事实、假设和规则。大型语言模型负责桥接语言与形式化——既能根据文档和当前场景状态回答问题，也能把自然语言编辑翻译为候选形式化语句。确定性的 ABDA 引擎依然是论辩、攻击和接受标签的唯一来源，模型的每条建议都须经用户验证确认后才生效。

Result: 提供了一个兼具可用性与严谨性的自然语言论辩讨论系统 ABDA-NL：用户以自然语言完成查询、解释、反事实探索和知识库编辑；所有论辩语义结果仍由确定性引擎保证，大型语言模型仅作为翻译与问答的辅助，并通过用户确认机制防止错误或不一致的建议进入系统。

Conclusion: 通过在确定性论辩引擎与大型语言模型之间建立受控的自然语言桥接，ABDA-NL 显著提升了 ABDA 的可访问性和可维护性，同时确保论证、攻击关系和接受标签的唯一权威来源仍是确定性引擎，并以用户验证为安全闸门，兼顾了易用性与可靠性。

Abstract: ABDA-NL adds a natural-language interface to ABDA, a system for argument-based discussion using ASPIC- knowledge bases under grounded semantics. Users see which conclusions are accepted, rejected, or undecided, open an interactive rendering of the grounded discussion game to learn why, explore what-if alternatives by suspending assumptions and rules or changing preferences, ask questions that are answered from a scenario's reference documents, and author new facts, assumptions, and rules in plain English. A large language model provides the bridge between language and formalism: it answers questions from the documents and the current state of the scenario, and it translates plain-English edits into candidate formal statements. The deterministic ABDA engine remains the sole source of arguments, attacks, and acceptance labels, and every proposal of the model is validated and confirmed by the user before it takes effect.

</details>


### [267] [PG-SFT: Balancing Capability Acquisition and Retention in Offline Agent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00949)
*Ronghua Li,Zi Liang,Zhishan Li,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)，利用智能体轨迹的轮次级信息增益作为指标来调整监督强度，从而在获取新工具使用能力与保留基础模型既有能力之间取得更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的监督微调（SFT）虽能提升目标基准表现，但会损害基础模型的其他能力（如通用推理、工具调用、代码生成）。作者发现仅用 KL 惩罚或限制更新幅度等约束分布漂移的方法并不能阻止这种能力退化，因此希望研究如何更好地平衡新能力获取与旧能力保留之间的权衡。

Method: 提出 Privilege-Guided SFT（PG-SFT），利用智能体轨迹的轮次级（turn-level）信息增益作为指示器来调节监督强度，即在信息增益高的位置施加更强监督、在信息增益低的位置弱化监督，从而有选择地偏离基础行为，而非逐 token 等强度地模仿推理和动作。

Result: 在评估基准上，PG-SFT 显著降低了分布漂移与广泛的能力退化，仅以目标任务的轻微性能下降为代价，取得了相比标准 SFT 及 KL 惩罚/限制更新幅度等基线更有利的权衡。

Conclusion: 获取—保留权衡不仅取决于模型是否锚定到其基础行为，还取决于监督应在何处、以多大强度偏离该行为；PG-SFT 通过轮次级信息增益自适应调节监督强度，为训练专用工具使用智能体时兼顾能力获取与能力保留提供了有效思路。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on offline agent trajectories is the standard approach for training specialized tool-using agents, but forcing models to imitate reasoning and actions token by token may harm other capabilities (e.g., general reasoning, tool calling, code generation) of the base model. In this work, we focus on studying \emph{how to better balance the trade-off between acquiring new capabilities and preserving existing ones during agent trace SFT}. By comparing several baselines in our setup, standard SFT improves the target benchmark while lowering several non-target benchmark scores; meanwhile, simply constraining distributional drift using KL penalty or limiting the update magnitude did not avoid this regression trend. Motivated by recent token-wise adaptive learning objectives, this work proposes \textbf{Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)} to leverage turn-level information gain of agent trajectories as an indicator to adjust supervision strength. PG-SFT yields a more favorable observed trade-off on the evaluated benchmarks, substantially reducing distributional drift and broad capability degradation at the cost of slight degradation in target-task performance. Our findings suggest that balancing the acquisition--retention trade-off depends not only on whether the model is anchored to its base behavior, but also on where and how strongly supervision should depart from that behavior.}

</details>


### [268] [Cybernetic and Epistemic: A Missing Vocabulary for Trustworthy Agentic Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00961)
*Jérémie Lumbroso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，当代码生成交由AI系统后，瓶颈从"写代码"转向"监督写代码的系统"，并暴露出一个词汇缺口："人类监督"未能区分语言在委托渠道中的两种功能——协调行动（控制论式）与协调理解（认知式）。其核心论点是：监督失败源于"认知形式语言在做控制论工作"，即仅为获得批准而校准、而非为真而校准的解释。文章提出一个治理准则（每个后果性决策须附带"本可不同的条件"并以第三方可检验的形式表达），给出可操作的"两步重构测试"评分法，以及名为ORRCF的审议记录约定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成日益委托给AI系统，监督这些系统成为关键瓶颈，而现有CS教育研究用"人类监督"一词却无法区分语言的两类功能（协调行动 vs 协调理解）。于是"橡皮图章式"批准可以满足只核查"是否被批准"的监督，却无法让代理为其推理负责。作者希望建立一种可审计、可核查的监督标准，使监督真正能约束代理而非流于表面。

Method: 采用概念区分与案例研究相结合的方法：(1) 借用网络安全/控制论与认识论的区分，界定"控制论语言"（词与世界相符即成功）与"认知/认知形式语言"（词须应答世界且可被核查）两类功能；(2) 呈现三个委托实例（说明性而非对照证据），证明认知式参与既可行又可审计；(3) 一个公开记录案例展示按自身陈述撤回建议；(4) 一个失败案例展示仅凭裁量、无需理由的监督；(5) 提出操作性准则并形式化为"两步重构测试"（考虑扰动下第二读者能否预测代理行为来给委托记录评分），以及使条件成为每次记录决策必需组件的ORRCF记录约定。

Result: 三个委托实例表明认知式参与既切实可行又保持可审计性；一个公开记录显示建议得以按其自身陈述被撤回；一个失败案例暴露了无需为裁量选择提供理由的监督缺陷。基于此提出治理准则：每个后果性选择都应附带"在何种条件下本会作出不同选择"，且以第三方可检验的形式给出；否则第三方无法区分真实决策与橡皮图章。两步重构测试与ORRCF约定使该准则可操作。

Conclusion: 作者主张，负责任的AI代理治理不能仅依赖技术性信任属性，还必须在组织/语言层面要求每个后果性决策附带可检验的反事实条件。通过两步重构测试和ORRCF记录约定，可将"人类监督"从橡皮图章式的批准升级为可问责、可审计的认知式监督，从而弥合CS教育领域中的词汇缺口。

Abstract: As code generation is increasingly delegated to AI systems, the bottleneck is shifting from writing code to supervising the systems that write it --- a shift CS-education researchers have begun to name. This shift exposes a vocabulary gap: the field asks for "human oversight" without a working distinction between the two things language does in a delegation channel --- coordinate action (cybernetic: words succeed when the world comes to match them) and coordinate understanding (epistemic: they succeed when they answer to the world and a hearer can check that they do). The failure this names is not cybernetic language but epistemic-form language doing cybernetic work: explanation-shaped output calibrated for approval rather than truth. Oversight that checks only whether an output was approved is satisfiable by rubber-stamping; oversight that holds an agent accountable requires the reasoning behind its work be retrievable and checkable. We present three delegation episodes --- illustrations, not controlled evidence --- in which epistemic engagement proved practicable while remaining auditable, one public record where a recommendation was withdrawn on its own stated terms, and one failure case illustrating oversight that requires no reasons for its discretionary choices. We propose a criterion for agentic-system governance, alongside existing technical trust properties: every consequential choice should carry the condition under which it would have gone otherwise, in a form a third party can test. Without such a condition, a third party cannot distinguish a decision from a rubber stamp. We give the criterion an operational form --- a two-part reconstruction test scoring a delegation record by whether a second reader can predict what the agent does under a perturbation --- and a deliberation-recording convention, ORRCF, that makes the condition a required component of every recorded choice.

</details>


### [269] [VeriHarness: Scaling Agentic Verification for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00972)
*Caiqi Zhang,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Zoey CuiZhu,Nigel Collier,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 VeriHarness，将生成器所用的基础 LLM 转化为智能体式验证器，通过分歧解决与共识挑战两大机制实现无参考答案条件下的长程任务验证，在五个基准上取得最优选择分数，并经证据支持修订带来显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体执行的长程任务日益复杂，验证其输出愈发困难；而在测试时无法获得参考答案或评分标准，且基础模型固定、不可升级。重复采样能产生多个包含互补正确论断的 rollout，但缺乏可靠机制来判断该信任哪些论断——研究发现分歧常暴露正确替代方案，而共识会掩盖错误，因此需要一个能借助环境证据进行验证的机制。

Method: VeriHarness 将生成器所用的底层 LLM 转化为智能体式验证器，为其配备工作区、证据工具与可复用的验证技能。其核心包含两个模块：(1) 分歧解决器（disagreement resolver），将竞争性论断与环境证据对照核查；(2) 共识挑战器（consensus challenger），检验共享论断并搜索被遗漏的需求。两者得出的发现用于指导最终产物的选择与修订；验证技能还可以从失败反馈中自我改进。

Result: 在五个长程工作区基准和两个前沿模型上，VeriHarness 在所有评估基线中取得最高选择分数；经证据支持的修订进一步提升了平均性能，相较单次 rollout 的增益在 Gemini 3.5 Flash 上达 6.2 分、在 Claude Opus 4.8 上达 6.4 分。此外验证技能能借助失败反馈自我改进。

Conclusion: VeriHarness 是一种新颖且关键的扩展长程智能体验证的方法，通过将基础 LLM 转化为带环境证据的智能体验证器（而非依赖外部参考答案）有效提升验证能力，并支持验证技能的自我改进。作者公开了约 26,000 条 rollout（总成本超 10 万美元），以支持未来的智能体验证研究。

Abstract: As LLM agents undertake increasingly complex, long-horizon tasks, verifying their outputs becomes increasingly challenging. We study how verification capability can be strengthened with a fixed base model, without access to reference answers or grading rubrics at test time. Repeated sampling yields multiple rollouts that can contain complementary correct claims, but we need a reliable verification mechanism to determine which claims to trust. We first find that disagreement often exposes correct alternatives, while consensus can conceal errors. These observations motivate VeriHarness, which turns the underlying LLM a generator uses into an agentic verifier by giving it a workspace, evidence tools, and reusable verification skills. A disagreement resolver checks competing claims against environmental evidence, while a consensus challenger tests shared claims and searches for omitted requirements. Their findings guide the selection and revision of the final artifact. Across five long-horizon workspace benchmarks and two frontier models, VeriHarness achieves the highest selection scores among the evaluated baselines. Evidence-backed revision further improves average performance, bringing gains over a single rollout to 6.2 points with Gemini 3.5 Flash and 6.4 points with Claude Opus 4.8. We further show that verification skills can self-improve from failure feedback, demonstrating VeriHarness as a novel and critical approach for scaling long-horizon agentic verification. We release the full pool of approximately 26,000 rollouts across all five benchmarks and both models, produced at a cost of over $100,000, to support future research on agentic verification.

</details>


### [270] [RISED: RubrIcs for agentic multi-environment Selection and sElf-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00979)
*Jingtan Wang,Sirajul Salekin,Young mok Jung,Javier Movellan,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RISED，一种在多样交互环境中训练通用 LLM 智能体的方法。它利用跨环境共享的评测量规（rubrics）标签来刻画每个 rollout 的行为画像，进而引导在线数据选择与策略监督（正向量规用于自蒸馏教师、负向量规用于引导后续生成），在多个模型骨干上取得了最高平均通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有课程学习与数据选择策略往往在“环境层面”分配训练，或仅基于局部标量奖励信号，未考虑批次内跨环境 rollout 之间的关系；同时由于不同环境学习速度不一致，同一批次中可能出现“全失败”与“全成功”的 rollout 组并存，导致这些数据缺乏组内相对奖励信号。两者共同暴露了仅依赖标量奖励的局限性：无法提供跨环境关系信息，也无法在奖励相同时提供组内对比。这促使引入更丰富的文本反馈（如描述 rollout 行为的量规）来指导学习。

Method: 核心做法是超越于量规仅作为奖励的用法，将其重新用于双重目的：(1) 用一个 LLM 裁判基于跨环境共享的预定义量规词表为每个 rollout 打标签，形成行为画像；该画像用于在线数据选择，使所选数据与混合环境批次的整体行为构成对齐，同时限制与已选数据的重叠。(2) 将正向量规（描述期望行为）作为偏好上下文提供给一个在线策略自蒸馏教师，提供 token 级监督；负向量规（描述不期望行为）引导后续 rollout 生成，避免重复失败模式。这些组件共同构成 RISED。

Result: 在多个模型骨干上，RISED 在所有环境中取得了最高的平均通过率，并且在每个单独环境中均排名第一或第二。基于量规的分析还进一步刻画了这些性能提升背后的行为变化。

Conclusion: RISED 通过以量规为媒介整合跨环境关系信息与组内行为对比，弥补了纯标量奖励在多环境强化学习中的信息不足，实现了对在线数据选择与策略监督的双重引导，并在通用 LLM 智能体训练中取得一致且领先的性能。

Abstract: Training a single LLM agent jointly across diverse interactive environments has attracted increasing attention as a route to generalist agents. Existing curriculum and data-selection strategies often allocate training at the environment level or prioritize local reward-based signals, without explicitly considering relationships between current rollouts across environments for prompt-group selection. Meanwhile, as environments are learned at different rates, all-failure and all-success rollout groups can coexist within a batch, leaving those data without group-relative reward signals. Both challenges highlight limitations of relying solely on scalar rewards in multi-environment RL: they provide limited information about cross-environment relationships and no within-group reward contrast when rewards are identical. This motivates richer textual feedback, such as rubrics describing rollout behaviours, to guide learning. Beyond rubrics' usage as reward, we repurpose rubrics to guide both online data selection and policy supervision. An LLM judge tags each rollout using a predefined rubric vocabulary shared across environments. The resulting profiles guide the selection of data that aligns with the overall behavioural composition of the mixed-environment batch while limiting overlap with already-selected data. Available positive rubrics (describing desired behaviours) provide privileged context for an on-policy self-distillation teacher, supplying additional token-level supervision, while negative rubrics (describing undesired behaviours) guide subsequent rollout generation away from recurring failure modes. Together, these components form RISED. Across model backbones, RISED achieves the highest mean pass rate across environments and ranks first or second in every individual environment. Rubric-based analysis of RISED can further characterize the behavioural changes accompanying these gains.

</details>


### [271] [Evaluating LLM-Generated Preference Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01000)
*Fan Huang,Minsuk Kim,C. Tyler Diggans,Filippo Radicchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了 LLM 作为概率生成器在航空出行、餐厅、消费品三类偏好分布上的表现，发现各模型自身采样自洽且最可能结果稳定，但跨模型家族与规模之间分歧巨大，且模型选择比提示词措辞影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 日益被用作模拟、合成数据生成和决策支持的概率生成器，但其所产生分布的结构与可靠性尚未被充分研究；同时普遍存在一种假设，认为能力足够的 LLM 在替代调查受访者时会产生相似的偏好分布。作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 系统分析九种开源权重模型在三个选择领域（航空出行、餐厅、消费品）生成的偏好分布；考察分布的自我一致性、最可能结果在重复采样下的稳定性，以及跨模型家族与规模的一致性；并通过温度变化、贪心解码、提示词与顺序扰动进行稳健性检验。

Result: 所有模型均表现出自我一致性，最可能结果在重复采样下迅速稳定；但跨模型家族和规模存在显著分歧，即使是最可能的结果之间也几乎没有共识；这些模式在九种模型、三个领域及温度、解码策略、提示词与顺序扰动下均保持稳健；结果受模型选择的影响大于受提示词措辞的影响。

Conclusion: LLM 生成的偏好分布主要取决于所选的模型而非提示词表述，这挑战了"能力足够的 LLM 在替代调查受访者时会产生相似偏好分布"的常见假设；将 LLM 用作模拟或调查代理时应认识到其分布对模型选择的强敏感性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as probabilistic generators for simulation, synthetic data generation, and decision support in settings where real-world data are unavailable. Yet, the structure and reliability of the distributions they produce remain understudied. Here, we systematically analyze LLM-generated distributions of preferences for air travel, restaurants, and consumer products. Encouragingly, all models considered in our analysis exhibit self-coherence, with the most probable outcomes stabilizing rapidly under repeated sampling. At the same time, we observe substantial discordance across both model families and scales, with little consensus even among their most probable outcomes. These patterns hold across nine open-weight models, three choice domains, and show robustness under temperature changes, greedy decoding, and perturbations of prompt and ordering. Our findings indicate that outcomes are influenced more by the choice of model than by the wording of the prompt, challenging the common assumption that sufficiently capable LLMs produce similar preference distributions when used as stand-ins for survey respondents.

</details>


### [272] [Calibration-risk routing for controlled world-model adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01001)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Model-Corrected World Model (MC-WM)，通过将初始目标数据划分为不相交的拟合、选择与校准分区，选择标准化校准风险更低的模型族，并利用学习到的置信信号与确定性有效性谓词来加权单步想象策略更新，从而缓解基于模型的强化学习中模拟器到目标的偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习虽可利用模拟经验，但模拟器到目标域的偏移会引发模型选择难题：在目标数据有限时，仅修正模拟器或仅直接拟合目标模型都可能失败。需要一种能稳健选择并融合模型的机制。

Method: 提出 Model-Corrected World Model (MC-WM)：将初始目标数据划分为不相交的拟合、选择与校准三个分区；部署标准化校准风险更低的模型族；利用学习到的置信信号和确定性有效性谓词对单步想象策略更新进行加权，而无需改写环境固有的物理奖励。

Result: 在三个受控的 MuJoCo（Multi-Joint dynamics with Contact）偏移场景下，共评估了 540 个唯一上报的运行单元；其中一个精确路由单元在部署前伪影门控后被重复执行一次，最终完成 541 次执行。

Conclusion: MC-WM 通过数据分区、标准化校准风险选择和置信加权想象更新，为模拟器到目标偏移下的模型选择提供了一种稳健方案，并在受控 MuJoCo 偏移场景中完成了大规模评估。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) can exploit simulated experience, but a simulator-to-target shift creates a model-selection problem: correcting the simulator and fitting the target directly can each fail under limited target data. We introduce the Model-Corrected World Model (MC-WM), which separates initial target data into disjoint fit, selection, and calibration partitions and deploys the family with lower standardized calibration risk. A learned confidence signal and deterministic validity predicates weight one-step imagined policy updates without rewriting physical rewards. We evaluate 540 unique reported run cells across three controlled Multi-Joint dynamics with Contact (MuJoCo) shifts; one exact-routing cell was repeated after a pre-deployment artifact gate, giving 541 completed executions.

</details>


### [273] [What Can Analogy Tell Us About Artificial Consciousness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01002)
*Keith J. Holyoak,Martin M. Monti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估"其他实体是否有意识"的因果框架，强调应区分"与意识生成相关的因果相似性"与"下游行为/认知效应的相似性"，并用它解释为何对AI意识的类比证据目前仍很薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意识只能通过第一人称直接体验，因此判断其他实体（包括动物和AI）是否有意识只能依赖类比推理。现有类比判断缺乏系统方法，且在AI快速发展的背景下迫切需要更严谨的评估框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个因果框架来评估证据类比：核心区分在于"可能参与意识生成的因素的相似性"与"下游行为或认知效应的相似性"。该框架以因果相关性为权重来衡量源—目标相似性，同时容纳未知原因、抑制性差异以及通向意识的替代路径。

Result: 将该框架应用于生物系统时，它解释了为何类比支持通常随与人类的因果距离增大而减弱；应用于当代AI时，它表明行为相似性仅为意识提供有限证据，因为相关的因果对应关系仍未充分确立。

Conclusion: 该框架厘清了何种证据能够增强人工意识的主张：需要建立与意识生成机制相关的因果对应，而非仅依赖行为或认知层面的相似性。

Abstract: Who or what is conscious? Because subjective experience is directly accessible only in the first person, judgments about consciousness in other entities depend partly on analogy. Historically, such inferences have focused on nonhuman animals, but advances in artificial intelligence have raised the possibility of conscious AI. Here we develop a causal framework for evaluating such evidential analogies. The key distinction is between similarities in factors plausibly involved in generating consciousness and similarities in downstream behavioural or cognitive effects. Our framework weights source-target similarity by causal relevance while allowing for unknown causes, disabling differences and alternative routes to consciousness. Applied to biological systems, it explains why analogical support generally weakens with increasing causal distance from humans. Applied to contemporary AI, it suggests that behavioural similarity provides only limited evidence for consciousness because relevant causal correspondences remain poorly established. The framework also clarifies what evidence would strengthen claims of artificial consciousness.

</details>


### [274] [Beyond Answer Confidence: A Controlled Audit of Self-Knowledge in a Black-Box Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01006)
*Sharath M Shankaranarayana,Davor Runje,Jan Jannink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了决策模型 Jev 的置信度是否反映"缺失知识"而非仅仅统计校准，发现其在熟悉任务上校准良好，但无法指示知识缺失；针对性的是/否问题能更清晰地读取出证据是否充分与结果是否已定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准只保证概率在平均意义上可用，却无法区分低置信度是源于随机性还是缺乏知识，也无法判断模型是否在超出已知范围时降低置信度。作者希望区分并审计这一关键差异。

Method: 在决策模型 Jev 上，使用 15+ 个公开数据集和 6 个生成任务族，采用配对干预（对同一题目改变所提供的信息量）进行审计；并引入针对性的是/否问题（结果是否已定、证据是否充分、模型是否知道答案）作为读出手段，同时用去除日期或使用真实姓名等对照来控制表面线索。

Result: Jev 在熟悉封闭选择题上置信度校准良好，但无法指示缺失知识：无答题相关信息时仍会给显著选项高达 0.80 的置信度；对超出知识边界后的新闻，置信度超过准确率 0.21–0.33，且用早期月份重校准无法弥合。是/否问题读出更敏锐：判断结果是否已定 AUROC=1.00，证据是否充分 AUROC=0.95（同题置信度仅 0.85）。"是否知道答案"可标记虚构实体和边界后新闻（0.91），但加上真实姓名或去掉日期后失去优势。

Conclusion: 黑盒知识审计需要为表面线索设置显式对照；单纯依靠答案置信度不足以判断模型是否知道自己不知道，针对性的元认知式提问在受控条件下能提供更可靠的缺失知识信号。

Abstract: Decision models return probabilities intended for routing, abstention and automated action. Calibration makes those probabilities useful on average, but does not establish whether low confidence reflects chance or missing knowledge, nor whether confidence falls when a model moves beyond what it knows. We audit this distinction in Jev, a decision model, with over 15 public datasets and 6 generated task families, with paired interventions that vary the information supplied for a fixed item. Jev's confidence is calibrated on familiar closed-choice tasks but fails as an indicator of missing knowledge: with no answer-relevant information it assigns up to 0.80 to a salient option, and on news beyond an observed knowledge boundary it exceeds accuracy by 0.21--0.33, a gap that recalibration on earlier months does not close. Targeted yes/no questions give sharper readouts of the case: whether an outcome is settled (AUROC 1.00) and whether the evidence suffices (0.95, against 0.85 for confidence on the same items). Asking whether Jev knows the answer appears to flag fabricated entities and post-boundary news (0.91), but with realistic names or with dates removed it shows no advantage over answer uncertainty. Black-box knowledge audits therefore need explicit controls for surface cues. Code: https://github.com/Syntheme/beyond-answer-confidence.

</details>


### [275] [From Discovery to Decision: Finite-Budget Recoverability in LLM Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01014)
*Shaoang Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多LLM响应投票中的"发现-决策"差距：在固定调用预算下，候选答案被发现后还需积累足够票数成为最终多数。作者刻画了可达性阈值、转换窗口、锁定证书等，并证明合并错误答案身份无法提升多数准确率，输入置换可大幅提升准确率，精确锁定可节省28-30%调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投票聚合多个LLM响应是测试时扩展的常见方法，但在固定调用预算下，一个正确答案即使被发现，也未必能获得足够票数成为最终获胜者，形成"发现到决策"的差距。现有工作未充分刻画这一差距及如何高效利用预算。

Method: 作者通过"已实现投票状态"和"剩余调用预算"刻画差距；推导尖锐的可恢复性阈值；证明随采样进行，候选集只会扩张而可达终点获胜者集合只会收缩，形成候选级转换窗口；在给定iid响应律下给出精确有限期终点概率；证明合并错误答案身份保持单次正确性且不能提升多数准确率；利用单例可达性构建无金标准的精确锁定证书，对已知答案全集给出首次触发时刻（最早前缀）；在Word16实验中进行输入置换并评估精确锁定效果。

Result: 理论证明合并错误答案身份不能改善多数准确率，错误概率重分配的影响取决于当前投票状态；单例可达性给出精确锁定证书。实证表明多数被发现但未被选中的正确答案仅在发现后才失去可达性；Word16受控实验中输入置换将原始多数准确率提升21.1个百分点而单次正确性基本不变；在16次调用预算下精确锁定节省28-30%的调用且保留所有固定预算输出。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了LLM投票中的发现-决策差距，提出了可恢复性阈值和转换窗口等理论框架，并通过精确锁定证书和输入置换等方法在几乎不损失单次正确性的前提下显著提升多数准确率并节省调用预算。

Abstract: Voting over multiple LLM responses is a common primitive in test-time scaling and ensemble inference. Collecting more responses can expand the candidate pool and increase the chance that a correct answer is discovered. Under a fixed call budget, a discovered answer still needs to accumulate enough support within the remaining calls to become the final plurality winner, creating a discovery-to-decision gap. In this work, we characterize this gap through the realized vote state and remaining call budget. We derive a sharp recoverability threshold and show that, as sampling proceeds, the observed candidate set can only expand while the set of reachable endpoint winners can only contract, inducing a candidate-level conversion window. Under a specified iid response law, the same state yields exact finite-horizon endpoint probabilities. We further show that merging wrong-answer identities preserves single-call correctness and cannot improve plurality accuracy, and that the effect of redistributing wrong-answer probability depends on the realized vote state. Singleton reachability yields a gold-free exact locking certificate. For a known answer universe, its first trigger is the earliest prefix at which all admissible continuations yield the same fixed-budget output. Empirically, most discovered-but-unselected correct answers lose reachability only after discovery. In a controlled Word16 study, input permutation improves raw-plurality accuracy by 21.1 points with essentially unchanged single-call correctness. Exact locking saves 28-30% of calls at a 16-call budget while preserving every fixed-budget output.

</details>


### [276] [Pay for the Fault, Not the Flow: Label-Free In-Flow Multi-Agent Workflow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01017)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Shengyu Chen,Zach Chen,Wei Cheng,Qingyun Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InFlowOp，一种基于统一无标签成本函数的多智能体工作流构建与运行时修正方法，并配套构建 Braid 基准来评测需要多智能体协调的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体工作流在构建阶段面临任务划分粒度、智能体分配、是否新建专家等关键决策难题，且各子任务是否成功需等工作流运行后才可知；工作流改进成本高昂，故障定位依赖参考答案、评分结果或训练好的评估器，修正往往要重执行、重搜索或重训练。作者希望以一个无需标签的统一成本，贯穿工作流的构建与运行，解决上述问题。

Method: InFlowOp 将每个决策定价为单一的无标签成本，该成本权衡"智能体能力与子任务需求的匹配程度"与"智能体运行开销"。执行前，依据成本而非固定模板双向确定任务分解粒度与智能体分配；执行中，利用同一成本选择最便宜的修正动作来纠正故障，使同一成本同时服务于工作流的构建与运行。为评估工作流级能力，作者提出 Braid 基准，其任务需要超越单智能体能力的多智能体协调。

Result: 在多个领域与不同骨干模型上，InFlowOp 相比单智能体基线最高提升 +11.97%，并结合流内优化（in-flow optimization）取得 +9.64% 的提升。

Conclusion: InFlowOp 通过统一的标签无关成本有效连接了工作流的构建与运行阶段，在复杂多智能体任务上显著优于单智能体方法；Braid 基准为工作流级评估提供了新平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly construct multi-agent workflows that decompose a complex task and assign specialist agents from a pool. However, building such a workflow well remains challenging: how finely to divide the task, which agent to trust with each subtask, and when to create a new specialist are all critical decisions a workflow constructor needs to settle up front. Thus, whether each subtask succeeds remains unknown until the workflow runs. Yet, improving a workflow is costly. Locating a fault usually requires a reference answer, a graded outcome, or a trained assessor, and the fix is applied to the whole workflow through re-execution, re-search, or retraining. We propose InFlowOp, which prices every decision in one label-free cost that weighs how well an agent's competence meets what a subtask demands against how much that agent takes to run. Before execution, InFlowOp bidirectionally determines the granularity of task decomposition and agent assignment following from the cost rather than from a fixed template. During execution, InFlowOp corrects a fault with the cheapest move via the same cost that serves the workflow both as it is built and as it runs. Facing the workflow-level evaluation challenge, we introduce Braid, a benchmark whose tasks require multi-agent coordination beyond single-agent capability. Across various domains and backbones, InFlowOp outperforms single agent baselines by up to $+11.97\%$, achieving $+9.64\%$ with in-flow optimization. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/InFlowOp/.

</details>


### [277] [Beyond Final Accuracy: Auditing Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01042)
*Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Peiyu Zhang,Anzhe Cheng,Heng Ping,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ICR（独立推理—通信—修订）框架，在固定初始推理轨迹并设置无消息修订对照的条件下，将多智能体通信评估为“独立推理之后的答案修订”，从而把通信收益与额外推理收益分离，并区分纠错与破坏两类影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体通信方法通常在为其设计的系统内评估，导致系统性能提升无法归因：难以区分是有效通信、良好的智能体架构，还是单纯增加推理计算所致；同时最终准确率把“纠错”和“腐坏”混为单一结果，掩盖了通信如何改变决策。

Method: 提出 Independent–Communicate–Revise (ICR) 控制框架：智能体先独立推理得到答案，再通过消息通信并修订答案。框架固定初始推理轨迹，基于两位智能体初始正确性衡量“纠正（correction）”与“保持（preservation）”，并以“无消息修订”作为对照基线，量化超越额外推理的增益；并比较接收方策略（如结构化验证策略）对不同信道的影响。

Result: 在四个推理基准上的审计表明：相似的总体准确率可能掩盖截然不同的修订行为；与仅传输答案相比，完整推理在所有四个基准上同时提高了纠正率、降低了保持率，说明更丰富的消息会同步放大有益与有害影响；在 MedQA 与 GPQA-D 上的接收方策略比较显示，结构化验证策略使各信道普遍转向更高保持、更低纠正，但对选择性的影响因信道与任务而异。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了将通信质量视为信道固有属性的观点，主张以“选择性修订”为中心重新定位评估，提出 ICR 作为统一框架，用于考察消息内容与接收方策略如何共同产生收益与危害。

Abstract: Multi-agent communication aims to help agents benefit from one another's information. Yet improvements in system performance leave a fundamental ambiguity: do they reflect effective communication, a favorable agent architecture, or simply additional reasoning? Because communication methods are commonly evaluated within the systems they were designed for, these factors are difficult to disentangle. Final accuracy further merges corrected errors and corrupted answers into a single outcome, obscuring how communication changes decisions. We introduce Independent--Communicate--Revise (ICR), a controlled framework that evaluates communication as answer revision following independent reasoning. ICR fixes initial reasoning trajectories, measures correction and preservation conditional on both agents' initial correctness, and uses a no-message revision control to quantify gains beyond additional reasoning. Across four reasoning benchmarks, our audit of textual and latent communication reveals that similar aggregate accuracy can conceal substantially different revision behaviors. Compared with transmitting answers alone, full reasoning increases correction while reducing preservation on all four benchmarks, so richer messages amplify beneficial and harmful influence alike. Receiver-policy comparisons on MedQA and GPQA-D further show that a structured verification policy shifts every channel toward greater preservation and lower correction, while its effect on selectivity varies across channels and tasks. These findings challenge treating communication quality as an intrinsic property of a channel. ICR therefore recenters evaluation on selective revision, providing a unified framework for examining how message content and receiver policies jointly produce benefits and harms.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Weighted Data Selection: Sharp Upper-Half and Five-Dimensional Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00101)
*Zhongxuan Liu,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限加权最小二乘中最小范数学习器在小权重再训练支持下的风险，证明了精确定律 Γ_d(n)=3−n/d 在 ⌈3d/2⌉≤n≤2d−1 范围内成立，并通过 Lean 4 验证了上界与紧致性构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在刻画：当训练支持经过小幅再加权、且使用最小范数（minimum-norm）学习器时，究竟保留了多少风险。作者希望给出一个在中间预算区间内、对任意观测特征秩都成立的精确风险定律，并建立其上下界的紧致性。

Method: 作者采用有限加权最小二乘框架，对选择子集施加"保留完整特征张成"的约束；利用平衡单纯形锚点做降维，通过正权重提升（positive-weight lifting）与独立线性压缩（independent-line compression）封闭风险上界；用偏移坐标对（shifted coordinate pairs）构造匹配的下界；对 (d,n)=(5,6) 情形用单纯形块分解 5=3+2、电路覆盖、比较二阶矩与电路平面概率计算出精确超额 6/5，并用极性面几何处理共享的秩三电路；全部上界与锐利性构造在 Lean 4 中得到形式化验证。

Result: 证明了在 ⌈3d/2⌉≤n≤2d−1 的整个区间内，精确定律 Γ_d(n)=3−n/d 成立，该保证覆盖每一个观测特征秩并适用于保持完整特征张成的选择；在 (d,n)=(5,6) 上进一步证明了 Γ_5(6)=11/5，与 5=3+2 的单纯形块预测一致，并在任意相互作用配置下成立，超额部分为 6/5。

Conclusion: 论文建立了中间预算选择问题中的一般单纯形块前沿，将多个精确风险定律统一起来，并通过 Lean 4 形式化验证保证了上界与下界构造的正确性，从而完整刻画了小权重再训练支持下的最小范数学习器风险。

Abstract: How much risk does a small reweighted training support retain? For finite weighted least squares with the minimum-norm learner, we prove the exact law $Γ_d(n)=3-n/d$ throughout $\lceil3d/2\rceil\leq n\leq2d-1$. The guarantee covers every observed feature rank and uses selections that preserve the full feature span. Balanced simplex anchors reduce dimension; positive-weight lifting and independent-line compression close the risk bound. Shifted coordinate pairs attain the matching lower bound. The complete dataset-level upper bound and sharpness construction are verified in Lean 4. At the smaller budget $(d,n)=(5,6)$, we also prove $Γ_5(6)=11/5$, matching the simplex-block prediction from $5=3+2$ over arbitrary interacting configurations. Circuit covers, comparison second moments, and circuit-plane probabilities give the sharp excess $6/5$, while polar-face geometry resolves shared rank-three circuits. The general simplex-block frontier connects these laws within the intermediate-budget selection problem.

</details>


### [279] [Transferable Graph Metanetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00420)
*Yuxin Ma,Adir Dayan,Yam Eitan,Haggai Maron,Soledad Villar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出可迁移图元网络（Transferable Graph Metanetworks），通过遵循不变性与连续性两大原则的修改，使以神经网络权重为输入的元网络能够在不同宽度的输入网络上实现尺寸泛化，并在μP参数化训练下获得理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间网络（元网络）大多只在固定尺寸的输入网络上训练和评估，无法实现分布外尺寸泛化，导致"在小网络上训练、在大网络上评估"的效率优势远未实现。作者旨在突破这一局限，使元网络性能可跨不同宽度的输入网络迁移。

Method: 提出可迁移图元网络，对图元网络范式做出一组修改，遵循两条原则：(1) 不变性——对不同宽度网络表示同一函数的方式保持不变；(2) 连续性——表示相似函数的权重应获得相似的预测。同时研究了输入网络从随机初始化独立训练时尺寸泛化的可行性，并借助无限宽度极限理论进行分析。

Result: 在所有测试任务上，所提修改显著提升了尺寸泛化能力；在采用最大更新参数化（μP）训练的输入网络上表现最强，训练宽度放大至42倍时仍保持稳健。理论上证明了模型对μP训练输入具有尺寸泛化保证，并解释了在其他参数化下泛化失败的原因。

Conclusion: 通过在元网络设计中引入不变性与连续性原则，可实现跨宽度的可迁移元学习，尺寸泛化能力显著提升，并在μP参数化下获得理论与实验的双重支撑，验证了在小网络上训练、大网络上评估的可行性。

Abstract: A weight space network (or metanetwork) takes the weights of another neural network as input and predicts properties of it. Most prior work trains such models on input networks of one or a few fixed sizes and evaluates them in-distribution. The few attempts at out-of-distribution size generalization remain limited in scope and have achieved only modest success. Consequently, the potential efficiency gains of training on small networks and evaluating on much larger ones remain largely unrealized. We propose Transferable Graph Metanetworks, which extend the graph metanetwork paradigm with a set of modifications that make performance transferable across input networks of different widths. The modifications follow two principles: invariance to the ways in which networks of different widths represent the same function, and continuity, such that weights representing similar functions receive similar predictions. We further study whether size generalization is possible for input networks trained independently from random initialization. Empirically, our modifications significantly improve size generalization on every task we consider. Performance is strongest on input networks trained under the maximal-update parameterization ($μ$P), where it remains robust up to $42\times$ the training width. Theoretically, we explain these observations with infinite-width limit theory: we prove size-generalization guarantees for our model on $μ$P-trained inputs, and explain why it can fail under other parameterizations.

</details>


### [280] [Learning to Cover Locally: Graph Neural Combinatorial Optimization under a Hard Information Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00422)
*Johannes F. Loevenich,Thies Moehlenhof,Laurin Holz,Maxime Schwarzer,Tobias Huerten,Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究具有硬信息视界（每个节点仅能观察其 k 跳邻域）的组合优化问题，以 OLSRv2 路由协议的加权 MPR 选择（NP 难的 2 跳覆盖问题）为例，从理论上刻画了信息视界与 GNN 能力的关系，并通过实验证明信息视界（而非模型容量）是决定求解质量的关键变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经组合优化假设集中式求解器可读取完整实例；但许多真实网络（如 OLSRv2 路由协议）中，每个节点只能在固定的 k 跳信息视界内做局部决策，且这些局部承诺必须能组合成全局可行解。作者希望探究在这种硬信息视界约束下，求解器的能力边界与可达的最优性。

Method: 将问题形式化为"局部集合覆盖"（local set cover），并实例化为 OLSRv2 的加权 MPR 选择。理论上证明：①任一视界差一跳的确定性选择器要么覆盖失败，要么与最优值相差 Δ 倍；②在决定节点处读出的 L 层 GNN 恰好等价于 L 跳选择器，因此模型容量无法弥补半径；③深度 O(Δ) 的 GNN 可复现 RFC 7181 覆盖贪心，宽度 O(c_max·Δ) 时复现其加权度量版本，两者均继承 (1+ln Δ₂) 近似比。实验上采用带覆盖补全解码器的 3 层 GATv2，以 CP-SAT 最优解进行行为克隆，并与贪心及限制为 1 跳的相同学习器对比，同时在真实网络（单元成本实例、真实营级机动场景）上做迁移验证。

Result: 3 层 GATv2 达到 cost/opt = 1.030±0.001（贪心为 1.138），在 100% 覆盖下闭合了 79.1% 的差距；将同一学习器限制为 1 跳时性能塌缩至 1.344，比贪心还差。迁移实验中，OLSRv2 原始选择代码在 200/200 个单元成本实例上与基数贪心完全一致；在 40,308 个真实营级机动实例上，冻结模型在全覆盖下闭合 48% 的差距。

Conclusion: 信息视界（而非模型容量）是决定求解质量的最重要变量。GNN 的深度严格对应其信息半径，容量无法弥补视界不足；而在达到足够视界时，GNN 可恢复协议的近似保证并通过学习进一步逼近最优。这为在协议强加的硬信息视界下设计分布式组合优化求解器提供了理论依据与实证支持。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization typically assumes a centralized solver that reads the whole instance. We study the opposite: combinatorial optimization under a hard information horizon, where every node commits to its share of a global solution seeing only its $k$-hop neighborhood, and those commitments must compose into a globally feasible solution. We formalize this as local set cover and instantiate it on weighted multipoint relay (MPR) selection, the NP-hard 2-hop covering problem of the Optimized Link State Routing Protocol version 2 (OLSRv2) routing protocol (RFC~7181), whose horizon is imposed by the protocol, not chosen by the modeler. We prove two results. Any deterministic selector whose horizon is one hop short must either fail coverage or land a factor $Δ$ from optimal, and an $L$-layer graph neural network (GNN) read out at the deciding node is exactly an $L$-hop selector, so capacity cannot buy back radius. Conversely, at the horizon a \ac{GNN} of depth $O(Δ)$ reproduces the RFC~7181 covering greedy, and at width $O(c_{\max}Δ)$ its metric-aware weighted analogue, inheriting the $(1+\lnΔ_2)$-approximation in both cases. Empirically, a 3-layer \ac{GATv2} with a coverage-completing decoder, behavior-cloned from the CP-SAT optimum, reaches $\text{cost}/\text{opt}=1.030\pm0.001$ against greedy's $1.138$, closing $79.1\%$ of the gap at $100\%$ coverage. Restricting the same learner to one hop, on identical instances with the same decoder and demonstrations, collapses it to $1.344$, far worse than greedy. Two transfer checks target real-world networks. OLSRv2's unmodified selection code matches our cardinality greedy on $200/200$ unit-cost instances, and on $40{,}308$ instances of real battalion mobility the frozen model closes $48\%$ of the gap at full coverage. The information horizon, not the model capacity, is the most significant variable.

</details>


### [281] [Target-Dependent Limits of Causal Repair: A Leading-Log Frontier in a Gaussian Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00424)
*Qinchuan Cheng,Jiaqi Liu,Ruixuan Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在已知干预几何的标量高斯因果实验中，量化“可估计的因果改进潜力”与“实际习得修复的真实增益”之间的评估差距，刻画了联合优化学习器与评估器时的最优评估速率前沿，并证明在常规 1/k 学习尺度下存在 k^{-2} 的评估下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果预测器“潜在可改进多少”的可估计性，并不等同于对“实际学到的修复所带来的真实增益”的可评估性。作者旨在在一个可解的高斯因果实验框架中严格刻画这一差距，揭示评估目标本身如何改变信息需求。

Method: 在具有已知干预几何的标量高斯因果实验中，利用辅助数据以有界污染方式估计效应大小，利用诊断信息识别方向；以平方损失增益作为评估目标，在均匀学习均方误差 η 的约束下联合优化学习器与评估器；采用诊断-弃权规则处理未知时序参数，并用精确高斯模拟将前沿迁移到自适应采样。

Result: 在常规 1/k 学习尺度下，任何可行学习器都承受 k^{-2} 的评估下限，即便潜在量能以更快速率估计；在量值丰富机制下，评估指数为 min{ℓ_k, 2kη_k/U}（到一阶相对阶），其中 ℓ_k=log(1/(k^2 E_k))、E_k 为辅助精度；诊断-弃权规则在未知时序参数下达到该指数。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了在此实验中评估目标如何改变信息需求，属于特定实验内的刻画，并非一般性因果可识别性声明。

Abstract: Knowing how much a causal predictor could improve need not reveal the gain of the repair actually learned. We quantify this gap in a scalar Gaussian causal experiment with known intervention geometry: auxiliary data identify effect magnitude up to bounded contamination, while diagnostics identify direction. The target is the squared-loss gain of the realized trained repair relative to a fitted reference. Jointly optimizing the learner and assessor under uniform learning MSE $η$ avoids the trivial solution of making no repair. At the usual $1/k$ learning scale, every feasible learner incurs a $k^{-2}$ assessment floor, even when oracle potential is estimable at a faster rate. In the magnitude-rich regime, we characterize a sharp leading-log frontier: the assessment exponent is $\min{\ell_k,2kη_k/U}$ to first relative order, where $\ell_k=\log(1/(k^2E_k))$ and $E_k$ is auxiliary precision. A diagnostic-abstention rule attains this exponent with unknown nuisance parameters. We also bound the critical allowance window and transfer the frontier to adaptive sampling by exact Gaussian simulation. Finite-grid experiments distinguish sign-tail suppression from total MSE and expose conservative finite-budget behavior. The result isolates how the assessment target changes information requirements in this experiment; it is not a general causal identifiability claim.

</details>


### [282] [ChainLoRA: Geometry-Preserving Task Vector Merging for Continual Learning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00431)
*Hang Yin,Haozhe Wang,Yuhua Luo,Zhangqi Pan,Xiaoxing Wang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ChainLoRA 是面向大语言模型的免重放持续参数高效微调框架，通过链式更新的任务向量几何、单侧正交代理与后流自适应SVD合并，在参数预算内兼顾知识保留与新任务适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续参数高效微调需同时平衡旧知识保留、新任务适应与严格参数预算，现有方法难以在免重放条件下有效抑制灾难性遗忘与任务间干扰。

Method: 提出链式更新的任务向量几何视角，将遗忘建模为方向重叠与系数耦合的可测交互；训练阶段采用链式更新初始化与单侧正交代理（仅依赖上一个载体，历史足迹与正则开销恒定）；合并阶段用自适应SVD提取共享载体并经Procrustes适配对齐到最新任务，配合有效秩惩罚促进任务子空间高效利用。

Result: 在 Large 与 SuperNI 基准上达到所评估免重放方法的最优性能，在 Standard CL 上保持竞争力，并在三个基准上取得几乎最接近重放方法的平均分数。

Conclusion: ChainLoRA 通过几何近似分离共享与任务专有分量、单侧代理约束任务间干扰及有效秩惩罚，在免重放持续学习设定下以恒定开销实现领先性能并接近重放方法水平。

Abstract: Continual parameter-efficient fine-tuning for large language models (LLMs) must balance retention of previously acquired knowledge, adaptation to new tasks, and strict parameter budgets. We present \textbf{ChainLoRA}, a replay-free continual merging framework built on chain-updated task-vector geometry. From a parameter-merging perspective, we formulate a geometric view of forgetting through a measurable interaction between task updates, separating directional overlap from coefficient coupling. Building on this view, ChainLoRA combines chain-updated training with post-stream adaptive SVD merging. During training, initialization and a one-sided orthogonality proxy use only the last carrier, keeping their historical-state footprint and regularization overhead constant as the task stream grows. At merging time, Adaptive SVD extracts a shared carrier and aligns it to the latest task through Procrustes adaptation. Our theoretical analysis shows that Procrustes adaptation facilitates geometric approximate separation of shared and task-specific components. The one-sided proxy further bounds inter-task interference. An effective-rank penalty additionally promotes efficient utilization of the task subspace during continual learning. Experiments show that ChainLoRA achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated replay-free methods on the Large and SuperNI benchmarks, while remaining competitive on Standard CL and attaining almost the closest average scores to the evaluated replay-based method across all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [283] [Fractional Laplace Neural Operators: Exact Architectures, an Expressivity Frontier at Criticality, and Certified Stability for Memory-Driven Network Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00515)
*Mauricio Herrera-Marín*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出分数阶拉普拉斯神经算子(fLNO)，将遗传网络动力学的Volterra解核结构嵌入算子学习中，实现精确记忆结构、物理坐标与稳定性保证的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子学习函数空间之间的映射，而遗传网络动力学由具有非有理拉普拉斯符号的Volterra解核描述。现有有限有理实现无法复现分支点产生的非整数临界渐近行为，且在半直线上最优有理逼近速率仅为根指数级，催生了对能精确嵌入分数阶记忆结构的算子架构的需求。

Method: 提出分数阶拉普拉斯神经算子(fLNO)。对可交换的激励-Laplacian对，单个块图谱层即可精确表示完整线性Volterra解算子；建立有限有理实现的表达力边界（几何逼近与根指数最优速率）；推导可训练参数化以构造性强制预设稳定裕度；通过graphon转移定理区分真正算子一致性与参数共享；在受控近临界实验中恢复分支坐标。

Result: 在通用数据基准上，正有理算子在有限时域上可匹配甚至超过fLNO精度；而在受控近临界实验中，fLNO以少得多的参数更忠实地恢复分支坐标。无约束有理拟合可能越过稳定边界，而认证参数化不会。四参数谱律从48节点图零重训练迁移到192节点图，相对误差为0.51-0.62%。在智利余震序列及智利与21个意大利地区的更新模型上展示结构化推断及显式不确定性。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一种算子学习架构，使精确记忆结构、物理坐标与稳定性保证可与竞争性精度共存。

Abstract: Neural operators learn maps between function spaces, while hereditary network dynamics are described by Volterra resolvents with non-rational Laplace symbols. We introduce a fractional Laplace neural operator (fLNO) that embeds this structure in the learned map. For commuting excitation--Laplacian pairs, one block graph-spectral layer represents the full linear Volterra solution operator exactly. We establish an expressivity frontier for finite rational realizations: they approximate fractional memory geometrically on compact frequency windows, but cannot reproduce the non-integer critical asymptotics generated by a branch point, and on the half-line the best rational rate is root-exponential. The same theory yields trainable parametrizations that enforce a prescribed stability margin by construction, and a graphon-transfer theorem separates genuine operator consistency from parameter sharing. In a common-data benchmark, positive rational operators can match or exceed fLNO accuracy on finite horizons, whereas in controlled near-critical experiments fLNO recovers the branching coordinate more faithfully with far fewer parameters; unconstrained rational fits can cross the stability boundary, while certified parametrizations cannot. A four-parameter spectral law transfers without retraining from graphs of size 48 to 192 with 0.51--0.62% relative error. Applications to Chilean aftershock sequences and to renewal models for Chile and 21 Italian regions illustrate structured inference with explicit uncertainty. The contribution is an operator-learning architecture in which exact memory structure, physical coordinates and stability guarantees coexist with competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [284] [Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Image Regression Models with Application to an Inertial Confinement Fusion Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00535)
*Carrie J. Lei-Cramer,Michael S. Jones,Laura J. Wendelberger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACPNN，一种基于最近邻的输入自适应共形预测框架，用于图像回归任务，通过学习带 ARD 核的高斯过程尺度距离来定义邻域，从而以低计算成本实现局部自适应的不确定性量化，并在惯性约束聚变模拟的扩散模型上验证了其有效性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中，黑盒图像模型越来越多地用于高风险决策场景，但大多数方法只提供点估计而缺乏预测不确定性的量化；同时模型内部的可访问性与可解释性有限，难以评估输入空间不同区域的可靠性。因此亟需能够提供输入依赖不确定性估计的方法，以指导模型开发和后续实验。

Method: 提出自适应共形预测框架 ACPNN（Adaptive Conformal Prediction using Nearest Neighbors）。它利用邻近样本的信息生成局部自适应的不确定性估计，同时保持低计算成本。邻域结构通过缩放距离度量来定义，该度量由一个带自动相关性确定（ARD）核的高斯过程学习得到。

Result: 在用于模拟惯性约束聚变（ICF）仿真的扩散模型上验证了 ACPNN 的有效性，证明其能够实现可靠且自适应的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: ACPNN 为图像回归提供了一种输入自适应、低计算成本的共形预测框架，通过邻近样本与 ARD 高斯过程学习距离度量，能够对黑盒模型给出可靠且局部自适应的不确定性估计，适用于科学机器学习中的高风险决策场景。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is critical in scientific machine learning, where black-box, image-based models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings. In many such applications, model outputs inform costly decisions, yet most methods provide only point estimates without quantifying predictive uncertainty. This challenge is compounded by the limited accessibility and interpretability of model internals, making it difficult to assess reliability across different regions of the input space. As a result, there is a growing need for methods that can provide input-dependent uncertainty estimates to guide both model development and downstream experimentation. To address this need, we propose Adaptive Conformal Prediction using Nearest Neighbors (ACPNN), an input-adaptive conformal framework for image regression. ACPNN leverages information from neighboring samples to produce locally adaptive uncertainty estimates while maintaining low computational cost. The neighborhood structure is defined using a scaled distance metric learned via a Gaussian Process with an automatic relevance determination (ARD) kernel. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ACPNN on a diffusion model for emulating inertial confinement fusion (ICF) simulations, showing that it achieves reliable and adaptive uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [285] [Learning to Price Electricity for Optimal Demand Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00755)
*Jing Shang,Mohammad Mehrabi,Xinyang Zhou,Mahmoud Saleh,Andrey Bernstein,Stefan Wager*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的上下文能源定价算法，将定价建模为 Stackelberg 博弈，并利用 Mehrabi 等人的平均场解表示，学习从上下文特征到可行价格信号的约束映射，通过美国多个城市的电网模拟验证，证明融入上下文信息能显著提升需求响应项目的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用时变电价引导消费者需求响应、使能源需求与可再生能源生产更好匹配具有重要价值。但最优价格通常需要随时间响应天气预测、日出日落时间、星期模式等复杂信号而变化，现有方法无法高效利用这类丰富的上下文信息，因此需要一种能有效融入上下文信息的定价方法。

Method: 将情境化能源定价建模为 Stackelberg 博弈，采用 Mehrabi 等人（2024）提出的平均场解表示，提出基于神经网络的算法，学习从上下文特征到可行价格信号的受约束映射，从而使价格随天气、日照、星期等上下文信息动态调整。

Result: 在美国多个城市的能源电网模拟中验证了该方法，结果表明融入上下文信息能够显著提高需求响应项目的价值，相比不考虑上下文的方法实现明显增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经网络上下文定价算法能够高效利用丰富的情境信息生成可行电价信号，并在模拟中显著提升需求响应的价值，为更智能、更贴合可再生能源供给的定价提供了有效途径。

Abstract: There is considerable interest in using time-varying electricity prices to shape consumer demand response, and better align energy demand with renewable production. However, optimal prices generally vary over time in response to complex signals such as weather forecasts, sunrise/sunset times, and day-of-week patterns; and existing methods are not able to make efficient use of such rich contextual information. Here, we propose a neural-network-based algorithm for contextual energy pricing, modeling pricing as a Stackelberg game and leveraging a mean-field solution representation from Mehrabi et al.~(2024). The approach learns constrained mappings from contextual features to feasible price signals. We validate our approach by simulating the energy grid in several US cities, and show that incorporating contextual information can considerably increase the value of the demand response programs.

</details>


### [286] [Inference for stochastic differential equations driven by weighted sub-fractional Brownian motion using neural networks and the Euler approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00793)
*J. H. Ramirez-Gonzalez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究由加权次分数布朗族高斯过程驱动的随机微分方程，在固定观测时域和已知初值下，从离散观测中估计漂移、扩散与噪声协方差（协方差指数、时间权重等）。采用欧拉近似重建高斯驱动增量，利用联合密度构建轨迹似然，并用神经网络与径向基表示建模未知函数，通过似然剖面估计协方差指数与扩散尺度，并在二十种系数设定下与两种神经方法对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中常需从离散观测反推随机系统的漂移、扩散及驱动噪声的协方差结构；传统参数化方法依赖先验函数形式，难以处理复杂或未知的非参数结构，故需结合数据驱动的表示学习与统计推断，在非参数框架下同时估计函数型系数与高斯噪声的协方差特征。

Method: 针对 dX_t=a(X_t)dt+σ(X_t)dZ_t^{β,f}，其中 Z^{β,f} 为加权次分数布朗族高斯过程（协方差核由时间权重 f 与含协方差指数 β∈(0,2) 的核 q_β 组成）。方法步骤：1) 用欧拉近似从观测转移重建高斯驱动增量；2) 依据其联合密度构造轨迹似然；3) 用神经网络和径向基表示分别建模漂移 a、扩散 σ 及归一化时间权重 f；4) 对协方差指数 β 与扩散尺度采用似然剖面（profile likelihood）估计；5) 在相同仿真轨迹的二十种系数设定下，与两种神经方法比较。

Result: 所提方法成功联合估计漂移、扩散及噪声协方差结构（协方差指数与时间权重）。在二十种系数设定的仿真中，与两种神经网络替代方法的对比验证了该方法的有效性与相对优势。

Conclusion: 结合欧拉近似、轨迹似然、神经/径向基表示与似然剖面，可在加权次分数布朗高斯噪声驱动的非线性随机微分方程中，从离散观测一致地估计漂移、扩散及协方差结构，为非参数下含高斯过程噪声的 SDE 推断提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We consider the estimation of drift, diffusion, and noise covariance from discrete observations of stochastic differential equations driven by Gaussian processes. For a fixed observation horizon $T>0$ and a known initial state $x_0\in\mathbb R$, we study \begin{equation*}
  dX_t=a(X_t)\,dt+σ(X_t)\,dZ_t^{β,f},
  \qquad X_0=x_0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T. \end{equation*} \smallskip\noindent Here $a:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R$ is the drift coefficient, $σ:\mathbb R\to(0,\infty)$ is the diffusion coefficient, and $Z^{β,f}$ is a centered Gaussian process from the weighted sub-fractional Brownian family, with covariance \begin{equation*}
  \operatorname{Cov}(Z_s^{β,f},Z_t^{β,f})
  =\int_0^{s\wedge t} f(r)q_β(s-r,t-r)\,dr,
  \qquad 0\leq s,t\leq T. \end{equation*} \smallskip\noindent Here $s\wedge t=\min\{s,t\}$. The temporal weight $f:[0,T]\to[0,\infty)$ is measurable, bounded, and positive almost everywhere, and $β\in(0,2)$ is the covariance exponent. For $u,v\geq0$, the kernel is $q_β(u,v)=[u^β+v^β-(u+v)^β]/(1-β)$ when $β\ne1$. Its continuous extension at $β=1$ is $q_1(u,v)=(u+v)\log(u+v)-u\log u-v\log v$, with $0\log0=0$.
  Using the Euler approximation, we reconstruct the Gaussian driving increments from observed transitions and use their joint density to obtain a trajectory likelihood. Neural and radial-basis representations model the drift, diffusion, and normalized temporal weight, while a likelihood profile estimates the covariance exponent and diffusion scale. We compare the method with two neural alternatives on the same simulated trajectories in twenty coefficient settings.

</details>


### [287] [Block Optimism for Nonstationary Bandits with Latent Linear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00911)
*Taehyun Hwang,Hyunjun Choi,Heesang Ann,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究具有潜在线性动态的非平稳随机多臂赌博机问题，提出基于自适应块级乐观的UCB算法，在双线性奖励观测和开环动作序列基准下，首次将遗憾界从 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 改进为 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作会同时影响即时奖励和未观测潜在状态的未来演化，导致奖励具有历史依赖性，形成非平凡的长期规划问题。已有 explore-then-commit 方法仅达 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 遗憾，作者希望利用潜在动态的结构信息，通过更有效的探索-利用权衡来获得更优的 $\sqrt T$ 级遗憾界。

Method: 核心步骤是"循环近似"（cyclic approximation）：在稳定动态条件下，无限记忆的奖励过程可被截断，开环基准可由有限记忆的块级代理优化问题近似。基于该归约，作者提出的 UCB 块算法为截断后的动态参数维护置信集，并乐观地选择动作块（block），实现自适应块级乐观探索。

Result: 证明了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$ 的遗憾界，相比同一模型下此前的 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 保证有显著改进，是首个在双线性奖励观测与开环动作序列基准的潜在线性动态赌博机问题上获得 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$ 遗憾的结果。

Conclusion: 通过循环近似将无限记忆问题归约为有限记忆块级优化，并借助块级 UCB 乐观策略，实现了对既有 explore-then-commit 方法的实质性超越，为潜在线性动态赌博机建立了首个最优 $\sqrt T$ 级遗憾保证。

Abstract: We study an endogenous nonstationary stochastic bandit problem with latent linear dynamics, where actions affect both immediate rewards and the future evolution of an unobserved latent state. Rewards are bilinear in the current action and latent state, inducing history-dependent rewards and a nontrivial long-horizon planning problem. The existing explore-then-commit approach achieves $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret by uniformly exploring to estimate the latent dynamics and then committing to an optimized open-loop action sequence. We show that this rate can be improved via adaptive block-level optimism. Our key step is a cyclic approximation: under stable dynamics, the infinite-memory reward process can be truncated, and the open-loop benchmark can be approximated by optimizing a finite-memory block-level proxy. Building on this reduction, we propose a UCB-based block algorithm that maintains confidence sets for the truncated dynamics parameters and selects blocks optimistically. We prove a regret bound of order $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$, significantly improving over the previous $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ guarantee for the same model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$ regret guarantee for latent linear-dynamics bandits with bilinear reward observations and an open-loop action-sequence benchmark.

</details>


### [288] [The Price of Correlated Tests: How Strict Should a Model Release Gate Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00993)
*Marco Pollanen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将模型发布门控（release gate）视为设计问题：通过选择“需通过多少测试”的门槛，使放行的模型满足可靠性目标，同时尽可能多保留优秀模型。作者用双类别潜在因子模型量化成本，证明在两类共享同一潜在相关时更严格的门总提升可靠性，并给出基于精确二项边界的验证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法要求模型通过全部自动化测试，看似安全却会误杀许多本可服务用户的优质模型，且无法说明通过测试的模型到底有多可信。因此需要在“可靠性”与“保留优质模型数量”之间做权衡，把发布门控本身当作可优化对象。

Method: 构建双类别潜在因子模型，将两方面的代价显式化，并把每个计算化为一维积分；证明当两类共享同一潜在相关时，更严的门总提高可靠性，故保模型最多的门即满足可靠性目标的最宽松门；分析测试间相关性对所需测试数量与保留比例的影响；提出基于精确二项边界的验证程序，即使门从固定的候选短名单中选取也能认证。

Result: 在全部通过（pass-all）的门控下，任何低于完美（100%）的可靠性目标在模型内都可达，但随着测试套件增大，保留的优质模型比例趋于零。测试间的相关性决定了代价：某配置下 99% 目标需 8 个独立测试，而在潜在相关 0.3 时需 74 个测试、0.5 时需 5,182 个测试，且此时门保留的优质模型不足十分之一。验证流程基于精确二项边界，可从标注数据认证门，即使门选自固定短名单。

Conclusion: 发布门控应作为设计问题来处理：选择最宽松但仍满足可靠性目标的门，能在保证可信度的前提下保留最多的优质模型。测试之间的相关性是决定所需测试数量和保留比例的关键因素，高相关会急剧抬高成本；所提出的验证程序能为选定的门提供可靠的统计认证。

Abstract: Before a machine learning model ships, it often has to pass a suite of automated tests. Requiring every test to pass looks safe, yet it can reject many models that would have served users well, and it does not say how trustworthy a passing model actually is. We treat the release gate as a design problem: choose how many tests a model must pass so that cleared models meet a stated reliability target, while keeping as many good models as possible. A two-class latent-factor model makes both costs explicit and reduces each calculation to a one-dimensional integral. We prove that when both classes share the same latent correlation, a stricter gate always raises reliability, so the gate that keeps the most good models is the most lenient one that still meets the target. Under pass-all gating, any reliability target short of perfection is attainable within the model, but the share of good models kept tends to zero as the suite grows. Correlation between tests sets the price. In one configuration, a 99 percent target needs 8 independent tests, but 74 tests at a latent correlation of 0.3 and 5,182 at 0.5, where the gate keeps fewer than one good model in ten. We also give a validation procedure, built on exact binomial bounds, that certifies a gate from labelled data even when the gate is chosen from a fixed shortlist.

</details>


### [289] [Tolerance-Based Fairness Auditing: Violation Certification and Sensitivity Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01005)
*Jie Tang,Chuanlong Xie,Lixing Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对算法公平性审计中"可容忍偏差阈值"问题，提出统一的基于容忍度的公平性审计框架，覆盖两种互补目标（违规认证与敏感性筛查），并给出对应的方法、实验与 COMPAS 实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法不公平问题日益突出，亟需透明的公平性审计；而可容忍的不公平程度取决于具体法律、伦理或应用情境。给定预设容忍阈值后，如何判断群体间差异是否超出允许容差，是亟待解决的统计问题。

Method: 提出统一框架，针对两种互补审计目标分别设计方法：(1) 违规认证——控制误报违规声明，采用约束经验似然检验，利用最不利点校准，并可结合错误标记率控制实现多子组同时审计；(2) 敏感性筛查——减少漏报违规，采用自适应边界代理原则，构建分裂经验似然与调整分裂经验似然检验。

Result: 数值实验揭示了各方法在错误控制与敏感性之间存在不同的权衡关系；COMPAS 数据分析验证了该框架在预测性公平审计中的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够依据预设容忍阈值，在违规认证与敏感性筛查两种目标下有效开展公平性审计，为不同审计需求提供对误报与漏报有针对性控制的统计工具。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence is increasingly deployed, algorithmic unfairness has raised growing concerns and intensified demands for transparent fairness auditing. In practice, the tolerable degree of algorithmic unfairness depends on the specific legal, ethical, or application context. Given a prespecified tolerance threshold, an important statistical question is how to determine whether a group disparity exceeds the allowable tolerance across different auditing objectives. To address this problem, we develop a unified tolerance-based fairness auditing framework for two complementary auditing objectives: violation certification, which prioritizes control of false violation declarations, and sensitivity screening, which prioritizes reducing missed violations. For the first objective, we develop a constrained empirical likelihood test for formal settings that uses least-favorable-point calibration and can be combined with false flagging rate control for simultaneous subgroup auditing. For the second objective, we develop split empirical likelihood and adjusted split empirical likelihood tests using an adaptive boundary-proxy principle for early-warning settings. Numerical experiments show the distinct error-control--sensitivity trade-offs of these procedures. A COMPAS analysis illustrates the framework in predictive fairness auditing.

</details>


### [290] [Posterior sampling by source-space MCMC via prior-based few-step transport maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01034)
*Hoang Phuc Hau Luu,Marcelo Hartmann,Zhongjian Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"源空间广义贝叶斯推断"框架，用一步或少数步的改进MeanFlow（iMF）映射表示隐式先验，并在其高斯源空间中进行后验采样，兼顾了计算效率和后验稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推断越来越多地使用仅以样本表示的隐式先验（如历史集合、模拟器输出、预训练生成模型）；测试时引导（广义贝叶斯）也面临同一问题——显式正权重（如指数化奖励）对隐式先验进行倾斜。这类问题需要在保持后验稳定性的同时，高效地采样与估计。

Method: 用一/少数步改进MeanFlow（iMF）映射表示先验，并在其高斯源空间中做后验采样；建立精确后验与学习后验之间的Wasserstein误差界，误差用联合总体iMF与辅助速度损失表示，并分解为训练次优性与模型类逼近误差；在iMF源空间中采用带预处理Crank-Nicolson更新的并行回火（parallel tempering），并提出融合分裂哈密顿蒙特卡洛（split HMC）的混合变体提升采样效率。

Result: 合成实验表明所提框架能准确、高效地近似后验分布；CLIP引导的ImageNet实验证明其能引导预训练的iMF图像先验朝向文本指定的偏好。

Conclusion: 该框架通过源空间广义贝叶斯推断，将廉价的少步先验传输与后验稳定性保证相结合，为基于样本隐式先验的贝叶斯推断和测试时引导提供了一种准确、高效且有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian inference increasingly uses informative but implicit priors represented only by samples, such as historical ensembles, simulator outputs, and pretrained generative models. The same computational problem appears in the test-time guidance task (generalized Bayes), where an explicit positive weight, e.g., an exponentiated reward, tilts an implicit prior. We develop a framework for source-space generalized Bayesian inference that combines inexpensive few-step prior transports with posterior stability guarantees. Specifically, we represent the prior using a one- or few-step improved MeanFlow (iMF) map and perform posterior sampling in its Gaussian source space. We establish Wasserstein error bounds between the exact and learned posteriors in terms of the joint population iMF and auxiliary-velocity loss, decomposed into training suboptimality and model-class approximation error. In the iMF source space, we adopt parallel tempering with preconditioned Crank-Nicolson updates and introduce a hybrid variant that incorporates split Hamiltonian Monte Carlo to improve sampling efficiency. Synthetic experiments show that the proposed framework can approximate posterior distributions accurately and efficiently, while CLIP-guided ImageNet experiments demonstrate its ability to steer a pretrained iMF image prior toward text-specified preferences.

</details>


### [291] [Optimal Transport Meets Reinforcement Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01413)
*Yujie Zhu,Charles A. Hepburn,Matthew Thorpe,Giovanni Montana*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了最优传输（OT）在强化学习（RL）目标与算法中的应用，包括分类现有方法、分析背后的动机、讨论成本设计与计算挑战，并提出未解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 中常用的概率分布散度（如 KL 散度等）在分布重叠较弱时失效，而弱重叠在模仿学习、离线 RL 及分布偏移下的部署中十分常见；OT 通过"移动概率质量"的成本来度量分布差异，能编码任务几何结构，因此具有优势。

Method: 本文为综述类研究。作者收集并分类将 OT 纳入 RL 目标与算法的方法，对每种方法识别四个维度：OT 所扮演的角色、所比较的分布、所使用的 OT 公式化形式、以及对时序结构的处理方式；同时分析不同 OT 选择的动机、成本设计与计算成本等实践考量。

Result: 形成了对 OT 在 RL 中应用的系统分类与统一视角，明确了各类方法的 OT 使用模式和动机，并归纳了成本设计、可扩展性等实际挑战。

Conclusion: OT 为弱重叠分布的比较提供了有效替代方案，已在多个 RL 场景中发挥作用；但仍存在可扩展的轨迹级传输、对质量不匹配的原则性处理、以及 OT 正则化 RL 的理论分析等未解问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms frequently compare probability distributions, such as state visitation distributions induced by policies and experts, action distributions from learned policies and offline datasets, or transition distributions from learned models and environments. However, commonly used divergences may become ineffective when these distributions overlap weakly, which is frequently encountered in imitation learning, offline RL, and deployment under distribution shift. Optimal transport (OT) offers an alternative by measuring the cost of \emph{moving} probability mass from one distribution to another under a ground cost that encodes task geometry. This survey covers how OT is used inside RL objectives and algorithms. For each method, we identify: the role OT plays, the distributions compared, the OT formulation used, and the treatment of temporal structure. Beyond categorising existing methods, we discuss the motivations behind different OT choices, practical considerations such as cost design and computational challenges, and highlight open problems including scalable trajectory-level transport, principled handling of mass mismatch, and theoretical analysis for OT-regularised RL.

</details>


### [292] [Zero Flux: Flow-Based Comparison of High-Dimensional Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01472)
*Leyang Wang,Yakun Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Zero Flux 准则，将流匹配中"中点向量场为零当且仅当分布相同"的思想推广到离散分布，通过局部概率通量分解联合分布差异，并给出有限样本误差界及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较两个高维离散分布极具挑战，因为状态空间指数增长且交互结构复杂。现有基于流匹配的连续分布比较准则（中点向量场为零当且仅当分布相同）不能自然迁移到离散分布，故需要一种适用于离散域的、可解释且可高效估计的分布差异度量。

Method: 将连续域流匹配的中点向量场准则推广到离散域，提出基于局部概率通量的 Zero Flux 判据。在独立耦合假设下，证明所有局部概率通量在中点为零当且仅当两分布相同。该差异度量将联合分布差异分解为若干较小的局部贡献，并可从样本中高效估计，同时建立估计量的有限样本误差界。

Result: 在合成数据和真实类别数据上的实验表明，该方法能可靠恢复稀疏依赖信号，并在高维场景下稳定跟踪分布漂移。

Conclusion: Zero Flux 准则为高维离散分布比较提供了一种原则性、可分解且样本高效的度量，兼具理论保证（有限样本误差界）与实验有效性。

Abstract: Comparing two high-dimensional discrete distributions has always been a challenging task due to the exponentially growing state space and complex changes in interactions. A recent work suggests comparing distributions through a vector field trained using flow matching between two continuous distributions. The resulting vector field at mid-point vanishes if and only if two distributions identical. However, such a flow-based criterion does not naturally apply to discrete distributions. We extend this principle to the discrete domain and introduce the \emph{Zero Flux} criterion, a discrepancy based on local probability fluxes. Under independent coupling, we show that all local probability fluxes vanish at the midpoint if and only if two distributions are the same. This discrepancy decomposes the joint distributional difference into smaller, local contributions and can be efficiently estimated from samples. We establish finite sample error bounds for our estimator. Experiments on synthetic and real categorical data demonstrate reliable recovery of sparse dependence signals and stable tracking of distribution shifts in high dimensions.

</details>


### [293] [The hidden advantage of mask resampling: a theory of masked autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01578)
*Jorge Medina Moreira,Lorenzo Bardone,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过高维模型理论分析，证明掩码线性重构能以线性样本复杂度恢复共享潜在特征，而等价于PCA的非掩码重构在此情形下失败；理论还预测掩码多样性可降低样本复杂度，并通过实验揭示标准训练流程中的随机裁剪与翻转会掩盖动态掩码的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么掩码预测能学到非掩码重构（等价于PCA）无法学到的有用表示，并厘清“掩码预测目标本身”与“掩码多样性”各自的统计收益，同时弥合理论与标准训练实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者构建了一个具有共享潜在结构与异构噪声的高维数据模型，对掩码自编码器（MAE）进行理论分析；证明掩码线性重构在非掩码重构失败的机制下能以线性样本复杂度恢复潜在特征，并引入每样本固定K个掩码的集合来刻画掩码采样的统计优势。在实验端，他们通过移除/保留随机裁剪与翻转，对比动态掩码与静态掩码在CNN自编码器和视觉Transformer上的下游表现，并在BERT上开展掩码多样性的补充实验。

Result: 理论上证明掩码线性重构具有线性样本复杂度，且非掩码PCA在此机制下失效；掩码多样性（更大的K）能降低样本复杂度。实验发现，随机裁剪和翻转会持续刷新预测任务，从而掩盖掩码采样带来的优势；移除这些变换后，动态掩码在CNN自编码器和视觉Transformer上均优于静态掩码，BERT实验也显示更大掩码多样性对下游语言任务有利。

Conclusion: 掩码预测目标的收益与掩码多样性的收益是可分离的；“可处理的”理论能够指导实验设计，从而揭示被标准训练实践（如随机裁剪、翻转）所掩盖的优势。这为理解掩码自编码器的表示学习机制及其与数据增强的交互提供了新见解。

Abstract: Why can masked prediction learn useful representations that unmasked reconstruction misses? We study this question in a high-dimensional model of a masked autoencoder (MAE) trained on data with shared latent structure and heterogeneous noise. We prove that masked linear reconstruction can recover the latent feature at linear sample complexity in regimes where unmasked linear reconstruction, equivalent to PCA, fails. The analysis also quantifies the statistical advantage of mask resampling, an established ingredient of masked pretraining. By introducing a fixed collection of $K$ masks per sample, we characterize its effect on feature recovery and downstream performance, identifying regimes where greater mask diversity lowers sample complexity. Guided by this prediction, we find that random cropping and flipping in standard image-training pipelines can obscure the advantage of mask resampling by renewing the prediction task even when the patch mask is fixed. Removing these transformations reveals a downstream advantage for dynamic over static masking in CNN autoencoders and vision transformers. A complementary BERT pilot finds benefits from greater mask diversity on downstream language tasks. Our results separate the benefit of the masked prediction objective from that of mask diversity, and show how a tractable theory can guide experiments that uncover advantages hidden by standard training practices.

</details>


### [294] [Error-Corrected Inference-Time Scaling for Imperfect Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01933)
*Zuokai Wen,Louis Grenioux,Weinan E,Jiequn Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出能量基Feynman-Kac校正器（EBFKC），在推理时对不完美的预训练能量基扩散模型进行在线误差校正，使其精确追踪目标概率路径并消除端点不匹配，实验表明其采样精度显著优于传统推理时缩放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时缩放方法普遍假设预训练扩散模型是精确的，但现实中数据和训练限制使模型存在误差。单纯增加蒙特卡洛粒子数只能降低蒙特卡洛误差，无法消除端点（终点分布）与目标分布的失配，也无法纠正概率路径追踪中的误差。因此需要一种无需额外训练即可在线校正这些系统性误差的框架。

Method: 提出能量基Feynman-Kac校正器（EBFKC）。首先推导Feynman-Kac动力学，使系统在连续时间粒子数极限下即使模型不完美也能精确追踪指定路径；随后用带方差控制引导的序列蒙特卡洛（SMC）近似这些动力学。为消除端点失配，将预训练能量作为扩散路径上的代理，并逐步引入学习到的终点能量与目标终端能量之间的差异。

Result: 在高斯混合模型、粒子系统、丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸四肽上的实验表明，在退火和奖励倾斜（reward tilting）设置下，EBFKC能紧密匹配目标分布和分子自由能曲面，而标准推理时缩放基线仍保留显著的采样误差。

Conclusion: EBFKC通过Feynman-Kac动力学与序列蒙特卡洛，在推理时无需额外训练即可纠正预训练能量基扩散模型的路径追踪误差和端点失配，显著提升了对目标分布和自由能曲面的采样精度，优于仅依赖增加粒子数的传统推理时缩放方法。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling adapts pretrained diffusion models to new sampling tasks without additional training. Existing methods rely primarily on Monte Carlo sampling with more particles, yet are premised on the pretrained model being exact. In practice, data and training limitations make the model imperfect, and these methods inherit its error. More particles reduce Monte Carlo error but cannot remove the mismatch between the endpoint and the desired target or the error in tracking the prescribed probability path. We introduce the Energy-based Feynman-Kac Corrector (EBFKC), a framework for energy-based diffusion models that corrects these errors on the fly given a reference energy. We first derive Feynman-Kac dynamics that track a prescribed path exactly in the continuous-time population limit even when the model is imperfect, and approximate these dynamics using sequential Monte Carlo with variance-controlling guidance. To remove the endpoint mismatch, we use the pretrained energy as a surrogate along the diffusion path and progressively incorporate the discrepancy between the learned and target terminal energies. Experiments on Gaussian mixture models, particle systems, alanine dipeptide, and alanine tetrapeptide show that our method closely matches target distributions and molecular free-energy profiles under annealing and reward tilting, whereas standard inference-time scaling baselines retain substantial sampling errors.

</details>


### [295] [Wasserstein Gradient Flows and Forward-Only Diffusion Are Not Enough for Multimodal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02081)
*Daniel McBride,Pratik Khandagale,Cristina Garcia-Cardona,Yen Ting Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文指出基于Wasserstein梯度流(WGF)和纯前向扩散过程(FODP)的采样方法，其"指数快速收敛"的理论保证被普遍误读；作者证明二者具有相同的密度演化并继承亚稳态/慢混合现象，在良分离多模态分布下会呈现指数级的混合时间，且log-linear退火不能消除这一指数瓶颈，揭示其根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有大量WGF/FODP采样算法声称具有指数快速收敛并能高效采样复杂多模态分布的流行观点，作者认为这一解读具有误导性，缺乏对其在面对良分离多模态时真实混合行为（亚稳态、慢混合）的清醒认识。

Method: 借助Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto(JKO)格式与Otto微积分，证明标准WGF采样动力学与过阻尼前向扩散具有相同密度演化；随后采用谱分析（spectral gap）与平均首次通过时间(MFPT)分析两种互补工具，刻画良分离多模态下的小谱隙与稀有跨模态跃迁；并针对常用的log-linear退火调度考察引入中间分布对总传输时间的影响。

Result: 良分离多模态可导致指数级长的混合时间，伴以小谱隙和稀有模态间跃迁；即使采用log-linear退火引入中间分布，也无法消除总传输时间的指数标度。该局限是结构性的，而非实现细节所致：纯局部、梯度驱动的传输机制在良分离模态间搬运概率质量需要指数时间。

Conclusion: 这是WGF与FODP类采样方法在其标准形式下的根本性局限，暗示未来高效多模态采样需发展本质非局部的机制。

Abstract: There has been a proliferation of sampling algorithms based on Wasserstein gradient flows (WGF) and forward-only diffusion processes (FODP), often accompanied by theoretical guarantees of exponentially fast convergence to the target distribution. These guarantees are frequently interpreted as evidence that such methods can efficiently sample complex multimodal distributions, often supported by empirical results. In this work, we argue that this interpretation is fundamentally misleading. By invoking the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme and Otto calculus, we establish that the canonical WGF sampling dynamics and overdamped forward diffusion share the same density evolution and therefore inherit the same metastability and slow-mixing phenomena long understood in nonequilibrium statistical physics. We analyze this family of samplers using two complementary tools -- spectral analysis and mean first-passage time (MFPT) analysis -- and show that well-separated multimodality can induce exponentially long mixing times associated with small spectral gaps and rare inter-mode transitions. For the commonly adopted log-linear annealing schedule studied here, we find that introducing intermediate distributions does not remove the exponential scaling of the total transport time. The limitation is structural rather than implementation-specific: purely local, gradient-driven transport mechanisms can require exponentially long times to transport probability mass across well-separated modes. We argue that this represents a fundamental limitation of WGF- and FODP-based sampling in their standard forms, and motivates future development of fundamentally nonlocal mechanisms for efficient multimodal sampling.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Dynamic Spatial Bayesian Machine Learning Model: Applications to Intergenerational Economic Mobility and Geographic Income Inequality in the United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00072)
*Hammed A. Olayinka,Saheed O. Olayemi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于高维时空面板数据的动态空间面板贝叶斯可加回归树模型（DSP-BART-HS，含 Horseshoe 收缩先验），并在九类数据生成场景（各 200 次重复）中与结构空间计量、非参数机器学习和区域小域估计方法全面对比，结果显示该模型在所有场景中均为最优或与最优无显著差异，但在时间外推任务上略逊于轻量级的 MTS-CAR-X。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在高维时空面板数据中难以同时兼顾个体层面非线性效应、不规则空间拓扑、密集政策干预与自动化变量选择；传统区域-时间聚合方法在个体非线性主导方差时预测性能严重退化（差距达三倍以上），亟需一种能灵活刻画非线性、空间外溢与高维稀疏结构的统一框架。

Method: 提出 DSP-BART-HS 模型：以空间面板结构为基础，结合贝叶斯可加回归树的树集成设计以捕捉个体层面非线性与交互效应，并引入 Horseshoe 收缩先验实现高维环境下的自动化变量选择与灵活推断；通过空间扩散机制支持零训练区域下的空间外推。评估采用九类数据生成场景、各 200 次重复，覆盖不规则空间拓扑、密集政策效应与非线性个体交互，并与结构空间计量、非参数机器学习及小域估计方法对比，辅以重复切分不确定性量化和配对显著性检验。

Result: DSP-BART-HS 在所有场景中均为最优或与最优估计量统计上不可区分；传统区域-时间聚合比较方法在个体层面非线性主导时预测性能退化三倍以上，而树集成设计有效解决了该局限；在零训练区域的空间留出验证中仍保持较强预测精度。实证部分在美国县级面板（代际经济流动性与地理收入不平等）上，在不可见区域、随机与时间留出三种设定下均表现良好；主要局限是在时间外推任务中，轻量的单一区域自回归设定 MTS-CAR-X 取得了稳健且持续的优势。

Conclusion: DSP-BART-HS 为分层时空面板数据提供了强预测性能、自动化变量选择与灵活推断能力，显著优于传统聚合方法，并在空间外推上表现稳健；但时间外推场景下轻量级空间自回归模型 MTS-CAR-X 更具优势，提示在该任务上应权衡模型复杂度与结构假设。

Abstract: We develop a Dynamic Spatial Panel Bayesian Additive Regression Trees model with Horseshoe shrinkage (DSP-BART-HS) for high-dimensional spatio-temporal panel data. We jointly evaluate the model against a comprehensive suite of structural spatial econometrics, non-parametric machine learning methods, and small-area estimators across nine data-generating scenarios spanning 200 replicates each, including irregular spatial topologies, dense policy effects, and non-linear individual-level interactions. DSP-BART-HS is the best or statistically indistinguishable from the best estimator in every scenario. Conventional region-time-aggregate comparators suffer severe performance degradation -- trailing by a factor of three or more -- whenever individual-level non-linearity drives outcome variance, a limitation this framework's tree-ensemble design directly addresses. The model also maintains strong predictive accuracy under a zero-training-region spatial holdout via its spatial diffusion mechanism. We demonstrate practical utility across two U.S. county-level panel applications -- intergenerational economic mobility and geographic income inequality -- under genuine unseen-region, random, and temporal holdouts, with results validated through repeated-split uncertainty quantification and paired significance testing against every comparator. One clear limitation emerges: under temporal extrapolation specifically, a lighter per-region autoregressive specification (MTS-CAR-X) achieves a robust, and consistent advantage. DSP-BART-HS nonetheless establishes strong predictive performance, automated variable selection, and flexible inference for hierarchical spatio-temporal panel data.

</details>


### [297] [Variable Selection for Infectious Disease Transmission via Penalized Pairwise Accelerated Failure Time Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00176)
*Jeongjin Lee,Patrick M. Schnell,Grzegorz A. Rempala,Eben Kenah*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对传染病传播中接触间隔（contact intervals）的时间-事件数据，提出带自适应LASSO惩罚的成对加速失效时间（AFT）模型，用于在存在传播诱导依赖的情况下进行协变量选择与回归，并给出选择一致性与渐近正态性的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病传播时间-事件数据的回归与变量选择面临传播诱导的个体间依赖，违反标准独立性假设；需要一种既刻画这种依赖、又能同时纳入感染性与易感性协变量效应并区分内部传播与外部感染来源的方法。
