Method: 提出成对加速失效时间模型，以有序对的接触间隔为结局，同时建模感染性与易感性协变量效应并联合表示人群内传播与外部来源感染。针对高维候选协变量，采用自适应LASSO惩罚的最大似然估计，配合坐标下降算法求解，并将分布的基线参数作为无惩罚的冗余参数处理。

Result: 在局部渐近框架下（信息在所有随访期间曾处于传播风险的秩序对中累积），证明了自适应LASSO估计量在剖面似然正则条件下具有选择一致性（oracle性质）和渐近正态性。模拟研究表明其变量选择准确，且估计精度优于无惩罚估计量和普通LASSO。在2009年洛杉矶县甲型H1N1流感家庭监测数据中，得到稀疏可解释模型，并提高所选协变量效应估计精度，包括抗病毒预防对易感性的影响。

Conclusion: 该方法为存在传播诱导依赖的传染病时间-事件数据提供了有效的惩罚成对回归框架，能够进行可靠的变量选择并获得更精确的协变量效应估计，兼具理论与实用价值。

Abstract: In infectious disease transmission, regression and variable selection for time-to-event outcomes are complicated by transmission-induced dependence that violates standard independence assumptions. Pairwise survival analysis addresses this dependence by modeling contact intervals in ordered pairs, defined as the time from the onset of infectiousness in the pair's first individual to their first infectious contact with the second individual. We propose a penalized pairwise accelerated failure time model for contact intervals that accommodates simultaneous covariate effects on infectiousness and susceptibility while jointly representing within-population transmission and infection from external sources. For variable selection with many candidate covariates, we develop adaptive LASSO penalized maximum likelihood estimation with a coordinate descent algorithm, treating distributional baseline parameters as unpenalized nuisance parameters. We establish selection consistency and asymptotic normality of the adaptive LASSO estimator under profile-likelihood regularity conditions in a local asymptotic regime in which information accumulates over all ordered pairs that are ever at risk of transmission during follow-up. Simulation studies show accurate variable selection and improved estimation accuracy compared with the unpenalized estimator and the LASSO. We illustrate the method using 2009 household influenza A (H1N1) surveillance data from Los Angeles County, where the adaptive LASSO produces a sparse, interpretable model and improves the precision of selected covariate effect estimates, including the effect of antiviral prophylaxis on susceptibility.

</details>


### [298] [Beyond Actor-Partner Associations: A Target-Trial Framework for Causal Spillover Effects in Longitudinal Dyadic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00181)
*Subir Hait*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个从APIM（行动者-伴侣相互依赖模型）问题到因果二元分析的设计与诊断桥梁，将APIM风格的问题形式化为可观测内干扰下的二元目标试验，阐明了在可交换性、正性和正确设定的条件下，APIM系数等于相应因果对比，并证明独立倾向分解导致一个由协变量依赖Cov(A1,A2|H)决定的单参数偏差，通过模拟和HRS夫妻数据验证了相关发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APIM常用于描述一方暴露与双方结局的关联，但其行动者与伴侣系数只有在满足额外设计与识别假设时才具有因果解释。作者希望将关联层面的APIM问题显式地桥接到二元干扰框架下的因果目标试验，揭示独立倾向分解（separate-propensity）带来的偏差来源，并指出仅使用灵活AIPW无法解决弱支持不稳定性。

Method: 作者将APIM问题形式化为可观测内干扰下的二元目标试验，建立关联与因果表述之间的三重联系：在联合可交换性、正性及同质且正确设定的处理条件均值下证明APIM系数等于因果对比；对二元处理，推导真实成员倾向相乘所产生的单参数偏差δ(H)=Cov(A1,A2|H)，其四个联合单元误差呈(+δ,-δ,-δ,+δ)且总变差为2|δ|，并给出IPW与AIPW偏差的闭式表示；采用36场景模拟与真实边际分解诊断，并将框架应用于2400对HRS夫妻研究退休转换与后续抑郁症状。

Result: 模拟与诊断显示，残余依赖下的独立倾向大偏差主要源于分解（factorization）偏差；灵活AIPW可减少非线性模型偏差，但无法减轻弱支持不稳定性。在最强依赖条件下，即使分解真实边际也会反转溢出对比，并将交互效应放大至真实值的近五倍。对HRS数据的应用中，共同退休罕见、残余处理依赖显著，且估计对支持选择和倾向截尾（flooring）敏感。

Conclusion: 本文核心贡献并非提出新的通用干扰或AIPW理论，而是提供一个从APIM问题到因果二元分析的设计与诊断桥梁，强调需关注联合处理分布、依赖结构及支持条件，以避免独立倾向分解导致的严重偏差。

Abstract: Actor-partner interdependence models (APIMs) describe how one member's exposure is associated with that member's and the partner's outcomes, but actor and partner coefficients are causal only under additional design and identification assumptions. We formulate APIM-style questions as dyadic target trials under within-dyad interference and make three links between the associational and causal formulations explicit. First, under joint exchangeability, positivity, and a homogeneous correctly specified treatment-related conditional mean, APIM actor, partner, interaction, and joint coefficients equal the corresponding causal contrasts. Second, for binary dyadic treatment, multiplying the true member-specific propensities induces an exact one-parameter discrepancy delta(H) = Cov(A_1, A_2 | H): the four joint-cell errors are (+delta, -delta, -delta, +delta), total variation is 2|delta|, and IPW contrast bias has a closed-form representation. Third, the same discrepancy affects AIPW through its product with outcome-regression error. A 36-scenario simulation and a true-marginal factorization diagnostic show that large separate-propensity bias under residual dependence is primarily factorization bias, while flexible AIPW reduces nonlinear-model bias but not weak-support instability. In the strongest dependence condition, factorizing even the true marginals reversed the spillover contrast and inflated the interaction to nearly five times its true value. We apply the framework to 2,400 Health and Retirement Study couples studying retirement transitions and later depressive symptoms. Joint retirement was rare, residual treatment dependence was substantial, and estimates were sensitive to support and propensity flooring. The contribution is a design-and-diagnostic bridge from APIM questions to causal dyadic analyses, not a new general interference or AIPW theory.

</details>


### [299] [Forecasting multiple density-valued time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00215)
*Han Lin Shang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对密度型时间序列的非线性约束问题，提出基于中心化对数比变换和累积分布函数的两种双射变换，将密度函数映射到无约束函数空间，并结合多总体函数型时间序列建模与性别/区域间差距建模两种方法，以日本1973-2024年分地区年龄别生命表死亡数据评估其1-20步预测精度并给出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度型时间序列在实践中常见，但由于密度函数非负且积分为1，不构成线性向量空间，导致标准时间序列方法无法直接应用，需要将密度映射到无约束空间以建立横截面与序列相关性的直接建模框架。

Method: 提出两种双射变换：中心化对数比变换（clr）与累积分布函数（CDF）变换，将密度函数一一映射到无约束函数空间；在该空间中采用两种建模途径——多总体函数型时间序列建模（刻画总体间相关性）以及性别与区域间的差距（gap）建模；利用日本分地区年龄别生命表死亡计数（1973-2024）进行实证比较。

Result: 基于日本分地区年龄别生命表数据，系统评估并比较了两种变换下、两种建模方法在1至20步超前点预测与区间预测的精度，并给出了一般性使用建议。

Conclusion: 通过将密度映射到无约束空间并结合函数型时间序列方法，可以有效地对多总体密度型面板数据进行建模与预测；不同变换与建模策略各有优劣，文中据此提出了一般性推荐。

Abstract: Density-valued time series are common in practice. However, since these functions are non-negative and must integrate to one, they do not form a linear vector space, which complicates the direct application of standard time-series methods. To address these constraints, we introduce two transformations, based on the centered log-ratio transformation and the cumulative distribution function, for modeling and forecasting balanced panel data represented by multiple densities. Both transformations map densities bijectively into an unconstrained function space in which cross-sectional and serial dependence can be modeled directly. Within the resulting unconstrained space, we consider two approaches: multi-population functional time-series modeling to account for correlations among populations, and gap modeling between gender and region. Using Japanese subnational age-specific life-table death counts from 1973 to 2024, we evaluate and compare the one- to 20-step-ahead point and interval forecast accuracy of the two approaches for each transformation, and offer some general recommendations.

</details>


### [300] [Heteroskedastic Canonical Polyadic Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00498)
*Kyle Ritscher,Carlos Llosa-Vite*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出异方差CP分解（HCP），在低秩均值张量基础上引入非恒定、低秩的精度张量来建模逐元素方差，并用交替块坐标上升法同时恢复均值与精度张量，计算复杂度与CP-ALS相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CP分解在最小化平方误差损失时等价于假设高斯模型具有低秩均值张量且各元素方差恒定（同方差），但真实数据常存在逐元素异质性（异方差），该假设过于严格，限制了模型对噪声结构的刻画能力。

Method: 提出异方差CP分解（HCP）框架：用非恒定、低秩的精度张量（方差张量的逆）建模元素级变异性，将低秩均值与低秩精度同时纳入高斯模型；开发交替块坐标上升（alternating block-coordinate ascent）方法联合估计两个张量。其每次因子更新的主导阶复杂度与CP-ALS相同，保持计算竞争力。

Result: 在合成实验和脑电图（EEG）应用中验证了HCP的有效性，表明该方法能从含噪观测中同时恢复低秩均值张量和精度张量，且计算开销与CP-ALS相当。

Conclusion: HCP将CP分解推广到异方差场景，通过同时建模低秩均值与低秩精度张量，提升了参数推断的灵活性，并在保持与CP-ALS相当计算复杂度的前提下，在合成数据与EEG应用上展示了良好性能。

Abstract: When minimizing the squared-error loss, the popular CP decomposition can be interpreted as parameter inference in a Gaussian model with a low-rank mean tensor and constant variance across the tensor entries. We introduce heteroskedastic-CP (HCP), which models entrywise variability with a non-constant, low-rank precision tensor, and develop an alternating block-coordinate ascent method to recover both the low-rank mean and precision tensors from noisy observations. Our procedure is computationally competitive, with the same leading-order factor-update complexity as CP-ALS. We demonstrate HCP on synthetic experiments and an EEG application.

</details>


### [301] [Three-group variance-ratio tests for heteroscedasticity in linear regression: an exact null distribution and an outlier-resistant version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00550)
*Ahmed El-Kotory,Ebrahim Khaled Ebrahim*

Main category: stat.ME

Abstract: Tests for heteroscedasticity in linear regression lose their level or power in three situations: when the data contain outliers, when the variance is not monotone, and when it changes along a variable outside the regressors, such as time. The proposed tests sort the observations by any chosen variable, split them into three equal parts, fit the regression in each part and compare the largest with the smallest error scale. With least squares fits and normal errors, the ratio follows Hartley's maximum F distribution with three groups exactly. With least trimmed squares fits, the ratio resists outliers spread along the ordering. Its square is approximately a maximum F ratio with effective degrees of freedom, whose limit we derive in closed form from the influence function of the trimmed variance. A factorial simulation covered 72 settings of variance shape, ordering variable, number of regressors, contamination and sample size. The robust test had the highest mean size-adjusted power, 67%, against 45% for the next test; when the variance changed along a regressor, which all tests were given, it led White's test after an outlier screen, 67% against 56%. With heavy-tailed t3 errors its lead was similar, 64% against 35%. Unlike the tests built on the regressors, it can follow a variance changing along time, and it identifies the part of the data where the variance changes. Two published robust versions of the Goldfeld-Quandt test, as implemented from their descriptions, did not hold their nominal level. The R package KOTORY implements the methods.

</details>


### [302] [Probabilistic linkage with graphs: leveraging cluster structure to improve the accuracy of entity resolution at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00566)
*Evelyn Lauren,Laura White,Trevor Graham Bell,Jacob Bor*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了11种图聚类算法在概率记录链接（实体解析）中的应用，发现来自相邻领域的Leiden、Louvain和标签传播等算法在准确率上比传统传递闭包减少最高66%的链接误差，且运行速度比实体解析领域的领先方法马尔可夫聚类快最多40倍，可直接集成到现有流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率记录链接产生的成对链接含噪声，需将其解析为独立个体。标准的传递闭包方法对每条链接独立施加固定阈值，但随着人口规模增长，误合并不同个体的风险上升，因此需要利用图结构来提高链接准确性。而相邻领域的图聚类算法虽解决相同任务，却从未在实体解析中被系统评估。

Method: 在三个模拟数据集和一个真实数据集上，系统评估了11种聚类算法——其中5种为实体解析领域已有方法、6种来自相邻领域，并针对链接图提出一项适应性改进。

Result: 来自相邻领域的Leiden、Louvain和标签传播算法准确率最高，相对传递闭包将链接误差降低最高66%；且三者运行速度比实体解析文献中的领先方法马尔可夫聚类快最多40倍。

Conclusion: 跨领域图聚类算法在实体解析中兼具高准确率与高效率，能够直接集成到现有大规模流水线中，优于传统的传递闭包和马尔可夫聚类方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic record linkage identifies records belonging to the same entity even when identifiers are incomplete or imperfect, and is increasingly used to conduct population-level inferences from administrative data at scale. Linkage produces noisy pairwise links that must then be resolved into distinct individuals. The standard approach, transitive closure, applies a fixed threshold to each link independently and assigns clusters from the resulting connected components. However, the risk of merging distinct individuals grows with population size, motivating approaches that use graph structure to improve linkage accuracy. Clustering algorithms from adjacent fields solve the same graph-clustering task in other domains, yet have never been systematically evaluated for entity resolution. We evaluated eleven clustering algorithms -- five established in entity resolution and six from adjacent fields -- across three simulated datasets and one real-world dataset, and propose an adaptation tailored to linkage graphs. Cross-field algorithms -- Leiden, Louvain, label propagation -- were among the most accurate, reducing linkage error by up to 66% relative to transitive closure. The three algorithms also ran up to 40 times faster than Markov clustering (a leading approach in the entity resolution literature) and integrate directly into existing pipelines at scale.

</details>


### [303] [Nonparametric Identification of Latent Dimension under Heavy-Tailed and Mixed-Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00588)
*Chetkar Jha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的非参数方法“Rank PA”（秩平行分析），通过秩变换恢复谱的可交换性，从而在高维混合信号和重尾误差下更稳健、一致地估计潜在维度 r。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平行分析（PA）被视为估计潜在维度 r 的金标准，但在高维、混合信号或重尾误差分布下失效。原因是列置换机制无法破坏数据矩阵中的信号，导致零假设下的谱被高估（inflated null spectrum），进而估计偏差。

Method: 作者提出非参数方案：证明潜在维度被唯一保留在秩变换后数据矩阵的“有序程度”中，秩变换恢复了置换下的谱可交换性（spectral exchangeability）。基于此构建 Rank PA，并证明其在宽松条件下的一致性。

Result: Rank PA 在无需因子普适性（pervasiveness）和四阶矩假设的宽松条件下保持一致性；并证明可一致估计至多 O(n^{1/3 - τ})（τ>0）个因子。数值模拟和对 FRED-MD 数据的分析验证了其稳健性能。

Conclusion: Rank PA 通过秩变换解决了 PA 在高维混合信号与重尾误差下的失效问题，提供了一致且稳健的潜在维度估计方法，理论保证和实证结果均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Principal component analysis and factor analysis are foundational to the study of high-dimensional data, yet their efficacy depends entirely on correctly identifying the latent dimension $r$. While parallel analysis (PA) is widely regarded as the gold standard for this task, its performance suffers in high-dimensional regimes consisting of mixed signals and/or heavy-tailed error distributions. This arises because under the preceding conditions, column-wise permutation mechanism (or any modern variations) applied to the data matrix $\textbf{X}$ fails to destroy the signal, leading to an inflated null spectrum. To address this, we propose a nonparametric solution. We show that the latent dimension is uniquely preserved in the degree of order within the rank-transformed data matrix, which restores spectral exchangeability under permutation. We prove the consistency of Rank PA under relaxed conditions, requiring neither the pervasiveness of factors nor restrictive fourth moment assumptions. We establish that Rank PA can consistently estimate the number of factors up to the maximum of $O(n^{1/3 - τ})$ where $τ>0$. The robust performance of our remedy is demonstrated through numerical simulations and an analysis of the FRED-MD dataset.

</details>


### [304] [Foundation or Formula? A Simulation-Based Comparison of Google's TimesFM-3 and Classical Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00589)
*Ebrahim Khaled Ebrahim,Somaia Mohamed Ali,Ahmed El-Kotory*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Time-series foundation models such as Google's TimesFM-3 forecast series they were never trained on, but whether they should replace classical methods such as ARIMA and exponential smoothing is hard to settle on public benchmarks, because few benchmark datasets are absent from every model's pre-training corpus. This exploratory study compares TimesFM-3 with classical methods on data generated from nine known processes, which the model cannot have seen. Across 7200 series and a pre-registered protocol with a 12-step horizon, neither family dominates. TimesFM-3's error is never more than 1.31 times that of the best method in a scenario, whereas every classical method's is at least 2.2 times somewhere; the largest classical failures occur with only two seasonal cycles, where the automatic methods fall back to non-seasonal models. With four or more cycles automatic ARIMA is 21-24% more accurate than TimesFM-3 on seasonal ARIMA data. Among five foundation models this robustness is specific to TimesFM-3, and it holds only at short horizons: at steps 25 to 48 the three foundation models tested there forecast a decline on a saturating process whose level stays flat, and automatic ARIMA has the smaller worst case elsewhere. On intermittent demand TimesFM-3 shows no advantage over simple benchmarks built from the history. Randomised parameters, heavy-tailed noise and outliers leave these patterns qualitatively unchanged; on the official test period of 1000 M4 monthly series TimesFM-3 is level with the fifth- to seventh-ranked M4 entries, behind the four best, and on 101 macroeconomic series observed after the models' documented training data it is statistically level with the classical methods. The study characterises the behaviour of a black-box model, not the reasons for it, and its findings are conditional on the processes and horizons examined.

</details>


### [305] [Standardized Dynamic Borrowing: Uniform Control of Information Borrowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00617)
*Huan Wang,Fei Wu,Yeh-Fong Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出标准化动态借用（SDB）方法，通过贝叶斯因子与其上确界的比值来动态更新源信息与无借用组分的权重，为临床试验提供透明、可预先设定的借用控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态借用能利用外部信息提高临床试验效率，但现有方法缺乏透明、可预先指定的借用控制机制，难以解释和约束借用的程度。

Method: SDB 将源信息驱动的组分与无借用组分相结合，并用贝叶斯因子除以在可容许目标数据上的上确界来更新两者的相对优势比；无借用组分的后验密度通过对目标数据似然归一化得到。源组分初始权重是其后验权重的严格一致上界，构成可解释的借用约束。当源与目标模型同属单参数标准指数族时，后验期望局部信息比有效样本量等于目标样本量加上借用的源先验有效样本量，再减去非负的异质性惩罚。正态结局下，借用取决于标准化源-目标冲突 Z 分数，并按标准正态核递减。作者还联合选择初始权重与目标样本量以满足预设的功效和 I 类错误要求。

Result: 正态和伯努利示例表明，SDB 能将源-目标兼容性转化为数据自适应借用，并能依据操作特性约束预先指定源组分初始权重和目标样本量。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了一个透明框架，用于预先指定、控制并解释动态借用。

Abstract: Dynamic borrowing can improve efficiency in clinical trials using external information, but existing methods may not offer transparent, prespecified control over borrowing. We introduce Standardized Dynamic Borrowing (SDB), which combines a source-informed component with a no-borrowing component and updates their relative odds using a Bayes factor standardized by its supremum over admissible target data. The posterior density under the no-borrowing component is obtained by normalizing the target-data likelihood. The prespecified source-component initial weight is a sharp uniform upper bound on its posterior weight, providing an interpretable borrowing constraint. When source and target models belong to the same one-parameter canonical exponential family, posterior expected local-information-ratio effective sample size equals target sample size plus borrowed source-prior effective sample size minus a nonnegative heterogeneity penalty. For normally distributed outcomes, borrowing depends on a standardized source-target conflict $Z$-score and decreases according to a standard normal kernel. We also jointly select the initial weight and target sample size to meet prespecified power and Type I error requirements. Normal and binomial illustrations demonstrate how SDB translates source-target compatibility into data-adaptive borrowing and how the calibration procedure prespecifies the source-component initial weight and target sample size under operating-characteristic constraints. These results provide a transparent framework for prespecifying, controlling, and interpreting dynamic borrowing.

</details>


### [306] [Discussion on "Principled type I error rate inflation in two-arm clinical trial designs with external control information borrowing" by Silvia Calderazzo, Manuel Wiesenfarth, Vivienn Weru, and Annette Kopp-Schneider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00711)
*Tim Friede,Christian Röver*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对 Calderazzo 等人针对含信息先验并借用外部对照数据的贝叶斯检验所提出的条件化方案进行评论，认为该条件化方案的实际影响有限，并建议若主要担忧是模型假设违背，应通过调整似然函数而非条件化加以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含信息先验的贝叶斯分析中，基于固定条件评估频率学派的一类错误率或功效并不能完全契合贝叶斯分析的目标。Calderazzo 等人提出对借用外部对照数据的检验程序进行条件化调整，但本文作者认为在同时依赖于参数和数据的前提下进行条件化需格外谨慎，因此有必要审视该方案的实际意义并提出更合理的替代思路。

Method: 本文采用评论/讨论的形式，针对 Calderazzo 等人提出的条件化检验程序，论证（a）该条件化方案在实践中的影响有限；（b）若核心问题在于模型假设被违背，更恰当的应对方式是调整似然函数（例如修改借用强度或稳健化外部数据与当前数据之间的不一致性），而非对检验程序本身进行条件化。

Result: 作者指出 Calderazzo 等人的条件化方案在实践层面意义有限；并且，针对假设违背这一根本问题，通过适应性调整似然函数来改进借用外部对照数据的方式是更优的解决方案。

Conclusion: 在借用外部对照数据的贝叶斯检验中，应避免同时以参数和数据为条件的不当条件化；当主要考量是假设违背时，宜通过调整似然函数来稳健化模型，而非依赖检验程序的条件化方案。

Abstract: Consideration of frequentist type-I error or power based on certain fixed conditions may not fully embrace the aims of a Bayesian analysis; this is particularly true in settings where informative priors are involved. Calderazzo et al. consider adaptations of a testing procedure involving Bayesian borrowing from external control data. Particular caution may be necessary here in order not to condition on parameters and data simultaneously. We consider their example case and argue that (a) the conditioning scheme may have limited practical implications, and (b) if the main concern are assumption violations, these might be better addressed by adapting the likelihood function.

</details>


### [307] [Factor Model Estimation for High-Dimensional Time Series with Heteroskedastic Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00766)
*Da Zhao,Jing Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因子载荷空间估计方法，将同期协方差与滞后自协方差信息结合，并利用HeteroPCA算法校正横截面异方差白噪声带来的对角污染，同时支持稀疏不相干条件下的已知位置噪声污染处理，并给出理论一致性、收敛速率及实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因子模型估计中，同期协方差矩阵在横截面异方差白噪声下受到异质对角噪声污染，而仅依赖滞后自协方差的估计方法在某些情形下损失了同期信息。作者希望同时利用滞后自协方差与同期协方差，在去除对角噪声的同时保留序列依赖信息，从而改进因子载荷空间的估计精度。

Method: 构建一个融合同期协方差与滞后自协方差的组合估计矩阵；针对横截面异方差白噪声引入的异质对角噪声，应用HeteroPCA算法进行校正；框架还允许在已知位置的条目上存在噪声污染，并施加稀疏性(sparsity)与不相干性(incoherence)条件；在相依观测的正则条件下建立理论分析。

Result: 在依赖观测的正则性条件下证明了估计量的一致性并导出收敛速率；识别出纳入同期协方差能相对纯滞后自协方差估计量改善收敛速率的参数区间；模拟实验评估了载荷空间估计误差以及同期协方差引入与对角校正的效果；对S&P 500股票收益的实证应用验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 将同期协方差与滞后自协方差结合并配合HeteroPCA对角校正，能够在横截面异方差白噪声及稀疏不相干噪声污染下有效估计因子载荷空间，理论上保证一致性并在特定区间提升收敛速率，模拟与实证均支持该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Factor modeling provides a framework for extracting common components from high-dimensional time series. We develop an estimator of the factor loading space that combines contemporaneous covariance with lagged autocovariance information. Under cross-sectionally heteroskedastic white noise, the contemporaneous covariance contains heterogeneous diagonal noise contributions. We apply the existing HeteroPCA algorithm to the combined estimation matrix to correct this contamination while retaining information from serial dependence. The framework also accommodates noise contamination on a known set of entries subject to sparsity and incoherence conditions. Under regularity conditions for dependent observations, we establish consistency and derive convergence rates, identifying regimes in which incorporating contemporaneous covariance improves the rate relative to the lagged-autocovariance estimator. Simulations evaluate loading-space estimation error and the effects of contemporaneous covariance inclusion and diagonal correction. An application to S\&P 500 stock returns illustrates the method.

</details>


### [308] [Scalar-on-function regression with measurement error in the functional regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00867)
*Xiaochen Cai,Fenglin Xie,Todd Ogden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对函数型协变量存在测量误差的标量对函数回归问题，本文提出基于模拟外推（SIMEX）的校正方法，通过估计误差方差并对系数函数序列外推到零误差，以修正误差导致的估计衰减，并通过模拟研究、方法扩展和弥散张量成像数据应用加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标量对函数回归方法大多默认函数型协变量可被精确观测，但实际中函数型预测变量常受测量误差污染，导致系数函数估计产生衰减（attenuation），因此需要提出能在此类误差情形下获得更准确系数函数估计的方法。

Method: 提出模拟外推（SIMEX）方法：先估计误差方差，建立一系列递增的附加误差方差与相应系数函数估计之间的关系，再外推至零误差情形以校正衰减；文中给出三种外推系数函数估计序列的方法。并与基于光滑样条和函数主成分分析的两种预平滑方法进行比较。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提模拟外推方法能够有效校正因测量误差引起的系数函数衰减；通过与两种预平滑方法的对比验证了其性能。方法还被扩展到更复杂噪声协方差结构、函数型预测变量的多重重复观测、广义响应以及 2D/3D 函数型预测变量等方向。

Conclusion: 模拟外推方法能够有效处理函数型协变量带测量误差的标量对函数回归问题，表现优于或可比于预平滑方法，并具备向多维与广义情形的良好扩展性；在弥散张量成像数据的实际应用中也展示了其实用价值。

Abstract: We consider the problem of scalar-on-function regression. Most existing methods implicitly assume that the functional covariates are observed exactly, but in practice, they are often contaminated by measurement error. Our goal, therefore, is to deal with the problem of scalar-on-function regression when the regressor functions are observed with error. In this paper, we propose a simulation-extrapolation method to correct for the attenuation of estimated coefficient functions caused by the error. The method first estimates the error variance, establishes the relationship between a sequence of added error variance and the corresponding estimates of coefficient functions, and then extrapolates to the zero-error. We describe three methods to extrapolate the sequence of estimated coefficient functions. In a simulation study, we compare the performance of the simulation-extrapolation method with two pre-smoothing methods based on smoothing splines and functional principal component analysis. Next, we discuss the extension of the method in several directions, allowing for more complex noise covariance structures, multiple replications of functional predictors, generalized responses, and 2D and 3D functional predictors. Finally, we illustrate the methods by an application to diffusion tensor imaging data.

</details>


### [309] [Modeling Bipartite Dynamic Networks: An Additive and Multiplicative Effects Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00900)
*Jing Luo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对二分（双模态）网络的纵向数据，提出了一种加性与乘性效应（AME）模型，用于刻画两类不同行动者之间随时间演化的依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常分析两类不同行动者的交互，形成二分网络。这类网络的依赖模式与单模态网络不同，需要专门针对其结构设计的模型；同时现有方法对纵向二分数据的动态依赖和潜在结构刻画不足。

Method: 首先区分并明确二分纵向数据的依赖结构与建模假设；其次构建二分动态 AME 模型，并基于块坐标下降法设计估计流程；再次引入平方迭代法（squared iterative method）提升计算效率。

Result: 模拟与应用（全球生产网络）表明，该模型改善了系数估计、更准确地恢复数据生成过程，并更好地捕捉乘法潜在结构；能揭示各国全球生产参与度的演化模式，而这些模式无法被观测协变量或静态设定所捕获。

Conclusion: 提出的二分动态 AME 模型有效刻画了纵向二分网络的依赖与潜在演化结构，并提供 R 包 RAMEN 以方便实现与应用。

Abstract: Researchers frequently study interactions between two distinct types of actors, represented as bipartite networks. These networks exhibit dependence patterns that differ from those in one-mode networks and therefore require models tailored to their structure. This paper develops an additive and multiplicative effects (AME) framework for longitudinal bipartite data. First, I distinguish the dependence structure and specify the corresponding modeling assumptions. Second, I introduce the bipartite dynamic AME model and develop an estimation procedure based on block coordinate descent. Third, I incorporate a squared iterative method to improve computational efficiency. Using simulations and an application to global production networks, I show that the model improves coefficient estimation, more accurately recovers the data-generating process, and better captures the multiplicative latent structure. The model reveals evolving patterns in countries' global production engagement that are not captured by observed covariates or static specifications. I provide an R package, RAMEN, to facilitate implementation.

</details>


### [310] [Quantile Social Autoregressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00933)
*Liyuan Wang,Danyang Huang,Wei Lan,Chih-Ling Tsai*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出分位数社会自回归模型，用同伴反应的实证分位数替代均值，以捕捉极端同伴行为的影响，并给出估计、识别与渐近理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同伴效应建模几乎都依赖线性均值模型（linear-in-means），对同伴反应取平均会掩盖分布中极端行为的作用，无法识别哪一段同伴反应分布对个体行为影响最强。

Method: 提出基于新型分位数社会规范的分位数社会自回归模型，将分位数水平作为未知参数直接从数据估计；引入伪反应工具构造新的矩条件，并对工具与残差做核平滑以处理分位数社会规范的非光滑性；建立均衡存在唯一性与识别条件，证明估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，并通过蒙特卡洛实验与实证应用验证。

Result: 新估计量具有一致性与渐近正态性；蒙特卡洛模拟检验了有限样本表现，实证应用显示可识别并解释估计得到的分位数水平及对应的同伴效应。

Conclusion: 分位数社会自回归模型克服了均值模型的局限，能够揭示同伴反应分布中起主导作用的极端分位数段，为同伴效应研究提供了更灵活、信息更丰富的建模与分析工具。

Abstract: Research on modelling peer effects has predominantly relied on linear-in-means models. However, averaging peer responses prevents these models from capturing the effects of extreme peer behavior. To address this limitation, we propose a quantile social autoregressive model based on a novel quantile social norm, which uses empirical quantiles of peer responses. By treating the quantile level as an unknown parameter estimated directly from the data, our approach identifies which segment of the peer response distribution most strongly influences individual behavior. To estimate the model, we introduce new moment conditions using pseudo-response instruments. Because the quantile social norm is nonsmooth, we apply kernel smoothing to the instruments and residuals, ensuring valid statistical inference. Additionally, we establish equilibrium existence and uniqueness and derive the identification conditions. Furthermore, we prove the consistency and asymptotic normality of our proposed estimator. Finally, Monte Carlo experiments examine the finite-sample performance of the estimator, and an empirical application illustrates the interpretation of the estimated quantile levels and their corresponding peer effects.

</details>


### [311] [Testing Procedures for Strict Pleiotropy in Genetic Association Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00962)
*Eva Biswas,Nilanjan Chatterjee,Zheyu Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出两种识别“严格多效性”SNP（即与多个性状均显著关联的位点）的检验方法，仅需GWAS的z-score即可使用，适用于独立或相关性状。第一种为频率学派方法，控制族错误率（FWER）；第二种基于局部假发现率（lfdr），在控制FDR的同时提升功效。作者通过大量模拟验证方法性能，并应用于阿尔茨海默病相关生物标志物，以区分疾病相关生物学与标志物特异性变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多效性是基因组生物学的基本特征。现有利用多效性提升关联检验功效的方法大多只能识别“至少与一个性状相关”的SNP，而识别“严格多效性”（与多个性状相关）的方法要么局限于少量性状，要么随性状数量增加而计算负担急剧上升。因此需要开发可扩展、能同时推断最小关联性状数量并控制错误率的检验方法。

Method: 作者开发了两个基于GWAS z-score的检验程序：（1）频率学派方法，用于识别严格多效性SNP并确定其最小关联性状数，控制族错误率（FWER）；（2）基于局部假发现率（lfdr）的方法，在维持FDR控制的同时提供更高功效。两种方法均适用于独立性状和存在相关性的性状。

Result: 大量模拟研究表明，所提方法在与现有严格多效性检验方法的比较中表现良好：频率学派方法有效控制FWER，lfdr方法在控制FDR的同时提升了检验功效。在阿尔茨海默病相关生物标志物的应用中，方法成功区分了可能代表标志物特异性变异的SNP与疾病相关生物学变异的SNP。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两类方法为识别严格多效性SNP提供了灵活、可扩展且错误率可控的工具，填补了现有方法在性状数量扩展性和严格多效性识别上的不足，并在真实数据中展示了其在解析疾病相关生物学与标志物特异性信号方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Pleiotropy, the phenomenon in which a single genetic variant influences multiple traits, is a fundamental feature of genome biology and an important consideration in statistical genetics. Many statistical methods exist that leverage pleiotropy to increase the power of association tests, but they are often designed to identify SNPs which are associated with at least one of the underlying traits. Only a limited number of methods exist for identification of ``strict pleiotropy'', i.e. those associated with more than one trait, but these methods are either restricted to a small number of traits or become computationally challenging as the number of traits increases. In this paper, we develop two testing procedures to identify SNPs under strict pleiotropy and determine the corresponding minimum number of associated traits. Both methods rely only on z-scores available from genome-wide association studies (GWAS) and are applicable to independent as well as correlated traits. The first procedure is frequentist in nature and is designed to control the family-wise-error rate. The second procedure is based on the local false discovery rate and provides improved power while maintaining FDR control. We conduct extensive simulation studies to compare the performance of the proposed methods with alternative methods for testing strict pleiotropy. Finally, we demonstrate an application of the proposed methods to Alzheimer's disease-related biomarkers for the goal of distinguishing SNPs that may represent biomarker-specific variations from those that are related to disease-relevant biology.

</details>


### [312] [Augmented Patient Preference Incorporated Reinforcement Learning (APP-RL) to Estimate the Optimal Dynamic Treatment Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01044)
*Yingchao Zhong,Lu Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 APP-RL 方法，通过数据增强将患者的潜在偏好融入基于树的强化学习，估计多阶段、多治疗场景下的动态治疗方案（DTR），并兼顾鲁棒性、效率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态治疗方案（DTR）文献通常只考虑患者的既往病史，而忽略了患者自身的治疗偏好；将偏好纳入决策可以更个性化地制定治疗策略，避免患者偏好被忽视导致的次优决策。

Method: 提出 APP-RL（Augmented Patient Preference incorporated Reinforcement Learning）方法：在每个阶段根据患者对问卷的回答推导其潜在偏好的后验分布，用估计的偏好对多个结局加权，以识别阶段性的个性化最优决策；在多阶段情形下，对每一阶段生长决策树，并通过逆向归纳递归实现算法。方法以数据增强方式融合偏好，并建立在树基强化学习框架之上。

Result: 仿真研究全面评估了所提方法的有限样本表现，显示 APP-RL 具有稳健性、高效性，并能给出可解释的动态治疗方案估计。

Conclusion: APP-RL 将患者潜在偏好纳入树基强化学习，为多阶段多治疗场景提供稳健、高效且可解释的个性化动态治疗方案估计，弥补了现有文献忽视患者偏好的不足。

Abstract: Dynamic treatment regimes (DTRs) are sequential decision rules that individualize treatments to each patient at each treatment stage adapting to their past clinical course. Existing literature typically accommodates each individual's medical history, but overlooks a patient's preferences. We propose a method that incorporates a patient's latent preferences through data augmentation into a tree-based reinforcement learning method to estimate optimal dynamic treatment regimes for multi-stage, multi-treatment settings. For each patient at each stage, we derive the posterior distribution of preferences given responses to a questionnaire, and then subsequently weight multiple outcomes with the estimated preferences to identify the optimal stage-wise personalized decision. For multiple stage situations, we grow a decision tree at each stage and implement the algorithm recursively using backward induction. Our proposed method, named Augmented Patient Preference incorporated Reinforcement Learning (APP-RL) is robust, efficient, and leads to interpretable DTR estimation. The finite-sample performances of the proposed method has been thoroughly evaluated through simulation studies.

</details>


### [313] [Polylogarithmic Sparsity of Randomly Reweighted NPMLEs for Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01088)
*Hansheng Jiang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文证明，对高斯位置混合模型的对数似然施加一个强度极小的随机扰动（随机重加权，权重服从Gamma分布且随n增长趋于1），所得的随机重加权NPMLE以高概率唯一、其支撑原子的数量呈多重对数级增长（O{(log n/log log n)^d + log n}），同时几乎极大化普通似然，并以仅差对数因子的参数化速率估计混合密度。稀疏性是估计量本身的性质，无需支撑惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯位置混合模型的非参数极大似然估计量（NPMLE）在无限维混合分布空间上求最优，但其极大化混合分布可能不唯一，且经典意义上其原子数量的上界随样本量n线性增长，导致估计的支撑过密、可解释性和计算复杂度欠佳。作者希望在不引入支撑惩罚、不依赖近似的前提下，使NPMLE自身获得稀疏的支撑。

Method: 作者对普通加权对数似然施加一个随机扰动，构造随机重加权的NPMLE：即极大化一个加权似然函数，其独立权重取为Gamma分布且随n增长围绕1集中。核心证明工具是一个正核混合的“有效维数原理”（effective-dimension principle）：拟合值的低维变化控制了所有极大化点集合中每个极值点的支撑大小，从而将稀疏性归结为证得拟合值变化的低维性。

Result: 所得的随机重加权NPMLE以高概率唯一，在d维情形下拥有O{(log n/log log n)^d + log n}个原子；它几乎极大化普通似然，并以参数化速率（仅差对数因子）在Hellinger距离意义下估计混合密度。该稀疏性成立的是估计量本身，而非其近似，且无需任何支撑惩罚。数值模拟显示其Hellinger风险与支撑规模均与普通NPMLE相当。

Conclusion: 通过随机重加权这一微小扰动即可使高斯位置混合的NPMLE获得精确的多重对数级稀疏性，同时几乎不损失统计效率，且稀疏性属于估计量自身的结构性质。有效维数原理为高维正核混合的支撑控制提供了新的理论视角，随机扰动为不唯一极大化点的选择提供了一种自然的稀疏化机制。

Abstract: The nonparametric maximum likelihood estimator (NPMLE) of a Gaussian location mixture maximizes the likelihood over the infinite-dimensional space of mixing distributions. The maximizing mixing distribution can be nonunique, and the classical bound on its number of atoms grows linearly with the sample size $n$. We show that a vanishingly small random perturbation of the likelihood yields exact polylogarithmic sparsity. The resulting randomly reweighted NPMLE maximizes a weighted likelihood whose independent weights, taken to be Gamma in our analysis, concentrate around one as $n$ grows. With high probability, it is unique, has $O\{(\log n/\log\log n)^d+\log n\}$ atoms in dimension $d$, nearly maximizes the ordinary likelihood, and estimates the mixture density at a Hellinger rate that is parametric up to logarithmic factors. This sparsity holds for the estimator itself, not for an approximation of it, and requires no support penalty. The proof rests on an effective-dimension principle for positive kernel mixtures: low-dimensional variation of the fitted values controls the support of every extreme point of the set of maximizers. Numerical illustrations verify that the reweighted NPMLE has Hellinger risk and support size comparable to those of the ordinary NPMLE.

</details>


### [314] [Certified Alpha Capacity: Statistical Evidence, Economic Lifetime, and Arbitrage under Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01115)
*Nicolò Bonacorsi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究交易信号能否在其经济价值衰减之前积累足够的统计证据以供可靠部署，通过在统一的Kullback-Leibler信息尺度上度量统计证据与剩余机会，在标准高斯模型中给出精确的可行性阈值，并推广到一般的信息下界，进而揭示有限信息预算带来的生存前沿、搜索惩罚及套利活动缩短可验证机会寿命的市场均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于统计显著性验证与市场机会时效性之间的张力：一个交易信号需要通过统计检验积累足够证据才能被认定可靠，但等到证据充分时，该信号的经济收益可能已被市场套利活动侵蚀殆尽。作者希望严格刻画这一"先验证再部署"过程中证据积累与价值衰减的权衡。

Method: 将统计证据与剩余经济机会统一映射到同一Kullback-Leibler信息尺度上度量；在标准（canonical）高斯模型中推导出精确的可行性阈值；在更一般设定下建立信息论下界（information lower bounds）；并利用有限信息预算（finite information budgets）构造生存前沿（survival frontiers）与搜索惩罚（search penalties），分析市场均衡中套利活动如何缩短可验证机会的存活时间。

Result: 给出了高斯模型下精确的可行性阈值以及一般情形下的信息论下界；证明有限信息预算会产生生存前沿和搜索惩罚；在市场均衡中，套利活动会系统性地缩短可被认证的机会的生命周期。通过资金费率（funding-rate）的回溯研究验证了持续性（persistence）的关键作用。

Conclusion: 能否在机会衰减前完成统计验证取决于统一的KL信息尺度下的可行性条件；有限信息预算导致生存前沿与搜索惩罚，而套利的市场均衡进一步压缩了可验证机会的有效期，持续性对信号的实际可部署性至关重要。

Abstract: In this paper we study whether a trading signal can accumulate enough statistical evidence for reliable deployment before its economic value decays. We solve the problem by measuring statistical evidence and remaining opportunity on a common Kullback-Leibler information scale, obtaining an exact feasibility threshold in a canonical Gaussian model and general information lower bounds beyond it. We show that finite information budgets generate survival frontiers, search penalties, and a market equilibrium in which arbitrage activity shortens the lifetime of certifiable opportunities; a retrospective funding-rate study illustrates the role of persistence.

</details>


### [315] [Spatio-temporal coarse-grained Hawkes processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01151)
*Tomoya Uda,Shinsuke Koyama*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出时空粗粒度霍克斯过程（spatio-temporal coarse-grained Hawkes process），一种无需引入潜在事件时间或位置的离散时间计数模型，用于近似由底层时空霍克斯过程生成的聚合观测数据，并提供比传统分箱泊松近似更高阶的近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空霍克斯过程的推断方法需要每个事件的发生时间和位置，但在许多应用中只能获得按时间区间和空间区域聚合的计数数据。因此需要一种直接对聚合计数建模、且避免引入潜在事件时间或位置的低成本方法。

Method: 提出时空粗粒度霍克斯过程：在时空分箱上对时间和空间触发效应求平均来构造有效激励核，并显式纳入同一时间分箱内的激励（intra-bin excitation）。推导其一阶和二阶性质，并在时间与空间离散精细化联合极限下，推导平稳均值与自协方差相对于对应聚合霍克斯过程的渐近近似误差。开发了基于矩的估计方法，并构建聚合ETAS（Epidemic-Type Aftershock Sequence）模型。

Result: 理论分析表明，通过计入分箱内激励，所提模型比传统分箱泊松近似具有更高阶的近似精度。数值实验验证了理论结果，并显示在广泛的聚合尺度下均能实现精确近似。将框架应用于地震发生数据的聚合ETAS模型，结果表明该方法为聚合时空事件数据的推断与预测提供了计算上高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的时空粗粒度霍克斯过程为仅含聚合时空计数数据的场景提供了一种理论上更精确、计算上高效的推断与预测框架，在包括地震余震序列在内的实际应用中表现出良好效果。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Hawkes processes are widely used to model self-exciting event data, but conventional inference methods require access to the occurrence time and location of every event. In many applications, however, observations are available only as counts aggregated over temporal intervals and spatial regions. We propose a spatio-temporal coarse-grained Hawkes process, a discrete-time count model that approximates aggregated observations generated by an underlying spatio-temporal Hawkes process. The proposed model constructs effective excitation kernels by averaging the temporal and spatial triggering effects over spatio-temporal bins while explicitly incorporating excitation occurring within the same temporal bin. This formulation enables direct modeling of aggregated count data without introducing latent event times or locations. We characterize the first- and second-order properties of the proposed process and derive asymptotic approximation errors for the stationary mean and autocovariance relative to those of the corresponding aggregated Hawkes process in the joint limit of fine temporal and spatial discretization. The analysis shows that the proposed model provides a higher-order approximation than the conventional binned Poisson approximation by accounting for intra-bin excitation. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate accurate approximation over a broad range of aggregation scales. We further develop a moment-based estimation procedure and apply the proposed framework to earthquake occurrence data through an aggregated Epidemic-Type Aftershock Sequence (ETAS) model. The results indicate that the proposed approach provides a computationally efficient alternative for inference and prediction from spatio-temporally aggregated event data.

</details>


### [316] [Testing and segmentation of joint and individual components in integrative multi-source factor models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01313)
*Kyoowon Kim,Sungkyu Jung*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出多源序贯对齐检验（MSSAT），利用真实联合成分在各数据源间表现为对齐分数子空间的几何观察，推导对齐统计量的渐近零分布，实现无需重采样的序贯检验来精确估计联合秩，在分离精度和计算速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源数据整合中，现有联合-个体模型依赖计算密集的优化或宽松的谱界限，导致分离精度不足和可扩展性差，需要更精确、可扩展的方法来区分共享（联合）结构与源特异（个体）变异。

Method: 提出多源序贯对齐检验（MSSAT）：基于真实联合成分在不同数据源间呈现为紧密对齐的分数子空间这一几何观察，推导对齐统计量的渐近零分布，构建严格的、免重采样的序贯检验程序来估计联合秩。

Result: 在大量模拟和真实数据（含TCGA多组学数据集）应用中，MSSAT相比竞争方法实现了更高的分离精度，且计算速度显著更快。

Conclusion: MSSAT通过渐近理论与几何对齐视角，为多源数据联合结构识别提供了理论严谨、计算高效且精度优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Disentangling shared (joint) structures from source-specific (individual) variations is a fundamental task in multi-source data integration. Existing joint-individual models often rely on computationally intensive optimization or loose spectral bounds, leading to suboptimal separation accuracy and poor scalability. In this paper, we propose the Multi-Source Sequential Alignment Test (MSSAT). MSSAT leverages the geometric observation that true joint components manifest as closely aligned score subspaces across different data sources. By deriving the asymptotic null distribution of our alignment statistic, we develop a rigorous, resampling-free sequential testing procedure to accurately estimate the joint rank. Extensive simulations and real data applications, including a TCGA multi-omics dataset, demonstrate that MSSAT achieves superior separation accuracy and substantially faster computation compared to competing methods.

</details>


### [317] [Efficient Bayesian inference for multiple network data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01532)
*Francesco Barile,Sara Capozio,Bernardo Nipoti*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究中心化 Erdős–Rényi 分布的分布性质，并提出适用于多元网络数据的半共轭贝叶斯方法；模拟显示 Gibbs 抽样与经验贝叶斯均能准确恢复网络摘要，后者随网络规模扩展更高效，并配套发布 R 包 BayesCER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Erdős–Rényi 模型在处理多元（多重）网络数据时缺乏便捷的半共轭贝叶斯框架，且对中心化版本的分布性质研究不足，需要一种既能准确估计网络参数又能随网络规模高效扩展的推断方法。

Method: 基于中心化 Erdős–Rényi 分布（Lunagòmez et al., 2021）的分布性质，提出半共轭贝叶斯建模方法，并分别采用 Gibbs 抽样与经验贝叶斯两种策略进行推断，以恢复网络摘要统计量。

Result: 模拟实验表明，Gibbs 抽样与经验贝叶斯均能准确恢复网络摘要；其中经验贝叶斯方法在网络规模增大时具有更好的计算可扩展性。同时提供 R 包 BayesCER 实现所提方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的半共轭贝叶斯方法为多元网络数据提供了实用的推断工具，经验贝叶斯在保持准确性的同时兼顾计算效率，配套软件 BayesCER 便于实际应用。

Abstract: We investigate distributional properties of the centered Erdős--Rényi distribution (Lunagòmez et al., 2021) and propose a semi-conjugate Bayesian approach to multiple network data. In simulations, both Gibbs sampling and empirical Bayes accurately recover network summaries, with the latter scaling efficiently with network size. As a companion to this note, we provide the R package BayesCER, which implements the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [318] [Identifying Panel Conditioning with Refreshment Samples: Sharp Bounds and Design Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01654)
*Shoki Okubo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在面板数据存在无限制流失（attrition）时，refreshment（补充）样本能识别哪些关于“面板条件效应”（panel conditioning）的信息。作者将条件效应刻画为重访时的确定性单调映射，证明候选映射与数据相容当且仅当留存者隐含潜在结果的留存缩放分布被补充样本分布集合支配，并进一步给出无流失时可识别性、有流失时由密度比条件决定的识别集、均值效应的裁剪界、跨波次识别不变性以及各类显式选择限制下的识别与偏差结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补充样本是应对面板流失的标准方法，但支撑其使用的识别结果通常假定“参与不会改变测量”。现实中，受访者可能因前期参与而改变后续回答（面板条件效应），且流失往往与潜在结果相关、不受限制。现有方法在流失无限制与存在条件效应同时成立时缺乏清晰的识别性刻画，因此需要回答：在无限制流失下，补充样本到底能识别出关于面板条件效应的什么内容。

Method: 将面板条件效应建模为重访（reinterview）时潜在结果上的确定性单调映射；允许流失机制完全无限制。通过留存缩放分布（retention-scaled distribution）与补充样本分布之间的集合支配（setwise dominance）刻画候选映射与数据的相容性，并构造显式流失过程进行理性化（rationalize）。借助密度比条件刻画有流失时的识别集；对无限制映射给出幸存者均值效应的裁剪（trimming）界；分析同队列内其他波次、流失模式和入组波次变量对识别集的影响，以及生存匹配、对称匹配、入组波次校正等显式选择限制下的识别与偏差。

Result: （1）候选映射与数据相容当且仅当留存者隐含潜在结果的留存缩放分布被补充样本分布集合支配，且每个此类映射都能由显式流失过程理性化。（2）无流失时映射在潜在结果支撑集上可识别；有流失时识别集由密度比条件决定，仅凭尾部行为不足以确定。（3）无限制映射下幸存者的均值效应具有熟悉的裁剪界；当某条目所有类别都被报告时，模型退化为“无条件效应”的检验。（4）同一队列内，其他波次、流失模式与入组波次变量不改变识别集，除非限制条件把各波次的选择关联起来。（5）在显式选择限制下，生存匹配、对称匹配与入组波次校正可识别幸存者效应；给出偏差表达式及补充样本安排（refreshment schedules）能识别条件路径曲率的秩条件。

Conclusion: 在流失无限制的前提下，补充样本仍能对面板条件效应提供有实质内容的识别信息，但其识别集由支配关系与密度比条件刻画，而非仅靠尾部信息。显式的跨波次选择限制能够收紧识别并识别出幸存者效应，而不同匹配与校正策略各有系统偏差。该框架为利用补充样本识别面板条件效应提供了清晰的识别理论与可操作的检验、界与秩条件，并用日本面板数据予以示例说明。

Abstract: Refreshment samples are the standard remedy for panel attrition, and the identification results behind them maintain that participation does not change measurement. We characterize what a refreshment sample identifies about panel conditioning, modelled as a deterministic monotone map at reinterview, when attrition is unrestricted. A candidate map is consistent with the data if and only if the retention-scaled distribution of the stayers' implied latent outcomes is setwise dominated by the refreshment distribution; every such map is rationalized by an explicit attrition process. Without attrition the map is identified on the latent-outcome support; with attrition, a density-ratio condition governs the identified set, and tail behaviour alone does not determine it. For an unrestricted map the survivors' mean effect has the familiar trimming bounds; for an item with all categories reported, the model reduces to a test of no conditioning. Within a cohort, other waves, dropout patterns and entry-wave items leave the set unchanged unless restrictions link selection across waves. Under explicit selection restrictions, survival matching, symmetric matching and entry-wave correction identify survivor effects. We derive their biases and give rank conditions under which refreshment schedules identify curvature in the conditioning path. A Japanese panel illustrates the results.

</details>


### [319] [Markov chain Monte Carlo for predictively oriented posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01818)
*Yann McLatchie,Louis Sharrock,David T. Frazier,Jeremias Knoblauch*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可逐点计算密度的预测导向后验的近似方法，解决了原方法无显式密度、无法用经典蒙特卡洛采样计算的问题，并通过流行病学、空间统计和低能核物理案例验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测导向后验在模型误设情况下仍能提供具有原则性的不确定性量化，但由于其不具备显式密度，无法直接应用经典蒙特卡洛采样算法，这限制了其在实践中的使用。

Method: 作者推导了预测导向后验的一个近似形式，使得其密度可以被逐点求值，并证明了该近似的误差会快速衰减。

Result: 所得的近似能够被逐点计算密度，且近似误差快速衰减，从而使得经典蒙特卡洛算法可以用于该后验的计算。

Conclusion: 该近似方法使预测导向后验在计算上变得可行，并在流行病学、空间统计和低能核物理等实际案例中得到验证，扩展了预测导向后验的适用范围。

Abstract: The predictively oriented posterior offers principled uncertainty quantification, even under model misspecification. However, it does not admit an explicit density and therefore cannot be computed using classical Monte Carlo sampling algorithms. We remedy this by deriving an approximation to the predictively oriented posterior whose density can be evaluated point-wise, and whose approximation error decays rapidly. These results are illustrated on case studies from epidemiology, spatial statistics, and low-energy nuclear physics.

</details>


### [320] [Generalized Engression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01823)
*Xinwei Shen,Zijian Guo,Francis Bach*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出广义 engression 模型，一个统一的条件分布回归框架，可处理连续、二值、分类、有序及排序等任意类型、且坐标间条件相关的多变量结局，并在边际与联合分布上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量结局各坐标类型可能不同（连续、离散、有序、排序等）且彼此条件相关，现有方法多为各类型单独设计、且大多只估计边际均值等汇总量，缺乏一个能统一刻画完整条件联合分布的非参数方法。

Method: 以 engression（基于评分规则的深度生成模型）为基础，引入数据类型特定的链接函数与随机扰动来平滑损失，使即便链接不连续也能进行基于梯度的训练，从而构建统一的非参数分布回归框架。

Result: 证明了连续、离散及混合结局的通用表示结果；在模拟实验、社区生态学242个物种基准数据及17维混合类型健康数据上，该方法在边际评分上与类型特定模型相当，在联合分布上优于它们，并达到或超过专用联合物种分布模型（JSDM）的水平。

Conclusion: 广义 engression 提供了一个适用于任意结局类型的统一分布回归方案，兼具表示理论保证与良好的实证表现，并已提供 Python 软件实现。

Abstract: We consider estimating the conditional distribution of a multivariate outcome given covariates when its coordinates may be continuous, binary, categorical, ordinal or rankings, and are conditionally dependent on one another. Different statistical methods have been developed for each outcome type, and most of them target a summary of the conditional distribution, such as the mean of each coordinate, rather than the joint distribution of the outcome vector. We develop generalized engression models, a unified nonparametric distributional regression framework for outcomes of any type. The proposed method builds upon engression, a scoring-rule-based deep generative model, and introduces a data-type-specific link function and a stochastic perturbation that smooths the loss, enabling gradient-based training even with discontinuous links. We establish universal representation results for continuous, discrete and mixed outcomes. In simulations and in two applications, 242 species in a community ecology benchmark and a 17-dimensional mixed-type health outcome, the method matches type-specific models on marginal scores, improves on them on the joint distribution, and matches or exceeds purpose-built state-of-the-art joint species distribution models. Software is available in Python.

</details>


### [321] [Transportability methods for failure-time outcomes under assumptions for relative effect measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02041)
*Sarah E Robertson,Lan Wen,Lawson Ung,Jon A Steingrimsson,Issa J Dahabreh*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出在相对效应尺度（尤其是风险比）上做可交换性的外推方法（风险比可移植性/transportability），用于将临床试验结果扩展到目标人群；并以 NLST 肺癌筛查试验外推至 NHIS 全国代表性人群为例，发现分布型可移植性严重低估全因死亡率，而风险比可移植性结果与观测到的基线风险吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在某些临床领域，相对效应指标（如风险比）跨人群比绝对指标更稳定，因此相对尺度上的可交换性假设可能比绝对尺度上的更合理；但现有大多数将试验结果外推到目标人群的方法依赖强且常不合理的分布型可移植性（分布可交换性）假设。

Method: 在失效时间结局（failure-time outcomes）下提出基于相对尺度可交换性（主要针对风险比）的识别结果，即风险比可移植性方法；并利用 NLST 数据将筛查策略对全因死亡率的影响外推到来自 NHIS 的全国代表性目标人群。

Result: NHIS 提供了全因死亡率数据且无筛查混杂，从而可对分布型可移植性分析做证伪评估。结果表明分布型可移植性方法大幅低估了死亡率风险；相比之下，风险比可移植性方法给出的估计合理，并与观测到的基线风险相符。

Conclusion: 在分布型可移植性失效的某些环境下，基于相对尺度（风险比）的可交换性假设可能更为合理；该风险比可移植性方法可作为更稳健的试验结果外推工具。

Abstract: In certain clinical areas, relative effect measures are believed to remain more constant across populations than absolute measures. This suggests that exchangeability assumptions on the relative scale may be more plausible than those on the absolute scale. Despite this, most methods for extending trial results to a target population rely on strong and often implausible distributional exchangeability assumptions between the trial and target population (distributional transportability).
  We propose identification results for failure-time outcomes under a more plausible assumption of exchangeability on the relative scale, focusing primarily on the risk ratio (risk ratio transportability). We applied these methods to estimate the effect of screening strategies on all-cause mortality, using data from the National Lung Screening Trial (NLST) to a nationally representative target population from the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS). All-cause mortality data was available from NHIS, along with no confounding of screening, allowing a falsification assessment of the distributional transportability analysis against the observed baseline risk.We found that this approach substantially underestimated mortality risk. In comparison, the risk ratio transportability approach produced reasonable estimates that matched the baseline risk.Thus, assumptions on relative scales may be more plausible in certain settings where distributional transportability fails.

</details>
